MathNet: a Global Multimodal Benchmark for Mathematical Reasoning and Retrieval
Authors: Shaden Alshammari, Kevin Wen, Abrar Zainal, Mark Hamilton, Navid Safaei, Sultan Albarakati, William T. Freeman, Antonio Torralba
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-20T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026; Website: http://mathnet.mit.edu
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mathematical problem solving remains a challenging test of reasoning for large language and multimodal models, yet existing benchmarks are limited in size, language coverage, and task diversity. We introduce MathNet, a high-quality, large-scale, multimodal, and multilingual dataset of Olympiad-level math problems together with a benchmark for evaluating mathematical reasoning in generative models and mathematical retrieval in embedding-based systems. MathNet spans 47 countries, 17 languages, and two decades of competitions, comprising 30,676 expert-authored problems with solutions across diverse domains. In addition to the core dataset, we construct a retrieval benchmark consisting of mathematically equivalent and structurally similar problem pairs curated by human experts. MathNet supports three tasks: (i) Problem Solving, (ii) Math-Aware Retrieval, and (iii) Retrieval-Augmented Problem Solving. Experimental results show that even state-of-the-art reasoning models (78.4% for Gemini-3.1-Pro and 69.3% for GPT-5) remain challenged, while embedding models struggle to retrieve equivalent problems. We further show that retrieval-augmented generation performance is highly sensitive to retrieval quality; for example, DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale achieves gains of up to 12%, obtaining the highest scores on the benchmark. MathNet provides the largest high-quality Olympiad dataset together with the first benchmark for evaluating mathematical problem retrieval, and we publicly release both the dataset and benchmark at https://mathnet.mit.edu.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MathNet：面向数学推理与检索的全球多模态基准
数学问题求解仍是大型语言模型和多模态模型推理能力的严峻考验，但现有基准在规模、语言覆盖和任务多样性方面存在局限。我们提出了MathNet，这是一个高质量、大规模、多模态、多语言的奥数级数学问题数据集，并配套一个用于评估生成模型数学推理能力和基于嵌入系统的数学检索能力的基准。MathNet涵盖47个国家、17种语言，跨越两个十年的竞赛，包含30,676道由专家编写的、覆盖多样领域的带解答问题。除核心数据集外，我们还构建了一个检索基准，由人类专家精选出数学等价且结构相似的问题对。MathNet支持三项任务：(i) 问题求解，(ii) 数学感知检索，(iii) 检索增强式问题求解。实验结果表明，即使是最先进的推理模型（Gemini-3.1-Pro为78.4%，GPT-5为69.3%）仍面临挑战，而嵌入模型难以检索到等价问题。我们进一步表明，检索增强生成性能对检索质量高度敏感；例如，DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale通过提升12%获得基准最高分。MathNet提供了目前最大的高质量奥数数据集，并首次提供数学问题检索评估基准，我们已公开发布数据集与基准（https://mathnet.mit.edu）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for large-scale, multilingual, and multimodal benchmarks to evaluate mathematical reasoning and retrieval, the authors introduce MathNet, a dataset of 30,676 Olympiad-level math problems across 47 countries and 17 languages, paired with a retrieval benchmark of expert-curated problem pairs. The dataset supports three tasks: problem solving, math-aware retrieval, and retrieval-augmented problem solving. Experimental results show that even top reasoning models (Gemini-3.1-Pro: 78.4%, GPT-5: 69.3%) struggle significantly, while embedding models fail to retrieve equivalent problems. Retrieval-augmented generation is shown to be highly sensitive to retrieval quality, with DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale achieving up to 12% performance gains when paired with high-quality retrieval.
为评估数学推理与检索能力，作者构建了MathNet——一个包含30,676道来自47个国家、17种语言的奥数级数学问题的大规模多模态多语言数据集，并配套构建了由人类专家筛选的数学等价问题对检索基准。该数据集支持三项任务：数学问题求解、数学感知检索与检索增强式问题求解。实验表明，即使顶尖推理模型（Gemini-3.1-Pro: 78.4%，GPT-5: 69.3%）仍表现不佳，而基于嵌入的检索系统在寻找数学等价问题时效果较差。此外，检索增强生成性能高度依赖检索质量，DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale在高质量检索支持下性能提升高达12%，在基准测试中表现最佳。
MUA: Mobile Ultra-detailed Animatable Avatars
Authors: Heming Zhu, Guoxing Sun, Marc Habermann
First: 2026-04-20T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:59:43+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/MUA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Building photorealistic, animatable full-body digital humans remains a longstanding challenge in computer graphics and vision. Recent advances in animatable avatar modeling have largely progressed along two directions: improving the fidelity of dynamic geometry and appearance, or reducing computational complexity to enable deployment on resource-constrained platforms, e.g., VR headsets. However, existing approaches fail to achieve both goals simultaneously: Ultra-high-fidelity avatars typically require substantial computation on server-class GPUs, whereas lightweight avatars often suffer from limited surface dynamics, reduced appearance details, and noticeable artifacts. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel animatable avatar representation, termed Wavelet-guided Multi-level Spatial Factorized Blendshapes, and a corresponding distillation pipeline that transfers motion-aware clothing dynamics and fine-grained appearance details from a pre-trained ultra-high-quality avatar model into a compact, efficient representation. By coupling multi-level wavelet spectral decomposition with low-rank structural factorization in texture space, our method achieves up to 2000X lower computational cost and a 10X smaller model size than the original high-quality teacher avatar model, while preserving visually plausible dynamics and appearance details closely resemble those of the teacher model. Extensive comparisons with state-of-the-art methods show that our approach significantly outperforms existing avatar approaches designed for mobile settings and achieves comparable or superior rendering quality to most approaches that can only run on servers. Importantly, our representation substantially improves the practicality of high-fidelity avatars for immersive applications, achieving over 180 FPS on a desktop PC and real-time native on-device performance at 24 FPS on a standalone Meta Quest 3.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MUA：移动设备超精细可动画虚拟人
构建逼真且可动画的全身数字人类一直是计算机图形学与视觉领域的长期挑战。近期可动画虚拟人建模的进展主要沿两个方向推进：提升动态几何与外观的保真度，或降低计算复杂度以支持资源受限平台（如VR头显）部署。然而，现有方法难以同时实现这两个目标：超高保真度虚拟人通常需在服务器级GPU上进行大量计算，而轻量级虚拟人则常受限于表面动态不足、外观细节减少及明显伪影。为弥合此差距，我们提出一种新型可动画虚拟人表示方法——小波引导多级空间因子化Blendshapes，并配套蒸馏流程，将预训练超高保真虚拟人模型中的运动感知服装动态与精细外观细节迁移至紧凑高效的表示中。通过结合多级小波频谱分解与纹理空间低秩结构因子化，我们的方法相较原始高保真教师模型，计算成本降低高达2000倍，模型体积缩小10倍，同时保持视觉上合理的动态与外观细节，与教师模型高度相似。与现有最先进方法的广泛对比表明，我们的方法在移动端虚拟人方案中显著优于现有方法，且在渲染质量上可媲美或超越仅能在服务器运行的方法。重要的是，我们的表示方法大幅提升了高保真虚拟人在沉浸式应用中的实用性，在桌面PC上实现超过180 FPS，在独立Meta Quest 3设备上实现24 FPS原生实时性能。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of creating photorealistic, animatable avatars that are both high-fidelity and computationally efficient for mobile platforms, the paper introduces Wavelet-guided Multi-level Spatial Factorized Blendshapes, a novel avatar representation combined with a distillation pipeline. This method transfers motion-aware clothing dynamics and fine-grained appearance details from a high-quality server-based avatar model into a compact, lightweight version using multi-level wavelet decomposition and low-rank texture factorization. Experimental results show the compact avatar achieves up to 2000X lower computational cost and 10X smaller model size while preserving visual quality comparable to the original model. It renders at 180 FPS on desktop and 24 FPS natively on Meta Quest 3, outperforming existing mobile avatar methods and matching or exceeding server-only approaches in visual fidelity.
为解决在移动设备上实现高保真、可动画的逼真数字人模型的难题，该论文提出了一种名为“小波引导多级空间因子化混合形”的新模型表示方法，并配套蒸馏流程，将高保真教师模型中的运动感知服装动态与细粒度外观细节迁移至轻量级模型。该方法结合多级小波谱分解与纹理空间低秩结构因子化，实现在保持视觉质量接近原模型的前提下，计算成本降低2000倍、模型体积缩小10倍。实验表明，该方法显著优于现有移动端方案，渲染质量可媲美或超越仅能在服务器运行的方案，可在桌面端实现180帧以上，于Meta Quest 3设备上实现24帧原生实时运行。
Self-Correcting Text-to-Video Generation with Misalignment Detection and Localized Refinement
Authors: Daeun Lee, Jaehong Yoon, Jaemin Cho, Mohit Bansal
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2024-11-22T18:31:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Findings. Project page: https://video-repair.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models have made remarkable progress in generating high-quality videos. However, they often struggle to align with complex text prompts, particularly when multiple objects, attributes, or spatial relations are specified. We introduce VideoRepair, the first self-correcting, training-free, and model-agnostic video refinement framework that automatically detects fine-grained text-video misalignments and performs targeted, localized corrections. Our key insight is that even misaligned videos usually contain correctly generated regions that should be preserved rather than regenerated. Building on this observation, VideoRepair proposes a novel region-preserving refinement strategy with three stages: (i) misalignment detection, where MLLM-based evaluation with automatically generated evaluation questions identifies misaligned regions; (ii) refinement planning, which preserves correctly generated entities, segments their regions across frames, and constructs targeted prompts for misaligned areas; and (iii) localized refinement, which selectively regenerates problematic regions while preserving faithful content through joint optimization of preserved and newly generated areas. On two benchmarks, EvalCrafter and T2V-CompBench with four recent T2V backbones, VideoRepair achieves substantial improvements over recent baselines across diverse alignment metrics. Comprehensive ablations further demonstrate the efficiency, robustness, and interpretability of our framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于对齐检测与局部优化的自修正文本到视频生成
近期文本到视频（T2V）扩散模型在生成高质量视频方面取得了显著进展，但常难以与复杂文本提示对齐，尤其当提示包含多个对象、属性或空间关系时。我们提出VideoRepair，这是首个自修正、无需训练且模型无关的视频优化框架，可自动检测细粒度的文本-视频对齐错误，并执行针对性局部修正。我们的核心洞察是：即使对齐错误的视频通常仍包含正确生成的区域，这些区域应被保留而非重新生成。基于此，VideoRepair提出一种新颖的区域保留优化策略，包含三个阶段：(i) 对齐错误检测，通过基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的自动评估问题识别错误区域；(ii) 优化规划，保留正确生成的实体，跨帧分割其区域，并为错误区域构建针对性提示；(iii) 局部优化，选择性重生成问题区域，同时通过联合优化保留区域与新生成区域，确保内容忠实性。在EvalCrafter和T2V-CompBench两个基准上，针对四种近期T2V骨干模型，VideoRepair在多种对齐度量上显著优于现有基线。全面消融实验进一步验证了框架的高效性、鲁棒性和可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the frequent misalignment between complex text prompts and generated videos in current text-to-video models, the authors propose VideoRepair, a training-free, model-agnostic framework that automatically detects and corrects localized video errors without retraining. It operates in three stages: first, it identifies misaligned regions using an MLLM-based evaluation system with auto-generated questions; second, it plans targeted refinements by preserving correctly generated content and constructing prompts for problematic areas; third, it locally regenerates only the misaligned regions while jointly optimizing preserved and new content to maintain fidelity. Experiments on EvalCrafter and T2V-CompBench with four T2V backbones show consistent performance gains across multiple alignment metrics, with ablation studies confirming the framework’s efficiency, robustness, and interpretability.
针对当前文本到视频扩散模型在处理复杂文本提示时常出现对齐偏差的问题，作者提出VideoRepair框架，这是一种无需训练、模型无关的自修正视频精修方案。该框架分为三阶段：首先利用基于多模态大语言模型的自动评估系统识别视频中对齐错误区域；其次规划精修策略，保留正确生成的实体并为其划分跨帧区域，生成针对性提示；最后进行局部重生成，同时联合优化保留内容与新生成内容。在EvalCrafter和T2V-CompBench两个基准上，针对四种主流T2V模型，该框架在多种对齐指标上显著优于现有基线，消融实验进一步验证了其高效性、鲁棒性和可解释性。
Sessa: Selective State Space Attention
Authors: Liubomyr Horbatko
First: 2026-04-20T17:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:59:08+00:00
Comments: Code available at: https://github.com/LibratioAI/sessa
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern sequence models are dominated by Transformers, where self-attention mixes information from the visible context in an input-dependent way. However, when retrieval is not sharp and attention remains diffuse over an effective support $S_{\mathrm{eff}}(t)$, the influence of any individual token is diluted, typically scaling as $O(1/S_{\mathrm{eff}}(t))$ and reaching $O(1/\ell)$ for old tokens in full-prefix settings. Structured state-space models process sequences recurrently through an explicit feedback path; selective variants such as Mamba make this feedback input-dependent, yet when freeze time cannot be sustained over long intervals, their long-range sensitivity decays exponentially with lag. Existing architectures therefore either retrieve from the past in a single read or propagate information through a single feedback chain. We introduce Sessa, a decoder that places attention inside a feedback path, enabling recurrent many-path aggregation within a layer. Under stated assumptions, Sessa admits regimes with a power-law memory tail in lag $\ell$ of order $O(\ell^{-β})$ for $0<β<1$, which is asymptotically slower than $1/\ell$; moreover, this rate is tight in an explicit diffuse uniform-routing setting where the influence is $Θ(\ell^{-β})$. Under the same conditions, only Sessa among the compared model classes realizes flexible selective retrieval, including non-decaying profiles. Empirically, under matched architectures and training budgets, Sessa achieves the strongest performance on our long-context benchmarks while remaining competitive with Transformer and Mamba style baselines on short-context language modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sessa：选择性状态空间注意力
现代序列模型主要由Transformer主导，其中自注意力以输入依赖的方式混合可见上下文信息。然而，当检索不够精确且注意力在有效支持集$S_{\mathrm{eff}}(t)$上保持弥散时，任何单个标记的影响会被稀释，通常按$O(1/S_{\mathrm{eff}}(t))$缩放，对于全前缀设置中的旧标记则降至$O(1/\ell)$。结构化状态空间模型通过显式反馈路径递归处理序列；选择性变体如Mamba使该反馈依赖于输入，但当冻结时间无法在长间隔内维持时，其长程敏感度随滞后呈指数衰减。现有架构因此要么单次读取过去信息，要么通过单一反馈链传播信息。我们引入Sessa，一种将注意力置于反馈路径内的解码器，使单层内可实现递归多路径聚合。在给定假设下，Sessa支持滞后$\ell$的幂律记忆尾部，阶数为$O(\ell^{-β})$（$0<β<1$），其衰减速率渐近慢于$1/\ell$；此外，在显式弥散均匀路由设置中，该速率紧致为$Θ(\ell^{-β})$。在相同条件下，仅Sessa在所比较的模型类别中实现灵活选择性检索，包括非衰减型曲线。实证表明，在匹配架构与训练预算下，Sessa在长上下文基准测试中表现最优，同时在短上下文语言建模任务中与Transformer和Mamba风格基线保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Transformers and state-space models in handling long-range dependencies—where attention dilutes over time and feedback chains decay exponentially—Sessa introduces a decoder architecture that embeds attention within a feedback loop, enabling recurrent, multi-path aggregation. This design allows Sessa to achieve power-law memory decay of order O(ℓ^−β) for 0<β<1, asymptotically slower than the 1/ℓ decay of standard models, and is proven tight under uniform routing. Empirically, Sessa outperforms other architectures on long-context tasks while remaining competitive on short-context benchmarks under matched resource constraints.
为了解决Transformer和状态空间模型在长距离依赖中注意力稀释和反馈链指数衰减的问题，Sessa在反馈路径中嵌入注意力机制，实现层内多路径递归聚合。该设计使其在滞后ℓ下可实现O(ℓ^−β)的幂律记忆尾部（0<β<1），渐近衰减速度慢于标准模型的1/ℓ，且在均匀路由设定下该衰减速率理论最优。实验表明，在相同架构与训练资源下，Sessa在长上下文基准上表现最优，同时在短上下文语言建模任务中与Transformer和Mamba类基线保持竞争力。
Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yunke Ao, Le Chen, Bruce D. Lee, Assefa S. Wahd, Aline Czarnobai, Philipp Fürnstahl, Bernhard Schölkopf, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-04-20T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:59:01+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) has become the predominant algorithm for on-policy reinforcement learning due to its scalability and empirical robustness across domains. However, there is a significant disconnect between the underlying foundations of trust region methods and the heuristic clipped objective used in PPO. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing the Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning (BRRL) framework. We formulate a novel regularized and constrained policy optimization problem and derive its analytical optimal solution. We prove that this solution ensures monotonic performance improvement. To handle parameterized policy classes, we develop a policy optimization algorithm called Bounded Policy Optimization (BPO) that minimizes an advantage-weighted divergence between the policy and the analytic optimal solution from BRRL. We further establish a lower bound on the expected performance of the resulting policy in terms of the BPO loss function. Notably, our framework also provides a new theoretical lens to interpret the success of the PPO loss, and connects trust region policy optimization and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). We additionally extend BPO to Group-relative BPO (GBPO) for LLM fine-tuning. Empirical evaluations of BPO across MuJoCo, Atari, and complex IsaacLab environments (e.g., Humanoid locomotion), and of GBPO for LLM fine-tuning tasks, demonstrate that BPO and GBPO generally match or outperform PPO and GRPO in stability and final performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有界比率强化学习
近端策略优化（PPO）因其可扩展性和在各领域的实证鲁棒性，已成为基于策略的强化学习的主流算法。然而，信任区域方法的基础与PPO中使用的启发式裁剪目标之间存在显著差距。本文通过引入有界比率强化学习（BRRL）框架弥合这一差距。我们提出了一种新颖的正则化和约束策略优化问题，并推导出其解析最优解。我们证明该解能确保性能单调提升。为处理参数化策略类，我们开发了一种名为有界策略优化（BPO）的策略优化算法，通过最小化策略与BRRL解析最优解之间的优势加权散度。我们进一步建立了所得策略预期性能相对于BPO损失函数的下界。值得注意的是，我们的框架还提供了新的理论视角来解释PPO损失的成功，并将信任区域策略优化与交叉熵方法（CEM）联系起来。我们还扩展BPO至组相对BPO（GBPO）以用于大语言模型（LLM）微调。在MuJoCo、Atari及复杂IsaacLab环境（如人形机器人运动）中的BPO实证评估，以及GBPO在LLM微调任务中的评估，表明BPO和GBPO在稳定性和最终性能上通常与PPO和GRPO相当或更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of theoretical grounding for PPO's clipped objective despite its empirical success, this paper introduces Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning (BRRL), a framework that formulates policy optimization as a regularized and constrained problem with an analytically derived optimal solution that guarantees monotonic performance improvement. To implement this in practice, the authors develop Bounded Policy Optimization (BPO), which minimizes an advantage-weighted divergence between the current policy and the BRRL-optimal solution, and prove a lower bound on expected performance in terms of the BPO loss. Experiments across MuJoCo, Atari, IsaacLab, and LLM fine-tuning tasks show that BPO and its group-relative variant GBPO match or exceed PPO and GRPO in both stability and final performance, while also providing a theoretical interpretation of PPO and linking it to trust region methods and the Cross-Entropy Method.
为弥合信任区域方法与PPO中启发式裁剪目标之间的理论鸿沟，本文提出有界比率强化学习（BRRL）框架，将策略优化建模为正则化约束问题，并推导出保证性能单调提升的解析最优解。作者进一步提出有界策略优化（BPO）算法，通过最小化策略与该最优解之间的优势加权散度进行优化，并建立性能下界。理论分析揭示了BRRL与PPO成功之间的联系，同时连接了信任区域方法与交叉熵方法。实验表明，BPO及其扩展版GBPO在MuJoCo、Atari、IsaacLab环境及LLM微调任务中，总体上与PPO和GRPO相比，在稳定性与最终性能上表现相当或更优。
Agentic Forecasting using Sequential Bayesian Updating of Linguistic Beliefs
Authors: Kevin Murphy
First: 2026-04-20T17:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:57:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present BLF (Bayesian Linguistic Forecaster), an agentic system for binary forecasting that achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ForecastBench benchmark. The system is built on three ideas. (1) A Bayesian linguistic belief state: a semi-structured representation combining numerical probability estimates with natural-language evidence summaries, updated by the LLM at each step of an iterative tool-use loop. This contrasts with the common approach of appending all retrieved evidence to an ever-growing context. (2) Hierarchical multi-trial aggregation: running $K$ independent trials and combining them using logit-space shrinkage with a data-dependent prior. (3) Hierarchical calibration: Platt scaling with a hierarchical prior, which avoids over-shrinking extreme predictions for sources with skewed base rates. On 400 backtesting questions from the ForecastBench leaderboard, BLF outperforms all the top public methods, including Cassi, GPT-5, Grok~4.20, and Foresight-32B. Ablation studies show that the structured belief state is as impactful as web search access, and that shrinkage aggregation and hierarchical calibration each provide significant additional gains. In addition, we develop a robust back-testing framework with a leakage rate below 1.5\%, and use rigorous statistical methodology to compare different methods while controlling for various sources of noise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用语言信念的序贯贝叶斯更新进行代理式预测
我们提出了BLF（贝叶斯语言预测器），这是一个用于二元预测的代理系统，在ForecastBench基准测试中达到最先进性能。该系统基于三个核心思想：(1) 贝叶斯语言信念状态：一种半结构化表示，结合数值概率估计与自然语言证据摘要，在迭代工具使用循环的每一步由大语言模型更新，这不同于将所有检索证据追加到不断增长的上下文中的常见方法。(2) 分层多试验聚合：运行K个独立试验，并使用数据依赖先验在logit空间进行收缩。(3) 分层校准：使用分层先验的Platt缩放，避免对基率偏斜的来源过度收缩极端预测。 在ForecastBench排行榜的400个回测问题上，BLF优于所有顶级公开方法，包括Cassi、GPT-5、Grok~4.20和Foresight-32B。消融研究表明，结构化信念状态的影响与网络搜索访问相当，而收缩聚合和分层校准各自提供了显著的额外增益。 此外，我们开发了一个鲁棒的回测框架，泄漏率低于1.5%，并使用严谨的统计方法在控制各种噪声来源的前提下比较不同方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more accurate and interpretable binary forecasting, the authors introduce BLF, an agentic system that uses sequential Bayesian updating of linguistic beliefs to improve prediction performance. The method combines numerical probability estimates with natural-language evidence summaries, updated iteratively by an LLM, avoiding the pitfalls of growing context. It also employs hierarchical multi-trial aggregation with logit-space shrinkage and hierarchical calibration to refine predictions. Experimental results on 400 ForecastBench backtesting questions show BLF outperforms top public models including Cassi, GPT-5, Grok-4.20, and Foresight-32B, with ablation studies confirming that structured belief states and both aggregation and calibration techniques contribute significantly to performance gains.
为提升二元预测的准确性和可解释性，作者提出BLF系统，该系统通过语言信念的序列贝叶斯更新实现预测优化。方法结合数值概率与自然语言证据摘要，在LLM驱动的迭代工具使用循环中动态更新，避免了上下文不断膨胀的问题，并采用分层多轮聚合与分层校准策略提升预测稳定性。在400个ForecastBench回测问题上，BLF超越了包括Cassi、GPT-5、Grok-4.20和Foresight-32B在内的所有主流公开模型，消融实验表明结构化信念状态与分层校准是其性能提升的关键因素。
ReCap: Lightweight Referential Grounding for Coherent Story Visualization
Authors: Aditya Arora, Akshita Gupta, Pau Rodriguez, Marcus Rohrbach
First: 2026-04-20T17:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:57:50+00:00
Comments: Diffusion Models, Story Visualization
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Story Visualization aims to generate a sequence of images that faithfully depicts a textual narrative that preserve character identity, spatial configuration, and stylistic coherence as the narratives unfold. Maintaining such cross-frame consistency has traditionally relied on explicit memory banks, architectural expansion, or auxiliary language models, resulting in substantial parameter growth and inference overhead. We introduce ReCap, a lightweight consistency framework that improves character stability and visual fidelity without modifying the base diffusion backbone. ReCap's CORE (COnditional frame REferencing) module treats anaphors, in our case pronouns, as visual anchors, activating only when characters are referred to by a pronoun and conditioning on the preceding frame to propagate visual identity. This selective design avoids unconditional cross-frame conditioning and introduces only 149K additional parameters, a fraction of the cost of memory-bank and LLM-augmented approaches. To further stabilize identity, we incorporate SemDrift (Guided Semantic Drift Correction) applied only during training. When text is vague or referential, the denoiser lacks a visual anchor for identity-defining attributes, causing character appearance to drift across frames, SemDrift corrects this by aligning denoiser representations with pretrained DINOv3 visual embeddings, enforcing semantic identity stability at zero inference cost. ReCap outperforms previous state-of-the-art, StoryGPT-V, on the two main benchmarks for story visualization by 2.63% Character-Accuracy on FlintstonesSV and by 5.65% on PororoSV, establishing a new state-of-the-art character consistency on both benchmarks. Furthermore, we extend story visualization to human-centric narratives derived from real films, demonstrating the capability of ReCap beyond stylized cartoon domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReCap：轻量级指代接地框架，用于连贯故事可视化
故事可视化旨在生成一系列图像，忠实描绘文本叙事，同时保持角色身份、空间配置和风格一致性。传统方法依赖显式记忆库、架构扩展或辅助语言模型，导致参数量激增和推理开销。我们提出ReCap，一种轻量级一致性框架，在不修改基础扩散主干网络的前提下提升角色稳定性与视觉保真度。ReCap的核心模块（COnditional frame REferencing）将代词等指代词视为视觉锚点，仅在角色被代词提及时激活，并依据前一帧传播视觉身份。这种选择性设计避免了无条件跨帧条件化，仅增加14.9万个参数，远低于记忆库和LLM增强方法的成本。为进一步稳定身份，我们在训练期间引入SemDrift（引导语义漂移校正），当文本模糊或指代时，去噪器缺乏定义身份的视觉锚点，导致角色外观跨帧漂移；SemDrift通过将去噪器表示与预训练DINOv3视觉嵌入对齐，以零推理开销强制语义身份稳定。ReCap在故事可视化两大主流基准FlintstonesSV和PororoSV上均超越当前最优方法StoryGPT-V，分别提升2.63%和5.65%的字符准确率，确立了两项基准的新最优角色一致性水平。此外，我们将故事可视化扩展至基于真实电影的人类中心叙事，证明ReCap在卡通风格领域之外的通用能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and complexity of maintaining visual consistency across frames in story visualization, ReCap introduces a lightweight framework that enhances character stability without altering the base diffusion model. Its CORE module treats pronouns as visual anchors, activating only when referenced and conditioning on the prior frame to preserve identity, adding just 149K parameters. To address semantic drift during vague or referential text, SemDrift corrects denoiser representations using pretrained DINOv3 embeddings during training, enforcing identity without inference overhead. Experiments show ReCap outperforms StoryGPT-V by 2.63% on FlintstonesSV and 5.65% on PororoSV in character accuracy, and successfully extends to real-film narratives beyond cartoon domains.
为降低故事可视化中跨帧一致性维护的计算开销，ReCap提出一种轻量级框架，在不修改基础扩散模型的前提下提升角色稳定性。其CORE模块将代词视为视觉锚点，仅在被引用时激活并依赖前一帧传递视觉身份，仅增加149K参数。为解决模糊或指代性文本导致的语义漂移，SemDrift在训练阶段通过DINOv3视觉嵌入对去噪器表示进行校正，强制保持身份一致性且无推理开销。实验表明，ReCap在FlintstonesSV和PororoSV两个基准上分别超越StoryGPT-V 2.63%和5.65%的字符准确率，并成功扩展至真实电影叙事场景，突破卡通领域限制。
When Can LLMs Learn to Reason with Weak Supervision?
Authors: Salman Rahman, Jingyan Shen, Anna Mordvina, Hamid Palangi, Saadia Gabriel, Pavel Izmailov
First: 2026-04-20T17:57:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:57:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models have achieved significant reasoning improvements through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Yet as model capabilities grow, constructing high-quality reward signals becomes increasingly difficult, making it essential to understand when RLVR can succeed under weaker forms of supervision. We conduct a systematic empirical study across diverse model families and reasoning domains under three weak supervision settings: scarce data, noisy rewards, and self-supervised proxy rewards. We find that generalization is governed by training reward saturation dynamics: models that generalize exhibit a prolonged pre-saturation phase during which training reward and downstream performance climb together, while models that saturate rapidly memorize rather than learn. We identify reasoning faithfulness, defined as the extent to which intermediate steps logically support the final answer, as the pre-RL property that predicts which regime a model falls into, while output diversity alone is uninformative. Motivated by these findings, we disentangle the contributions of continual pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, finding that SFT on explicit reasoning traces is necessary for generalization under weak supervision, while continual pre-training on domain data amplifies the effect. Applied together to Llama3.2-3B-Base, these interventions enable generalization across all three settings where the base model previously failed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型在弱监督下何时能学会推理？
大语言模型通过可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已实现显著的推理能力提升。然而，随着模型能力增强，构建高质量奖励信号愈发困难，因此有必要理解RLVR在较弱监督形式下何时能成功。我们对多种模型家族和推理领域，在三种弱监督设置（数据稀缺、奖励噪声、自监督代理奖励）下进行了系统性实证研究。我们发现，泛化能力由训练奖励饱和动力学决定：泛化能力强的模型在训练奖励与下游性能同步上升的预饱和阶段持续较久，而快速饱和的模型则倾向于记忆而非学习。我们识别出“推理忠实性”——即中间步骤在多大程度上逻辑支持最终答案——作为预测模型落入何种模式的预RL属性，而输出多样性本身无预测价值。受此启发，我们分离了持续预训练与监督微调的贡献，发现显式推理轨迹上的监督微调对弱监督下的泛化至关重要，而领域数据上的持续预训练则增强该效应。将这些干预措施应用于Llama3.2-3B-Base模型，使其在原先失败的三种弱监督设置下均实现了泛化。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates when large language models can reason effectively under weak supervision, motivated by the growing difficulty of constructing high-quality rewards for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. The authors empirically evaluate models across diverse families and reasoning tasks under three weak supervision conditions: scarce data, noisy rewards, and self-supervised proxy rewards. They find that generalization depends on reward saturation dynamics—models that generalize show a prolonged pre-saturation phase where training reward and downstream performance rise together, while those that saturate quickly memorize. Reasoning faithfulness, not output diversity, predicts whether a model will generalize, and the study shows that supervised fine-tuning on explicit reasoning traces is essential for generalization under weak supervision, while continual pre-training enhances its effect. Applied together, these methods enable Llama3.2-3B-Base to generalize across all three weak supervision settings where the base model previously failed.
该研究探讨大语言模型在弱监督条件下何时能有效进行推理，以应对随着模型能力增强而日益困难的高质量奖励信号构建问题。作者在三种弱监督设置（数据稀缺、奖励噪声、自监督代理奖励）下，对不同模型家族和推理任务进行系统性实验。结果发现，模型泛化能力取决于奖励饱和动力学——泛化能力强的模型在训练奖励与下游性能同步上升的预饱和阶段持续更久，而快速饱和的模型则倾向于记忆而非学习。推理忠实性（中间步骤是否逻辑支持最终答案）而非输出多样性，是预测模型是否泛化的关键预训练属性。研究进一步表明，显式推理轨迹上的监督微调对弱监督下的泛化至关重要，而持续预训练则能增强该效果。将二者应用于Llama3.2-3B-Base后，该模型在原本无法泛化的所有三种弱监督场景中均实现了泛化。
T-REN: Learning Text-Aligned Region Tokens Improves Dense Vision-Language Alignment and Scalability
Authors: Savya Khosla, Sethuraman T, Aryan Chadha, Alex Schwing, Derek Hoiem
First: 2026-04-20T17:57:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:57:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite recent progress, vision-language encoders struggle with two core limitations: (1) weak alignment between language and dense vision features, which hurts tasks like open-vocabulary semantic segmentation; and (2) high token counts for fine-grained visual representations, which limits scalability to long videos. This work addresses both limitations. We propose T-REN (Text-aligned Region Encoder Network), an efficient encoder that maps visual data to a compact set of text-aligned region-level representations (or region tokens). T-REN achieves this through a lightweight network added on top of a frozen vision backbone, trained to pool patch-level representations within each semantic region into region tokens and align them with region-level text annotations. With only 3.7% additional parameters compared to the vision-language backbone, this design yields substantially stronger dense cross-modal understanding while reducing the token count by orders of magnitude. Specifically, T-REN delivers +5.9 mIoU on ADE20K open-vocabulary segmentation, +18.4% recall on COCO object-level text-image retrieval, +15.6% recall on Ego4D video object localization, and +17.6% mIoU on VSPW video scene parsing, all while reducing token counts by more than 24x for images and 187x for videos compared to the patch-based vision-language backbone. The code and model are available at https://github.com/savya08/T-REN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：T-REN：学习文本对齐的区域令牌可提升密集视觉-语言对齐与可扩展性
尽管近期取得进展，视觉-语言编码器仍面临两大核心限制：(1) 语言与密集视觉特征间对齐较弱，影响开放词汇语义分割等任务；(2) 细粒度视觉表征所需令牌数量高，限制其在长视频中的可扩展性。本工作同时解决这两个问题。我们提出T-REN（文本对齐区域编码器网络），一种高效编码器，将视觉数据映射为紧凑的文本对齐区域级表征（区域令牌）。T-REN通过在冻结的视觉主干网络上添加轻量级网络实现，该网络训练用于将每个语义区域内的块级表征聚合为区域令牌，并与区域级文本标注对齐。相比视觉-语言主干网络，该设计仅增加3.7%的参数，却显著增强密集跨模态理解能力，并将令牌数量降低数个数量级。具体而言，T-REN在ADE20K开放词汇分割中提升5.9 mIoU，在COCO对象级图文检索中提升18.4%召回率，在Ego4D视频对象定位中提升15.6%召回率，在VSPW视频场景解析中提升17.6% mIoU，同时相比基于块的视觉-语言主干网络，图像令牌数量减少24倍以上，视频令牌数量减少187倍以上。代码与模型见https://github.com/savya08/T-REN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor alignment between visual and language features and the inefficiency of high token counts in dense vision-language models, this work introduces T-REN, a lightweight network that overlays a frozen vision backbone to generate compact, text-aligned region tokens. These tokens are created by pooling patch representations within semantic regions and aligning them with text annotations, requiring only 3.7% extra parameters. Experimental results show T-REN significantly improves dense cross-modal performance: +5.9 mIoU on ADE20K segmentation, +18.4% recall on COCO retrieval, +15.6% recall on Ego4D localization, and +17.6% mIoU on VSPW parsing, while reducing token counts by over 24x for images and 187x for videos compared to patch-based baselines.
为解决视觉-语言模型中跨模态对齐弱和令牌数量高的问题，T-REN提出一种轻量级网络，利用冻结的视觉主干将视觉补丁映射为紧凑的、与文本对齐的区域令牌。该网络通过在每个语义区域聚合补丁特征并将其与文本标注对齐进行训练，仅增加3.7%的参数，同时将令牌数量减少24倍以上（图像）和187倍（视频）。该设计显著提升密集视觉-语言任务性能：在ADE20K开放词汇分割中提升5.9 mIoU，在COCO文本-图像检索中提升18.4%召回率，在Ego4D视频目标定位中提升15.6%召回率，在VSPW视频场景解析中提升17.6% mIoU，兼具更强对齐能力和更高可扩展性。
Mechanisms of Multimodal Synchronization: Insights from Decoder-Based Video-Text-to-Speech Synthesis
Authors: Akshita Gupta, Tatiana Likhomanenko, Karren Dai Yang, Richard He Bai, Zakaria Aldeneh, Navdeep Jaitly
First: 2024-11-26T18:57:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:56:40+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, Decoder-only model, Speech Synthesis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unified decoder-only transformers have shown promise for multimodal generation, yet the mechanisms by which they synchronize modalities with heterogeneous sampling rates remain underexplored. We investigate these mechanisms through video-text-to-speech (VTTS) synthesis-a controlled task requiring fine-grained temporal alignment between sparse text, video, and continuous speech. Using a unified decoder-only transformer, dubbed Visatronic, trained on VoxCeleb2, we study: (i) how modalities contribute complementary information, (ii) how positional encoding strategies enable synchronization across heterogeneous rates, (iii) how modality ordering shapes the trade-off between in-domain performance and cross-domain transfer, (iv) how phoneme-level synchronization metrics provide diagnostic insight into per-phoneme timing errors. Our findings reveal that both "global sequential indexing'' (unique position IDs across modalities) and "co-temporal ordered indexing'' (identical IDs for temporally corresponding tokens) achieve strong synchronization performance, with co-temporal ordered indexing providing a simple mechanism without explicit timestamp metadata. Both text and video contribute complementary signals: text ensures intelligibility while video provides temporal cues and emotional expressiveness. Modality ordering reveals a consistent trade-off: video-first ordering achieves stronger in-domain performance while text-first ordering generalizes more robustly to unseen domains. Our findings also reveal, that diverse large-scale training enables transferable synchronization strategies. To enable fine-grained analysis, we also introduce TimeSync, a phoneme-level metric that reveals temporal misalignments overlooked by frame-level metrics. These insights establish VTTS as a valuable testbed for understanding temporal synchronization in unified multimodal decoders.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态同步机制：基于解码器的视频-文本到语音合成的启示
统一的仅解码器Transformer在多模态生成中展现出潜力，但其如何在异步采样率下同步模态的机制仍待探索。我们通过视频-文本到语音（VTTS）合成这一受控任务进行研究，该任务要求在稀疏文本、视频与连续语音之间实现精细的时间对齐。使用在VoxCeleb2上训练的统一仅解码器Transformer Visatronic，我们探讨：(i) 各模态如何贡献互补信息，(ii) 位置编码策略如何实现异步率下的同步，(iii) 模态顺序如何影响域内性能与跨域迁移的权衡，(iv) 音素级同步度量如何诊断音素级时间误差。研究发现，"全局顺序索引"（各模态间唯一位置ID）与"共时有序索引"（时序对应音素共享ID）均能实现强同步性能，其中共时有序索引无需显式时间戳元数据即可实现。文本与视频贡献互补信号：文本确保可理解性，视频提供时序线索与情感表达。模态顺序揭示一致权衡：视频优先顺序提升域内性能，文本优先顺序更稳健地泛化至未见域。研究还表明，大规模多样化训练可实现可迁移的同步策略。为支持精细分析，我们引入TimeSync，一种音素级度量，揭示帧级度量忽略的时序错位。这些发现确立VTTS为理解统一多模态解码器中时序同步机制的重要测试平台。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how unified decoder-only transformers synchronize heterogeneous modalities—text, video, and speech—in video-text-to-speech synthesis, motivated by the lack of understanding of temporal alignment mechanisms in multimodal generation. Using Visatronic, a model trained on VoxCeleb2, the authors analyze how modalities contribute complementary information, how positional encoding strategies enable synchronization, and how modality ordering affects in-domain vs. cross-domain performance. They find that both global sequential indexing and co-temporal ordered indexing achieve strong synchronization, with the latter being simpler and timestamp-free. Text ensures intelligibility while video adds temporal cues and emotion; video-first ordering boosts in-domain performance but text-first ordering generalizes better. They also introduce TimeSync, a phoneme-level metric that detects misalignments missed by frame-level metrics, revealing that large-scale training enables transferable synchronization strategies.
为理解统一解码器模型如何同步采样率不同的多模态数据（如视频、文本和语音），该研究以视频-文本到语音合成作为可控测试平台。基于VoxCeleb2训练的Visatronic模型分析了各模态如何提供互补信息、位置编码如何实现同步、以及模态顺序如何影响域内与跨域性能。结果表明，全局顺序索引和共时有序索引均能实现良好同步，后者无需显式时间戳；文本保障可懂度，视频提供时序与情感线索；视频优先顺序提升域内性能，文本优先顺序增强跨域泛化能力。新提出的音素级指标TimeSync揭示了帧级指标忽略的时序误差，证明大规模训练可实现可迁移的同步策略。
Back into Plato's Cave: Examining Cross-modal Representational Convergence at Scale
Authors: A. Sophia Koepke, Daniil Zverev, Shiry Ginosar, Alexei A. Efros
First: 2026-04-20T17:56:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:56:02+00:00
Comments: Project page: http://akoepke.github.io/cave_umwelten/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that neural networks trained on different modalities (e.g., text and images) align and eventually converge toward the same representation of reality. If true, this has significant implications for whether modality choice matters at all. We show that the experimental evidence for this hypothesis is fragile and depends critically on the evaluation regime. Alignment is measured using mutual nearest neighbors on small datasets ($\approx$1K samples) and degrades substantially as the dataset is scaled to millions of samples. The alignment that remains between model representations reflects coarse semantic overlap rather than consistent fine-grained structure. Moreover, the evaluations in Huh et al. are done in a one-to-one image-caption setting, a constraint that breaks down in realistic many-to-many settings and further reduces alignment. We also find that the reported trend of stronger language models increasingly aligning with vision does not appear to hold for newer models. Overall, our findings suggest that the current evidence for cross-modal representational convergence is considerably weaker than subsequent works have taken it to be. Models trained on different modalities may learn equally rich representations of the world, just not the same one.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重返柏拉图的洞穴：大规模考察跨模态表征收敛性
柏拉图表征假说认为，不同模态（如文本与图像）训练的神经网络会趋于对齐并最终收敛至对现实的同一表征。若成立，这将影响模态选择是否重要的判断。我们发现，该假说的实证证据脆弱，高度依赖评估环境。在小数据集（约1K样本）上，通过互近邻测量对齐度，但当数据集扩展至百万样本时，对齐度显著下降。剩余的表征对齐仅反映粗粒度语义重叠，而非一致的细粒度结构。此外，Huh等人的评估仅在一对一图像-文本设置下进行，而在现实中的多对多场景中该约束失效，进一步削弱对齐。我们还发现，报告中语言模型与视觉模型对齐度随模型增强而提升的趋势，在新模型中不成立。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，当前跨模态表征收敛性的证据远弱于后续研究所认为的强度。不同模态训练的模型可能学习到同样丰富的世界表征，但并非同一表征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the Platonic Representation Hypothesis that neural networks across modalities converge toward shared representations of reality, this study investigates whether such alignment holds at scale. Using mutual nearest neighbors as a metric, the authors find that alignment degrades significantly when scaling from small (≈1K) to large (millions) datasets, indicating that observed convergence is superficial and reflects only coarse semantic overlap. Evaluations in prior work, constrained to one-to-one image-caption pairs, are shown to be unrealistic and further reduce alignment. Crucially, the trend of stronger language models aligning more closely with vision models does not persist in newer architectures. The results collectively suggest that cross-modal convergence is fragile and overstated in prior literature, with models learning distinct but equally rich representations.
基于‘柏拉图表征假说’——即不同模态的神经网络会收敛到对现实的共同表征——本研究检验了该假设在大规模数据下的有效性。通过互近邻度量发现，当数据规模从约1K样本扩展到百万级时，模态间对齐显著下降，仅保留粗粒度语义重叠而非精细结构一致性。研究还指出，先前工作依赖于受限的一对一图文配对设置，且新模型中语言模型与视觉模型的对齐趋势并未延续，最终认为当前跨模态表征收敛的实证支持远弱于后续研究所假设的那样。
A multimodal and temporal foundation model for virtual patient representations at healthcare system scale
Authors: Andrew Zhang, Tong Ding, Sophia J. Wagner, Caiwei Tian, Ming Y. Lu, Rowland Pettit, Joshua E. Lewis, Alexandre Misrahi, Dandan Mo, Long Phi Le, Faisal Mahmood
First: 2026-04-20T17:55:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:55:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern medicine generates vast multimodal data across siloed systems, yet no existing model integrates the full breadth and temporal depth of the clinical record into a unified patient representation. We introduce Apollo, a multimodal temporal foundation model trained and evaluated on over three decades of longitudinal hospital records from a major US hospital system, composed of 25 billion records from 7.2 million patients, representing 28 distinct medical modalities and 12 major medical specialties. Apollo learns a unified representation space integrating over 100 thousand unique medical events in our clinical vocabulary as well as images and clinical text. This "atlas of medical concepts" forms a computational substrate for modeling entire patient care journeys comprised of sequences of structured and unstructured events, which are compressed by Apollo into virtual patient representations. To assess the potential of these whole-patient representations, we created 322 prognosis and retrieval tasks from a held-out test set of 1.4 million patients. We demonstrate the generalized clinical forecasting potential of Apollo embeddings, including predicting new disease onset risk up to five years in advance (95 tasks), disease progression (78 tasks), treatment response (59 tasks), risk of treatment-related adverse events (17 tasks), and hospital operations endpoints (12 tasks). Using feature attribution techniques, we show that model predictions align with clinically-interpretable multimodal biomarkers. We evaluate semantic similarity search on 61 retrieval tasks, and moreover demonstrate the potential of Apollo as a multimodal medical search engine using text and image queries. Together, these modeling capabilities establish the foundation for computable medicine, where the full context of patient care becomes accessible to computational reasoning.
