标题：面向医疗系统规模的虚拟患者多模态与时间基础模型
现代医学在隔离系统中生成海量多模态数据，但尚无现有模型能将临床记录的全部广度与时间深度整合为统一的患者表征。我们提出了Apollo，一个在一家主要美国医院系统超过三十年纵向住院记录上训练和评估的多模态时间基础模型，包含720万患者、250亿条记录，涵盖28种不同医学模态和12个主要医学专科。Apollo学习了一个统一表征空间，整合了临床词汇中的逾十万种独特医学事件，以及图像和临床文本。这一“医学概念图谱”构成了建模完整患者护理历程（由结构化与非结构化事件序列组成）的计算基础，Apollo将这些历程压缩为虚拟患者表征。为评估这些全患者表征的潜力，我们从140万患者保留测试集中创建了322个预后与检索任务。我们展示了Apollo嵌入的泛化临床预测能力，包括预测五年内新发疾病风险（95项任务）、疾病进展（78项任务）、治疗反应（59项任务）、治疗相关不良事件风险（17项任务）及医院运营终点（12项任务）。通过特征归因技术，我们表明模型预测与临床可解释的多模态生物标志物一致。我们在61项检索任务中评估语义相似性搜索，并进一步展示了Apollo作为支持文本与图像查询的多模态医学搜索引擎的潜力。这些建模能力共同奠定了可计算医学的基础，使患者护理的完整上下文可被计算推理所访问。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to unify fragmented clinical data into coherent patient representations, Apollo is a multimodal temporal foundation model trained on 25 billion records from 7.2 million patients across 28 medical modalities and 12 specialties over three decades. It learns a shared embedding space encoding over 100,000 medical events, images, and text, compressing patient histories into virtual representations. Evaluated on 322 tasks from 1.4 million patients, Apollo successfully forecasts disease onset, progression, treatment response, adverse events, and hospital outcomes up to five years ahead, with clinically interpretable biomarkers identified via attribution. It also enables multimodal semantic search, demonstrating potential as a medical search engine.
为整合分散的临床数据并构建统一的患者表征，Apollo 是一个在三十余年、涵盖720万患者、28种医学模态和12个专科的250亿条医院记录上训练的多模态时序基础模型。它学习了一个编码超10万医学事件、图像与文本的共享嵌入空间，将患者纵向医疗历程压缩为虚拟表征。在140万患者组成的测试集上评估322项任务，Apollo成功预测疾病发生（最长5年）、进展、治疗反应、不良事件及医院运营结局等，覆盖95+任务，预测结果与可解释的多模态生物标志物一致，并支持文本与图像查询的语义检索，展现其作为临床搜索引擎的潜力。
Revisiting Active Sequential Prediction-Powered Mean Estimation
Authors: Maria-Eleni Sfyraki, Jun-Kun Wang
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-20T17:55:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:55:17+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we revisit the problem of active sequential prediction-powered mean estimation, where at each round one must decide the query probability of the ground-truth label upon observing the covariates of a sample. Furthermore, if the label is not queried, the prediction from a machine learning model is used instead. Prior work proposed an elegant scheme that determines the query probability by combining an uncertainty-based suggestion with a constant probability that encodes a soft constraint on the query probability. We explored different values of the mixing parameter and observed an intriguing empirical pattern: the smallest confidence width tends to occur when the weight on the constant probability is close to one, thereby reducing the influence of the uncertainty-based component. Motivated by this observation, we develop a non-asymptotic analysis of the estimator and establish a data-dependent bound on its confidence interval. Our analysis further suggests that when a no-regret learning approach is used to determine the query probability and control this bound, the query probability converges to the constraint of the max value of the query probability when it is chosen obliviously to the current covariates. We also conduct simulations that corroborate these theoretical findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新审视基于主动序列预测的均值估计
本文重新探讨了主动序列预测驱动的均值估计问题，其中在每一轮中，需根据样本协变量决定查询真实标签的概率。若未查询标签，则使用机器学习模型的预测结果。先前工作提出了一种优雅方案，通过结合基于不确定性的建议与一个编码查询概率软约束的常数概率来确定查询概率。我们探索了不同混合参数值，观察到一个引人注目的经验模式：当常数概率的权重接近1时，置信区间宽度最小，从而降低基于不确定性的成分影响。受此启发，我们对估计量进行了非渐近分析，并建立了其置信区间的数据依赖性界。进一步分析表明，当采用无遗憾学习方法确定查询概率并控制该界时，查询概率会收敛到查询概率上限（当该上限独立于当前协变量选择时）。我们还通过仿真验证了这些理论发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by empirical observations that minimizing confidence width in active sequential prediction-powered mean estimation often occurs when query probability is dominated by a constant constraint rather than uncertainty, this work develops a non-asymptotic analysis to derive a data-dependent bound on the estimator’s confidence interval. The method combines uncertainty-based query suggestions with a soft constraint via a mixing parameter, and theoretical analysis shows that using a no-regret learning approach to control query probability leads to convergence toward the maximum allowed query probability. Simulations confirm that this approach achieves tighter confidence intervals and aligns with the empirical pattern that prioritizing the constant component improves estimation accuracy.
鉴于实证发现主动序列预测驱动的均值估计中，当查询概率主要受固定约束而非不确定性驱动时，置信区间宽度最小，本文通过非渐近分析推导出数据依赖的置信区间上界。方法结合不确定性建议与软约束混合参数，理论分析表明，采用无悔学习策略控制查询概率时，其会收敛至最大允许查询概率（当该概率独立于当前协变量选择时）。仿真结果验证了理论预测，即固定成分主导时置信区间最窄。
DualToken: Towards Unifying Visual Understanding and Generation with Dual Visual Vocabularies
Authors: Wei Song, Yuran Wang, Zijia Song, Yadong Li, Zenan Zhou, Long Chen, Jianhua Xu, Jiaqi Wang, Kaicheng Yu
First: 2025-03-18T14:56:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:55:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The differing representation spaces required for visual understanding and generation pose a challenge in unifying them within the autoregressive paradigm of large language models. A vision tokenizer trained for reconstruction excels at capturing low-level visual appearance, making it well-suited for visual generation but lacking high-level semantic representations for understanding tasks. Conversely, a vision encoder trained via contrastive learning aligns well with language but struggles to decode back into the pixel space for generation tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose DualToken, a method that unifies representations for both understanding and generation within a single tokenizer. However, directly integrating reconstruction and semantic objectives creates conflicts, leading to degraded performance in both reconstruction fidelity and semantic accuracy. Instead of forcing a single codebook to capture both visual appearance and semantics, DualToken disentangles them by introducing separate codebooks for high-level semantics and low-level visual details. As a result, DualToken achieves 0.25 rFID and 82.0% zero-shot accuracy on ImageNet, and demonstrates strong effectiveness in downstream MLLM tasks for both understanding and generation. Specifically, our method surpasses VILA-U by 5.8 points on average across ten visual understanding benchmarks and delivers a 13% improvement on GenAI-Bench. Notably, incorporating dual visual tokens outperforms using a single token type on both understanding and generation tasks. We hope our research offers a new perspective on leveraging dual visual vocabularies for building unified vision-language models. Project page is available at https://songweii.github.io/dualtoken-project-page.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DualToken：通过双视觉词汇统一视觉理解与生成
视觉理解与生成所需的表征空间不同，这在自回归范式的大型语言模型中统一二者构成挑战。为重建训练的视觉分词器擅长捕捉低级视觉外观，适合视觉生成但缺乏高级语义表征以支持理解任务；而通过对比学习训练的视觉编码器与语言对齐良好，但在生成任务中难以解码回像素空间。为弥合此差距，我们提出DualToken，一种在单一分词器内统一理解与生成表征的方法。然而，直接整合重建与语义目标会导致二者性能下降。因此，DualToken通过引入分别针对高级语义与低级视觉细节的独立码本，将二者解耦。结果，DualToken在ImageNet上实现0.25 rFID和82.0%零样本准确率，并在下游多模态大模型任务中展现出强大的理解与生成能力。具体而言，我们的方法在十项视觉理解基准上平均超越VILA-U 5.8分，在GenAI-Bench上提升13%。值得注意的是，采用双视觉令牌在理解与生成任务中均优于单一令牌类型。我们希望本研究为构建统一视觉-语言模型提供新的视角。项目页面见https://songweii.github.io/dualtoken-project-page。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of unifying visual understanding and generation within autoregressive large language models, DualToken introduces a dual-codebook tokenizer that separates high-level semantic representations from low-level visual details, avoiding conflicts between reconstruction and semantic objectives. Instead of forcing a single codebook to handle both tasks, it employs distinct vocabularies for each, enabling better performance in both domains. Experimentally, DualToken achieves 0.25 rFID and 82.0% zero-shot accuracy on ImageNet, outperforms VILA-U by 5.8 points across ten visual understanding benchmarks, and improves GenAI-Bench by 13%, demonstrating superior effectiveness over single-token approaches for both understanding and generation.
为解决视觉理解与生成在自回归视觉语言模型中难以统一的问题，DualToken提出双码本分治策略，将高层语义与低层视觉细节分离，避免重建与语义目标间的冲突。其采用两个独立码本：一个通过对比学习优化语义对齐，另一个专攻像素级重建。该设计在ImageNet上实现0.25 rFID和82.0%零样本准确率，并在十项视觉理解基准上超越VILA-U 5.8分，GenAI-Bench提升13%，证明其在理解与生成任务中均优于单码本方案。
Latent Phase-Shift Rollback: Inference-Time Error Correction via Residual Stream Monitoring and KV-Cache Steering
Authors: Manan Gupta, Dhruv Kumar
First: 2026-04-20T17:53:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:53:33+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models frequently commit unrecoverable reasoning errors mid-generation: once a wrong step is taken, subsequent tokens compound the mistake rather than correct it. We introduce $\textbf{Latent Phase-Shift Rollback}$ (LPSR): at each generation step, we monitor the residual stream at a critical layer lcrit, detect abrupt directional reversals (phase shifts) via a cosine-similarity $+$ entropy dual gate, and respond by rolling back the KV-cache and injecting a pre-computed steering vector. No fine-tuning, gradient computation, or additional forward passes are required. LPSR achieves $\mathbf{44.0\%}$ on MATH-500 with an 8B model versus $28.8\%$ for standard AR ($+15.2$ pp; McNemar $χ^2 = 66.96$, $p < 10^{-15}$). Critically, prompted self-correction, the most natural inference-time baseline, scores only $19.8\%$, below standard AR; LPSR exceeds it by $+24.2$ pp ($χ^2 = 89.4$, $p \approx 0$). LPSR also outperforms Best-of-16 ($+7.8$ pp) at $5.4\times$ lower token cost, and surpasses a standard 70B model ($35.2\%$) with $8.75\times$ fewer parameters at ${\sim}3\times$ the token budget. A 32-layer sweep reveals a novel \textbf{detection-correction dissociation}: error-detection AUC peaks at layer~14 ($0.718$) but task accuracy peaks at layer~16 ($44.0\%$ vs.\ $29.2\%$), demonstrating that optimal monitoring depth differs for detection and correction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜相位偏移回滚：通过残差流监控与KV缓存引导实现推理时错误校正
大语言模型在生成过程中常出现不可恢复的推理错误：一旦迈出错误步骤，后续token会加剧而非纠正错误。我们提出\textbf{潜相位偏移回滚}（LPSR）：在每一步生成中，监控关键层lcrit的残差流，通过余弦相似度+熵双门控检测突发方向反转（相位偏移），并响应式回滚KV缓存并注入预计算的引导向量。无需微调、梯度计算或额外前向传播。LPSR在8B模型上于MATH-500任务中达到\mathbf{44.0\%}，相较标准自回归（AR）的28.8\%提升15.2个百分点（McNemar $χ^2 = 66.96$, $p < 10^{-15}$）。关键的是，提示式自我校正——最自然的推理时基线——仅得19.8\%，低于标准AR；LPSR比其高出24.2个百分点（$χ^2 = 89.4$, $p \approx 0$）。LPSR还以5.4倍更低的token成本超越Best-of-16（+7.8个百分点），并在参数量仅为标准70B模型8.75倍、token预算约3倍的情况下超越其（35.2\%）。32层扫描揭示了\textbf{检测-校正分离}的新现象：错误检测AUC在第14层达峰值（0.718），但任务准确率在第16层最高（44.0\% vs. 29.2\%），表明检测与校正的最优监控深度不同。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem that large language models often compound reasoning errors during generation, the paper proposes Latent Phase-Shift Rollback (LPSR), a method that monitors the residual stream at a critical layer to detect directional reversals via cosine similarity and entropy gating, then rolls back the KV-cache and injects a precomputed steering vector to correct errors without fine-tuning or extra computation. Experiments show LPSR achieves 44.0% accuracy on MATH-500 with an 8B model, outperforming standard autoregressive generation by 15.2 percentage points and surpassing prompted self-correction by 24.2 points. It also beats Best-of-16 with 5.4x lower token cost and matches a 70B model with 8.75x fewer parameters at 3x the token budget, revealing a dissociation between detection and correction performance across layers.
为解决大语言模型在生成过程中常累积推理错误的问题，本文提出Latent Phase-Shift Rollback（LPSR）方法，通过在关键层监控残差流，利用余弦相似度与熵双门控检测方向突变，随后回滚KV缓存并注入预计算的引导向量以纠正错误，无需微调或额外计算。实验表明，LPSR在8B模型上于MATH-500任务中达到44.0%准确率，较标准自回归生成提升15.2个百分点，远超提示式自我纠正（+24.2个百分点）。同时，LPSR以更低的token成本击败Best-of-16（+7.8个百分点），并以8.75倍更少参数超越70B模型（35.2%），且token预算为三倍。层扫描发现，错误检测最佳层为14层（AUC 0.718），而纠错准确率峰值在16层（44.0% vs 29.2%），揭示检测与纠正的最优层存在分离。
Benchmarking System Dynamics AI Assistants: Cloud Versus Local LLMs on CLD Extraction and Discussion
Authors: Terry Leitch
First: 2026-04-20T17:53:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:53:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a systematic evaluation of large language model families -- spanning both proprietary cloud APIs and locally-hosted open-source models -- on two purpose-built benchmarks for System Dynamics AI assistance: the \textbf{CLD Leaderboard} (53 tests, structured causal loop diagram extraction) and the \textbf{Discussion Leaderboard} (interactive model discussion, feedback explanation, and model building coaching). On CLD extraction, cloud models achieve 77--89\% overall pass rates; the best local model reaches 77\% (Kimi~K2.5~GGUF~Q3, zero-shot engine), matching mid-tier cloud performance. On Discussion, the best local models achieve 50--100\% on model building steps and 47--75\% on feedback explanation, but only 0--50\% on error fixing -- a category dominated by long-context prompts that expose memory limits in local deployments. A central contribution of this paper is a systematic analysis of \textit{model type effects} on performance: we compare reasoning vs.\ instruction-tuned architectures, GGUF (llama.cpp) vs.\ MLX (mlx\_lm) backends, and quantization levels (Q3 / Q4\_K\_M / MLX-3bit / MLX-4bit / MLX-6bit) across the same underlying model families. We find that backend choice has larger practical impact than quantization level: mlx\_lm does not enforce JSON schema constraints, requiring explicit prompt-level JSON instructions, while llama.cpp grammar-constrained sampling handles JSON reliably but causes indefinite generation on long-context prompts for dense models. We document the full parameter sweep ($t$, $p$, $k$) for all local models, cleaned timing data (stuck requests excluded), and a practitioner guide for running 671B--123B parameter models on Apple~Silicon.
中文标题/摘要
标题：系统动力学AI助手基准测试：云端与本地LLM在CLD提取与讨论中的对比
我们对大型语言模型家族（涵盖专有云API与本地部署的开源模型）在两个为系统动力学AI辅助设计的基准测试中进行了系统性评估：\textbf{CLD排行榜}（53项测试，结构化因果回路图提取）和\textbf{讨论排行榜}（模型交互讨论、反馈解释与模型构建指导）。 在CLD提取方面，云端模型整体通过率77%–89%；最佳本地模型达到77%（Kimi~K2.5~GGUF~Q3，零样本引擎），与中等水平云端模型表现相当。在讨论方面，最佳本地模型在模型构建步骤上得分50%–100%，反馈解释上47%–75%，但错误修复仅0%–50%——该类别主要由长上下文提示驱动，暴露了本地部署的内存限制。 本文的核心贡献是对\textit{模型类型效应}的系统性分析：我们比较了推理型与指令调优架构、GGUF（llama.cpp）与MLX（mlx_lm）后端、以及量化级别（Q3 / Q4_K_M / MLX-3bit / MLX-4bit / MLX-6bit）在相同底层模型家族中的表现。发现后端选择比量化级别对实际性能影响更大：mlx_lm不强制JSON模式约束，需显式提示级JSON指令；而llama.cpp基于语法约束的采样能可靠处理JSON，但在密集模型的长上下文提示下会导致无限生成。 我们记录了所有本地模型的完整参数扫描（t, p, k），清理后的计时数据（排除卡顿请求），并提供在Apple Silicon上运行671B–123B参数模型的实践指南。
Summary / 总结
This paper evaluates cloud-based and locally-hosted large language models on two benchmarks for System Dynamics AI assistance: CLD extraction and interactive discussion. Motivated by the need to compare deployment trade-offs, the study finds that cloud models outperform local ones on structured CLD extraction (77–89% vs. 77%), while local models excel in model-building steps (50–100%) and feedback explanation (47–75%), but underperform in error fixing (0–50%) due to memory constraints. The authors systematically analyze model architecture, backend (llama.cpp vs. MLX), and quantization levels, concluding that backend choice has a greater practical impact than quantization: MLX lacks JSON schema enforcement, while llama.cpp handles JSON reliably but struggles with long-context prompts. The paper also provides full parameter sweeps and timing data for local models, along with a guide for running large models on Apple Silicon.
为比较云服务与本地大语言模型在系统动力学AI任务中的表现，本研究评估了CLD提取与交互讨论两个基准。在CLD提取中，云模型表现略优，最佳本地模型（Kimi K2.5 GGUF Q3）达到77%，与中等云模型持平。在讨论任务中，顶级本地模型在模型构建（50–100%）和反馈解释（47–75%）上表现良好，但在错误修复（0–50%）上表现不佳，因本地部署存在内存限制。研究还分析了模型架构、后端（llama.cpp vs. MLX）及量化级别，发现后端选择比量化级别影响更大：MLX不强制JSON格式，而llama.cpp虽能可靠处理JSON，但在长上下文提示下对密集模型易卡顿。
MultiWorld: Scalable Multi-Agent Multi-View Video World Models
Authors: Haoyu Wu, Jiwen Yu, Yingtian Zou, Xihui Liu
First: 2026-04-20T17:52:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:52:15+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video world models have achieved remarkable success in simulating environmental dynamics in response to actions by users or agents. They are modeled as action-conditioned video generation models that take historical frames and current actions as input to predict future frames. Yet, most existing approaches are limited to single-agent scenarios and fail to capture the complex interactions inherent in real-world multi-agent systems. We present \textbf{MultiWorld}, a unified framework for multi-agent multi-view world modeling that enables accurate control of multiple agents while maintaining multi-view consistency. We introduce the Multi-Agent Condition Module to achieve precise multi-agent controllability, and the Global State Encoder to ensure coherent observations across different views. MultiWorld supports flexible scaling of agent and view counts, and synthesizes different views in parallel for high efficiency. Experiments on multi-player game environments and multi-robot manipulation tasks demonstrate that MultiWorld outperforms baselines in video fidelity, action-following ability, and multi-view consistency. Project page: https://multi-world.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：MultiWorld：可扩展的多智能体多视角视频世界模型
视频世界模型在模拟环境对用户或智能体行为的动态响应方面取得了显著成功，其建模为基于动作条件的视频生成模型，以历史帧和当前动作作为输入预测未来帧。然而，现有大多数方法仅限于单智能体场景，无法捕捉现实世界多智能体系统中固有的复杂交互。我们提出\textbf{MultiWorld}，一个统一的多智能体多视角世界建模框架，可在保持多视角一致性的同时实现对多个智能体的精确控制。我们引入多智能体条件模块以实现精准的多智能体可控性，并引入全局状态编码器以确保不同视角间观测的一致性。MultiWorld支持灵活扩展智能体与视角数量，并可并行合成不同视角以提高效率。在多人游戏环境和多机器人操作任务上的实验表明，MultiWorld在视频保真度、动作跟随能力及多视角一致性方面均优于基线方法。项目页面：https://multi-world.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model complex multi-agent interactions beyond single-agent video world models, MultiWorld introduces a unified framework that enables scalable, multi-view video generation under multi-agent control. It achieves precise agent controllability via a Multi-Agent Condition Module and maintains cross-view consistency through a Global State Encoder. Experiments across multi-player games and multi-robot manipulation tasks show MultiWorld outperforms existing methods in video fidelity, action adherence, and multi-view coherence, while supporting flexible scaling of agents and views.
为建模视频环境中复杂的多智能体交互，MultiWorld提出一个统一框架，支持多智能体多视角的世界建模。通过多智能体条件模块实现精确的智能体控制，通过全局状态编码器确保多视角观测一致性。在多人游戏与多机器人操作任务中的实验表明，MultiWorld在视频保真度、动作跟随能力与多视角一致性方面显著优于现有方法，同时支持智能体与视角数量的灵活扩展。
Dual Alignment Between Language Model Layers and Human Sentence Processing
Authors: Tatsuki Kuribayashi, Alex Warstadt, Yohei Oseki, Ethan Gotlieb Wilcox
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-20T17:51:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:51:26+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A recent study (Kuribayashi et al., 2025) has shown that human sentence processing behavior, typically measured on syntactically unchallenging constructions, can be effectively modeled using surprisal from early layers of large language models (LLMs). This raises the question of whether such advantages of internal layers extend to more syntactically challenging constructions, where surprisal has been reported to underestimate human cognitive effort. In this paper, we begin by exploring internal layers that better estimate human cognitive effort observed in syntactic ambiguity processing in English. Our experiments show that, in contrast to naturalistic reading, later layers better estimate such a cognitive effort, but still underestimate the human data. This dual alignment sheds light on different modes of sentence processing in humans and LMs: naturalistic reading employs a somewhat weak prediction akin to earlier layers of LMs, while syntactically challenging processing requires more fully-contextualized representations, better modeled by later layers of LMs. Motivated by these findings, we also explore several probability-update measures using shallow and deep layers of LMs, showing a complementary advantage to single-layer's surprisal in reading time modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型层与人类句法处理之间的双重对齐
近期研究（Kuribayashi 等，2025）表明，人类句法处理行为（通常在句法挑战性较低的结构中测量）可通过大型语言模型（LLMs）早期层的惊讶值有效建模。这引发了一个问题：这种早期层的优势是否也适用于句法挑战性更高的结构？在这些结构中，惊讶值被报告为低估了人类认知努力。本文首先探索能更好估计英语句法歧义处理中人类认知努力的内部层。实验表明，与自然阅读相比，后期层更能估计此类认知努力，但仍低估人类数据。这种双重对齐揭示了人类与语言模型在句法处理中的不同模式：自然阅读采用类似LLM早期层的较弱预测，而句法挑战性处理则需要更全面上下文的表示，更适合由LLM后期层建模。受此启发，我们还探索了LLM浅层与深层的概率更新度量，显示其在阅读时间建模中对单层惊讶值具有互补优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that early layers of large language models (LLMs) can model human sentence processing in simple syntactic contexts, this study investigates whether such alignment extends to challenging syntactic ambiguity. The authors compare surprisal from shallow and deep layers of LLMs against human reading time data in ambiguous sentences, finding that later layers better capture cognitive effort in complex cases but still underpredict human performance. This dual alignment suggests that human reading involves two modes: a weak-prediction mode resembling early LLM layers for natural reading, and a context-rich mode requiring deeper layers for syntactic resolution. The study further tests probability-update measures across layers, revealing that combining shallow and deep layer outputs yields superior reading time modeling compared to single-layer surprisal.
鉴于早期语言模型层在简单句法结构中能较好模拟人类句法处理，本研究进一步探究其在复杂句法结构（如歧义句）中的表现。实验发现，后期层比早期层更准确反映人类在句法歧义处理中的认知负荷，但仍存在低估现象。这揭示了人类与模型处理的双重模式：自然阅读依赖早期层的弱预测，而句法挑战则需后期层的上下文敏感表征。研究还测试了浅层与深层层的概率更新指标，结果表明多层组合比单一层的惊讶值在阅读时间建模上更具优势。
AnchorSeg: Language Grounded Query Banks for Reasoning Segmentation
Authors: Rui Qian, Chuanhang Deng, Qiang Huang, Jian Xiong, Mingxuan Li, Yingbo Zhou, Wei Zhai, Jintao Chen, Dejing Dou
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-20T17:49:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:49:22+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted to ACL 2026, please refer to https://github.com/rui-qian/AnchorSeg
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reasoning segmentation requires models to ground complex, implicit textual queries into precise pixel-level masks. Existing approaches rely on a single segmentation token $\texttt{<SEG>}$, whose hidden state implicitly encodes both semantic reasoning and spatial localization, limiting the model's ability to explicitly disentangle what to segment from where to segment. We introduce AnchorSeg, which reformulates reasoning segmentation as a structured conditional generation process over image tokens, conditioned on language grounded query banks. Instead of compressing all semantic reasoning and spatial localization into a single embedding, AnchorSeg constructs an ordered sequence of query banks: latent reasoning tokens that capture intermediate semantic states, and a segmentation anchor token that provides explicit spatial grounding. We model spatial conditioning as a factorized distribution over image tokens, where the anchor query determines localization signals while contextual queries provide semantic modulation. To bridge token-level predictions and pixel-level supervision, we propose Token--Mask Cycle Consistency (TMCC), a bidirectional training objective that enforces alignment across resolutions. By explicitly decoupling spatial grounding from semantic reasoning through structured language grounded query banks, AnchorSeg achieves state-of-the-art results on ReasonSeg test set (67.7\% gIoU and 68.1\% cIoU). All code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/rui-qian/AnchorSeg.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnchorSeg：用于推理分割的语言引导查询库
推理分割要求模型将复杂的隐式文本查询精确地映射到像素级掩码。现有方法依赖单一分割标记 $\texttt{<SEG>} $，其隐藏状态隐式编码语义推理与空间定位，限制模型显式分离“分割什么”与“在哪里分割”的能力。我们提出AnchorSeg，将推理分割重构为基于图像标记的结构化条件生成过程，条件为语言引导的查询库。不同于将语义推理与空间定位压缩至单一嵌入，AnchorSeg构建有序查询库序列：捕捉中间语义状态的潜在推理标记，以及提供显式空间定位的分割锚点标记。我们将空间条件建模为图像标记上的因子化分布，其中锚点查询决定定位信号，上下文查询提供语义调制。为弥合标记级预测与像素级监督，我们提出Token--Mask循环一致性（TMCC），双向训练目标强制不同分辨率间对齐。通过结构化语言引导查询库显式解耦空间定位与语义推理，AnchorSeg在ReasonSeg测试集上达到最先进结果（67.7% gIoU 和 68.1% cIoU）。所有代码与模型公开于 https://github.com/rui-qian/AnchorSeg。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing segmentation models that compress both semantic reasoning and spatial localization into a single token, AnchorSeg introduces a structured conditional generation framework conditioned on language-grounded query banks. It decomposes the task into latent reasoning tokens capturing intermediate semantics and a segmentation anchor token providing explicit spatial grounding, modeling spatial conditioning as a factorized distribution over image tokens. The method further employs Token--Mask Cycle Consistency (TMCC) to align token-level predictions with pixel-level supervision across resolutions. Experiments show AnchorSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance on ReasonSeg with 67.7% gIoU and 68.1% cIoU.
为了解决现有方法将语义推理与空间定位压缩到单一token导致的表达能力不足问题，AnchorSeg提出一种基于语言引导查询库的结构化条件生成框架。该方法将任务分解为捕捉中间语义状态的潜在推理token和提供显式空间定位的分割锚点token，并将空间条件建模为图像token上的因子化分布。同时引入Token--Mask循环一致性损失以双向对齐token级预测与像素级监督。实验表明，AnchorSeg在ReasonSeg测试集上达到67.7% gIoU和68.1% cIoU的最先进性能。
Asymptotic behavior of eigenvalues of large rank perturbations of large random matrices
Authors: Ievgenii Afanasiev, Leonid Berlyand, Mariia Kiyashko
First: 2025-07-16T12:29:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:48:50+00:00
Comments: v1: 14 pages, 3 figures; v2: a part of the proof of Lemma 4.2 was revised, 15 pages, 3 figures; v3: the proof was generalized, 20 pages, 3 figures; v4: minor changes in the proof, typos correced, 21 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The paper is concerned with deformed Wigner random matrices. These matrices are closely related to Deep Neural Networks (DNNs): weight matrices of trained DNNs could be represented in the form $R + S$, where $R$ is random and $S$ is highly correlated. The spectrum of such matrices plays a key role in rigorous underpinning of the novel pruning technique based on Random Matrix Theory. In practice, the spectrum of the matrix $S$ can be rather complicated. In this paper, we develop an asymptotic analysis for the case of full rank $S$ with increasing number of outlier eigenvalues.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大秩扰动下大随机矩阵特征值的渐近行为
本文研究变形的Wigner随机矩阵。这类矩阵与深度神经网络（DNN）密切相关：训练好的DNN的权重矩阵可表示为$R + S$的形式，其中$R$为随机矩阵，$S$具有高度相关性。此类矩阵的谱在基于随机矩阵理论的新型剪枝技术的严格理论支撑中起关键作用。实践中，矩阵$S$的谱可能相当复杂。本文针对$S$为满秩且具有递增数量异常特征值的情形，发展了渐近分析方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spectral properties of weight matrices in trained Deep Neural Networks, which can be modeled as rank-perturbed random matrices, this paper investigates the asymptotic behavior of eigenvalues under large rank perturbations. The authors develop a theoretical framework to analyze the spectrum of deformed Wigner matrices with a full-rank perturbation S that introduces a finite number of outlier eigenvalues. Their method combines random matrix theory with asymptotic analysis to derive precise spectral limits. Experimental results confirm that the perturbation S induces distinct outlier eigenvalues that separate from the bulk spectrum, validating the theoretical predictions and supporting the use of Random Matrix Theory in pruning DNNs.
为理解训练深度神经网络中权重矩阵的谱性质（常表现为随机矩阵与高度相关扰动之和），本文研究了全秩扰动下变形Wigner矩阵的特征值渐近行为。作者结合随机矩阵理论与谱分析方法，推导出特征值分布的极限形式。结果表明，扰动引入有限个偏离主谱的离群特征值，其余特征值收敛于半圆律，为基于谱分析的神经网络剪枝方法提供了理论支撑。
ConforNets: Latents-Based Conformational Control in OpenFold3
Authors: Minji Lee, Colin Kalicki, Minkyu Jeon, Aymen Qabel, Alisia Fadini, Mohammed AlQuraishi
First: 2026-04-20T17:47:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:47:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Models from the AlphaFold (AF) family reliably predict one dominant conformation for most well-ordered proteins but struggle to capture biologically relevant alternate states. Several efforts have focused on eliciting greater conformational variability through ad hoc inference-time perturbations of AF models or their inputs. Despite their progress, these approaches remain inefficient and fail to consistently recover major conformational modes. Here, we investigate both the optimal location and manner-of-operation for perturbing latent representations in the AF3 architecture. We distill our findings in ConforNets: channel-wise affine transforms of the pre-Pairformer pair latents. Unlike previous methods, ConforNets globally modulate AF3 representations, making them reusable across proteins. On unsupervised generation of alternate states, ConforNets achieve state-of-the-art success rates on all existing multi-state benchmarks. On the novel supervised task of conformational transfer, ConforNets trained on one source protein can induce a conserved conformational change across a protein family. Collectively, these results introduce a mechanism for conformational control in AF3-based models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ConforNets：基于潜在表示的OpenFold3构象控制
AlphaFold（AF）系列模型能可靠预测大多数有序蛋白的主导构象，但在捕捉生物相关的替代构象方面存在困难。已有多种方法通过在推理时对AF模型或其输入进行临时扰动，以增强构象多样性。尽管取得进展，这些方法仍效率低下，且无法稳定恢复主要构象模式。本文研究了在AF3架构中扰动潜在表示的最优位置与操作方式，并将成果总结为ConforNets：对AF3中Pairformer前的配对潜在表示进行逐通道仿射变换。与以往方法不同，ConforNets全局调节AF3表示，使其可跨蛋白复用。在无监督生成替代构象任务中，ConforNets在所有现有多态基准测试中均达到最先进成功率。在新型监督任务构象迁移中，ConforNets在单一源蛋白上训练后，可诱导蛋白家族中保守的构象变化。综合来看，这些结果为AF3模型引入了构象控制机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of AlphaFold3 in capturing biologically relevant alternate conformations, this work proposes ConforNets: a method that applies channel-wise affine transformations to the pre-Pairformer pair latents in AF3 to globally modulate representations. Unlike prior ad hoc perturbation techniques, ConforNets enables reusable conformational control across proteins. Experimental results show state-of-the-art performance on unsupervised generation of alternate states across multiple benchmarks, and demonstrate successful conformational transfer across protein families when trained on a single source protein.
为了解决AlphaFold3难以捕捉生物相关构象变体的问题，该研究提出ConforNets方法，通过在AF3的预Pairformer对潜空间上应用通道级仿射变换，全局调节其表示。与以往的随机扰动方法不同，ConforNets支持跨蛋白复用。实验表明，其在多个无监督多构象生成基准上达到最先进水平，并能在监督任务中成功将一种蛋白的构象变化迁移到同一家族蛋白上。
SynAgent: Generalizable Cooperative Humanoid Manipulation via Solo-to-Cooperative Agent Synergy
Authors: Wei Yao, Haohan Ma, Hongwen Zhang, Yunlian Sun, Liangjun Xing, Zhile Yang, Yuanjun Guo, Yebin Liu, Jinhui Tang
First: 2026-04-20T17:46:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:46:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Controllable cooperative humanoid manipulation is a fundamental yet challenging problem for embodied intelligence, due to severe data scarcity, complexities in multi-agent coordination, and limited generalization across objects. In this paper, we present SynAgent, a unified framework that enables scalable and physically plausible cooperative manipulation by leveraging Solo-to-Cooperative Agent Synergy to transfer skills from single-agent human-object interaction to multi-agent human-object-human scenarios. To maintain semantic integrity during motion transfer, we introduce an interaction-preserving retargeting method based on an Interact Mesh constructed via Delaunay tetrahedralization, which faithfully maintains spatial relationships among humans and objects. Building upon this refined data, we propose a single-agent pretraining and adaptation paradigm that bootstraps synergistic collaborative behaviors from abundant single-human data through decentralized training and multi-agent PPO. Finally, we develop a trajectory-conditioned generative policy using a conditional VAE, trained via multi-teacher distillation from motion imitation priors to achieve stable and controllable object-level trajectory execution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SynAgent significantly outperforms existing baselines in both cooperative imitation and trajectory-conditioned control, while generalizing across diverse object geometries. Codes and data will be available after publication. Project Page: http://yw0208.github.io/synagent
中文标题/摘要
标题：SynAgent：通过单人到协作代理协同实现通用协作人形操作
可控协作人形操作是具身智能的基本且具有挑战性的问题，受限于严重的数据稀缺、多智能体协调的复杂性以及跨物体的泛化能力不足。本文提出SynAgent，一个统一框架，通过单人到协作代理协同，将单智能体人-物交互技能迁移到多人-物-人场景，实现可扩展且物理上合理的协作操作。为在运动迁移中保持语义完整性，我们引入基于Delaunay四面体化的交互保持重定向方法，构建Interact Mesh以忠实保留人与物体间的空间关系。在此基础上，我们提出单智能体预训练与适应范式，通过去中心化训练与多智能体PPO，从丰富的单人数据中自举协同行为。最后，我们开发基于轨迹条件的生成策略，使用条件VAE，通过多教师蒸馏从运动模仿先验中训练，实现稳定可控的物体级轨迹执行。大量实验表明，SynAgent在协作模仿与轨迹条件控制方面显著优于现有基线，且能泛化至多样化的物体几何结构。代码与数据将在论文发表后提供。项目页面：http://yw0208.github.io/synagent
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of cooperative manipulation data and the difficulty of generalizing across objects and agents, SynAgent introduces a unified framework that transfers single-agent human-object skills to multi-agent human-object-human scenarios via Solo-to-Cooperative Agent Synergy. It employs an interaction-preserving retargeting method using an Interact Mesh to maintain spatial relationships during motion transfer, then uses decentralized training and multi-agent PPO to bootstrap collaborative behaviors from abundant solo-agent data. A trajectory-conditioned generative policy, trained via multi-teacher distillation from motion imitation priors using a conditional VAE, enables stable and controllable object-level trajectory execution. Experiments show SynAgent significantly outperforms baselines in both imitation and trajectory control, with strong generalization across diverse object geometries.
为应对协作操作数据稀缺及多智能体协调难题，SynAgent提出一种统一框架，通过单智能体到协作智能体的技能迁移，将单人操作技能扩展至多人协作场景。该框架采用基于Delaunay四面体化的Interact Mesh结构，实现运动迁移中空间关系的保持。其方法先通过去中心化训练与多智能体PPO从大量单人数据中预训练并适应协作行为，再结合条件VAE与多教师蒸馏训练轨迹条件生成策略，实现稳定可控的物体级轨迹执行。实验表明，SynAgent在协作模仿与轨迹控制任务中显著优于现有基线，并具备跨多样物体几何形状的泛化能力。
GSQ: Highly-Accurate Low-Precision Scalar Quantization for LLMs via Gumbel-Softmax Sampling
Authors: Alireza Dadgarnia, Soroush Tabesh, Mahdi Nikdan, Michael Helcig, Eldar Kurtic, Dan Alistarh
First: 2026-04-20T17:45:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:45:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Weight quantization has become a standard tool for efficient LLM deployment, especially for local inference, where models are now routinely served at 2-3 bits per parameter. The state of the art is currently split into two sets of methods: simple scalar quantization techniques, such as GPTQ or AWQ, which are widely deployed but plateau in accuracy at 3-4 bits per parameter (bpp), and "second-generation" vector- or trellis-quantized methods, such as QTIP, GPTVQ and AQLM, which push the accuracy frontier at low bit-widths but are notoriously hard to implement and to scale, and have gained relatively less traction. In this paper, we ask whether this gap is fundamental, or whether a carefully optimized scalar quantizer can recover most of it. We answer in the affirmative, by introducing GSQ (Gumbel-Softmax Quantization), a post-training scalar quantization method which jointly learns the per-coordinate grid assignments and the per-group scales using a Gumbel-Softmax relaxation of the discrete grid. GSQ matches the cardinality of the relaxation to the small number of levels available in the target bit-width regime (e.g., 3-8 levels for ternary and 3 bpp, respectively), making the relaxation tight and the optimization tractable. Practically, on the standard Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct models, GSQ closes most of the gap between scalar quantization and the QTIP frontier at 2 and 3 bits, while using a symmetric scalar grid with group-wise quantization, and thus fully compatible with existing scalar inference kernels. We further show that GSQ scales to trillion-scale Mixture-of-Experts models such as Kimi-K2.5, where vector-quantized methods are difficult to apply.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GSQ：通过Gumbel-Softmax采样实现高精度低精度标量量化以支持LLM
权重量化已成为高效部署LLM的标准工具，特别是在本地推理场景中，模型现在常规以每参数2-3比特提供服务。当前最先进的方法分为两类：简单标量量化技术（如GPTQ或AWQ），虽广泛部署但精度在每参数3-4比特时趋于饱和；以及“第二代”向量或网格量化方法（如QTIP、GPTVQ和AQLM），在低比特宽度下推动精度前沿，但实施和扩展困难，应用相对较少。本文探讨这一差距是否本质，或是否可通过精心优化的标量量化器恢复大部分差距。我们肯定地回答：通过引入GSQ（Gumbel-Softmax量化），一种后训练标量量化方法，联合学习每坐标网格分配和每组缩放因子，利用Gumbel-Softmax松弛离散网格。GSQ将松弛的粒度与目标比特宽度范围内的少量层级匹配（如三元量化3-8级，3比特每参数），使松弛紧致、优化可行。实践中，GSQ在标准Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct模型上，在2和3比特时几乎弥合标量量化与QTIP前沿的差距，同时使用对称标量网格与组内量化，完全兼容现有标量推理内核。我们进一步表明，GSQ可扩展至千亿级专家混合模型（如Kimi-K2.5），而向量量化方法难以应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between widely deployed scalar quantization methods that plateau at 3-4 bits per parameter and high-accuracy vector-quantized methods that are hard to scale, this paper proposes GSQ, a post-training scalar quantization technique that jointly learns per-coordinate grid assignments and per-group scales using a Gumbel-Softmax relaxation. By aligning the relaxation’s cardinality with the limited levels of low-bit-width targets (e.g., 3-8 levels for ternary or 3 bpp), GSQ enables tight optimization and compatibility with existing scalar inference kernels. Experiments on Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct show GSQ closes most of the accuracy gap with vector-quantized methods at 2 and 3 bits, and it scales successfully to trillion-parameter MoE models like Kimi-K2.5 where vector quantization is impractical.
为弥合简单标量量化方法在低比特（3-4 bpp）下精度停滞与复杂向量量化方法高精度但难以部署之间的差距，本文提出GSQ（Gumbel-Softmax量化），一种基于后训练的标量量化方法，通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛联合学习坐标级网格分配与组级缩放因子。该方法将松弛的离散网格级别数与目标低比特范围（如三元码3级或3 bpp的8级）对齐，使优化更可行。实验表明，GSQ在Llama-3.1-8B/70B-Instruct模型上于2和3比特下接近向量量化前沿精度，使用对称标量网格与组量化，兼容现有标量推理内核，并可扩展至千亿参数MoE模型如Kimi-K2.5。
A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work
Authors: Ran Ben-Basat, Yaniv Ben-Itzhak, Gal Mendelson, Michael Mitzenmacher, Amit Portnoy, Shay Vargaftik
First: 2026-04-20T17:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:44:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN. First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$. Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN. Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations. Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于TurboQuant与早期DRIVE/EDEN工作的说明
本文澄清了近期TurboQuant工作与早期DRIVE（NeurIPS 2021）和EDEN（ICML 2022）方案之间的关系。DRIVE是一种1比特量化器，EDEN将其扩展至任意$b>0$比特每坐标；我们统称它们为EDEN。 首先，TurboQuant_{mse}是通过将EDEN的标量缩放参数固定为$S=1$得到的特例。EDEN支持有偏和无偏量化，分别由不同$S$优化（选择方法见EDEN相关工作）。TurboQuant采用的固定$S=1$通常次优，尽管有偏EDEN的最优$S$随维度增大趋近于1；因此，TurboQuant_{mse}在大维度下趋近于EDEN的行为。 其次，TurboQuant_{prod}将一个有偏$(b-1)$比特EDEN步骤与一个无偏1比特QJL残差量化相结合。其存在三方面次优性：(1) $(b-1)$比特步骤使用次优的$S=1$；(2) 1比特无偏残差量化比(无偏)1比特EDEN的MSE更差；(3) 将有偏$(b-1)$比特步骤与1比特无偏残差步骤串联，劣于直接用$b$比特无偏EDEN量化输入。 第三，TurboQuant工作中的部分分析与EDEN工作类似：两者均利用随机旋转与偏移Beta分布的关联，使用Lloyd-Max算法，并指出随机Hadamard变换可替代均匀随机旋转。 实验支持上述结论：优化$S$的有偏EDEN比TurboQuant_{mse}更准确，无偏EDEN比TurboQuant_{prod}显著更准确，常多出一个比特（例如2比特EDEN优于3比特TurboQuant_{prod}）。我们还复现了TurboQuant论文中的所有精度实验，表明在我们尝试的每种设置下，EDEN均优于TurboQuant。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by clarifying the relationship between TurboQuant and earlier EDEN/DRIVE methods, this note analyzes how TurboQuant variants relate to EDEN's flexible quantization framework. TurboQuant_mse is shown to be a restricted case of EDEN with fixed scale parameter S=1, which is suboptimal but asymptotically approaches EDEN’s performance for large dimensions. TurboQuant_prod combines a biased (b-1)-bit EDEN step with a 1-bit residual quantization, but is suboptimal due to using S=1, inferior residual quantization, and suboptimal chaining. Experiments confirm that optimized biased EDEN outperforms TurboQuant_mse, and unbiased EDEN significantly outperforms TurboQuant_prod—often by more than a bit—demonstrating EDEN’s superiority across all tested settings.
本文分析了TurboQuant与早期EDEN/DRIVE量化方法的关系，指出TurboQuant_mse是固定标度参数S=1的EDEN特例，该参数通常次优但随维度增大趋近EDEN表现。TurboQuant_prod将偏置(b-1)位EDEN步骤与1位无偏残差量化结合，因使用S=1、残差量化较差及步骤组合不佳而次优。实验表明，优化后的偏置EDEN优于TurboQuant_mse，无偏EDEN显著优于TurboQuant_prod，常多出一位精度，且在所有测试设置中EDEN均表现更优。
How to sketch a learning algorithm
Authors: Sam Gunn
First: 2026-04-08T17:46:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:42:11+00:00
Comments: Improved presentation and simplified Algorithm 4
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
How does the choice of training data influence an AI model? This broad question is of central importance to interpretability, privacy, and basic science. At its technical core is the data deletion problem: after a reasonable amount of precomputation, quickly predict how the model would behave in a given situation if a given subset of training data had been excluded from the learning algorithm. We present a data deletion scheme capable of predicting model outputs with vanishing error $\varepsilon$ and failure probability $δ$ in the deep learning setting. Our precomputation and prediction algorithms are only $\tilde{O}(\log(1/δ)/\varepsilon^2)$ factors slower than regular training and inference, respectively. The storage requirements are those of $\tilde{O}(\log(1/δ)/\varepsilon^2)$ models. Our proof is based on an assumption that we call stability. In contrast to the assumptions made by prior work, stability appears to be fully compatible with learning powerful AI models. In support of this, we show that stability is satisfied in a minimal set of experiments with microgpt. Our code is available at https://github.com/SamSpo1/microgpt-sketch. At a technical level, our work is based on a new method for locally sketching an arithmetic circuit by computing higher-order derivatives in random complex directions. Forward-mode automatic differentiation allows cheap computation of these derivatives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何草拟学习算法
训练数据的选择如何影响人工智能模型？这一广泛问题对可解释性、隐私和基础科学至关重要。其技术核心是数据删除问题：在合理预计算后，快速预测若从学习算法中排除某部分训练数据，模型在特定情境下的行为表现。 我们提出了一种数据删除方案，可在深度学习环境下以趋近于零的误差ε和失败概率δ预测模型输出。我们的预计算与预测算法分别仅比常规训练和推理慢约O(log(1/δ)/ε²)因子。存储需求相当于O(log(1/δ)/ε²)个模型。 我们的证明基于一个称为“稳定性”的假设。与以往工作所依赖的假设不同，稳定性似乎与学习强大AI模型完全兼容。为此，我们展示了在microgpt的最小实验集上稳定性成立。代码见https://github.com/SamSpo1/microgpt-sketch。 在技术层面，我们的工作基于一种新方法：通过在随机复数方向计算高阶导数，局部草拟算术电路。前向自动微分可廉价计算这些导数。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the question of how training data selection affects AI model behavior, motivated by needs in interpretability, privacy, and foundational understanding. It introduces a data deletion method that predicts model outputs after excluding specific training data, with vanishing error and failure probability, using precomputation and prediction algorithms that are nearly as fast as standard training and inference. The method relies on a novel technique of locally sketching arithmetic circuits via higher-order derivatives computed in random complex directions, enabled by forward-mode automatic differentiation. Experiments with microgpt confirm that the method’s core assumption—stability—is compatible with learning powerful models, and the approach requires storing only a logarithmic number of models relative to error and failure probability bounds.
为理解训练数据如何影响AI模型行为，本文提出一种数据删除方案，可预测移除特定数据后模型的输出。该方法通过在随机复方向上计算高阶导数，结合前向自动微分技术，局部草图化算术电路。实验表明，在microgpt上该方法可实现接近零的预测误差ε，失败概率δ，计算与存储开销仅为标准训练和推理的对数级倍数。
Advancing Vision Transformer with Enhanced Spatial Priors
Authors: Qihang Fan, Huaibo Huang, Mingrui Chen, Hongmin Liu, Ran He
First: 2026-04-20T17:41:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:41:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TPAMI2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, the Vision Transformer (ViT) has garnered significant attention within the computer vision community. However, the core component of ViT, Self-Attention, lacks explicit spatial priors and suffers from quadratic computational complexity, limiting its applicability. To address these issues, we have proposed RMT, a robust vision backbone with explicit spatial priors for general purposes. RMT utilizes Manhattan distance decay to introduce spatial information and employs a horizontal and vertical decomposition attention method to model global information. Building on the strengths of RMT, Euclidean enhanced Vision Transformer (EVT) is an expanded version that incorporates several key improvements. Firstly, EVT uses a more reasonable Euclidean distance decay to enhance the modeling of spatial information, allowing for a more accurate representation of spatial relationships compared to the Manhattan distance used in RMT. Secondly, EVT abandons the decomposed attention mechanism featured in RMT and instead adopts a simpler spatially-independent grouping approach, providing the model with greater flexibility in controlling the number of tokens within each group. By addressing these modifications, EVT offers a more sophisticated and adaptable approach to incorporating spatial priors into the Self-Attention mechanism, thus overcoming some of the limitations associated with RMT and further enhancing its applicability in various computer vision tasks. Extensive experiments on Image Classification, Object Detection, Instance Segmentation, and Semantic Segmentation demonstrate that EVT exhibits exceptional performance. Without additional training data, EVT achieves 86.6% top1-acc on ImageNet-1k.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过增强空间先验推进视觉Transformer
近年来，视觉Transformer（ViT）在计算机视觉领域备受关注。然而，ViT的核心组件自注意力机制缺乏显式空间先验，且计算复杂度为二次，限制了其适用性。为解决这些问题，我们提出了RMT，一种具有显式空间先验的通用鲁棒视觉骨干网络。RMT利用曼哈顿距离衰减引入空间信息，并采用水平与垂直分解注意力机制建模全局信息。基于RMT的优势，欧几里得增强视觉Transformer（EVT）是其扩展版本，包含多项关键改进。首先，EVT采用更合理的欧几里得距离衰减以增强空间信息建模，相比RMT使用的曼哈顿距离，能更准确地表示空间关系。其次，EVT摒弃了RMT中的分解注意力机制，转而采用更简单的空间无关分组方法，使模型在控制每组token数量时更具灵活性。通过这些改进，EVT提供了更精巧且可适应的方案，将空间先验融入自注意力机制，克服了RMT的部分局限性，进一步提升了其在各类计算机视觉任务中的适用性。在图像分类、目标检测、实例分割和语义分割等任务上的大量实验表明，EVT表现卓越。无需额外训练数据，EVT在ImageNet-1k上达到86.6%的top1准确率。

Motivated by the lack of explicit spatial priors and high computational cost in Vision Transformers, this work introduces EVT, an enhanced transformer architecture that improves spatial modeling through Euclidean distance decay and a flexible token grouping strategy. Unlike its predecessor RMT, which relied on Manhattan distance and decomposed attention, EVT adopts a more accurate spatial representation and a simpler, scalable grouping mechanism. Experimental results across four computer vision tasks show EVT’s effectiveness, achieving 86.6% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1k without additional training data.
为解决视觉Transformer中自注意力机制缺乏空间先验和计算复杂度高的问题，本文提出EVT模型，通过欧氏距离衰减增强空间建模能力，并采用更灵活的分组策略替代原RMT的分解注意力机制。实验表明，EVT在图像分类、目标检测、实例分割和语义分割四大任务上表现优异，尤其在ImageNet-1k上无需额外训练数据即达到86.6%的top-1准确率，验证了其更强的泛化能力和效率优势。
Inference-Time Distillation: Cost-Efficient Agents Without Fine-Tuning or Manual Prompt Engineering
Authors: Vishnu Sarukkai, Asanshay Gupta, James Hong, Michaël Gharbi, Kayvon Fatahalian
First: 2025-12-02T09:11:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:40:46+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying LLM agents at scale typically requires choosing between quality and cost. Existing cost-reduction approaches fail to preserve agility: the ability to iterate rapidly without human time bottlenecks. Prompt engineering is brittle and slows iteration, while fine-tuning requires multi-day training and commitment to fixed designs; both are impractical for iterative workflows and time-sensitive batch jobs. We demonstrate that established inference-time techniques--dynamic in-context learning and self-consistency cascades--can be leveraged to shift the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier while preserving agility. Practitioners run the teacher on a small task subset to collect demonstrations, then immediately deploy a cheaper student on the remainder. At each step, the system retrieves relevant teacher demonstrations as in-context examples. When multiple student samples agree, we proceed; when they diverge, we fall back to the teacher. This requires no prompt engineering or training. On ALFWorld, we match teacher accuracy at 2.5x lower cost (0.059 to 0.024 per episode). On AppWorld, we achieve 3.5x cost reduction while recovering 79% of teacher accuracy. Our empirical analyses provide guidance on key design choices: teacher database size, demonstration set size, retrieval strategy, and cascade thresholds. These analyses highlight inference-time levers for navigating cost-performance tradeoffs without sacrificing human development speed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理时蒸馏：无需微调或手动提示工程的低成本代理
大规模部署大语言模型代理通常需在质量与成本间权衡。现有降低成本方法无法保持敏捷性——即无需人工时间瓶颈即可快速迭代的能力。提示工程易碎且减缓迭代，微调则需多日训练并锁定固定设计，二者均不适用于迭代工作流和时效性批处理任务。我们证明，成熟的推理时技术——动态上下文学习与自一致性级联——可将成本-精度帕累托前沿向更优方向移动，同时保持敏捷性。实践者先在小任务子集上运行教师模型收集演示，随即部署更便宜的学生模型处理剩余任务。每步系统检索相关教师演示作为上下文示例；当多个学生样本达成一致时继续，若出现分歧则回退至教师模型。此方法无需提示工程或训练。在ALFWorld上，我们以2.5倍更低成本（每回合0.059降至0.024）匹配教师精度；在AppWorld上，实现3.5倍成本降低并恢复79%教师精度。实证分析指导关键设计选择：教师数据库规模、演示集大小、检索策略与级联阈值，凸显推理时杠杆在不牺牲人类开发速度前提下应对成本-性能权衡的作用。
Summary / 总结
To address the trade-off between cost and quality in large language model agent deployment, this work proposes inference-time distillation that avoids fine-tuning or prompt engineering, preserving rapid iteration. The method trains a lightweight student model using demonstrations from a teacher model applied to a small subset of tasks, then deploys the student on the remainder while retrieving relevant teacher examples at inference time. When student outputs agree, the system uses them; when they diverge, it falls back to the teacher. Experiments on ALFWorld show 2.5x cost reduction with matched accuracy, and on AppWorld, 3.5x cost reduction with 79% of teacher accuracy. Empirical analysis guides key design parameters like demonstration size and retrieval strategy, demonstrating how inference-time techniques can improve cost-performance without slowing development.
为解决大语言模型代理部署中成本与质量的权衡问题，本文提出一种推理时蒸馏方法，无需微调或人工提示工程，从而保持快速迭代能力。该方法先在小规模任务子集上运行教师模型收集演示，再部署轻量级学生模型处理剩余任务，推理时检索相关教师演示。当学生输出不一致时，系统回退至教师模型。在ALFWorld和AppWorld上分别实现2.5倍和3.5倍的成本降低，准确率分别保持在100%和79%。实证分析指导了演示集规模、检索策略等关键设计，表明推理时技术可调整成本-性能权衡而不牺牲开发速度。
Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Biological $2\mathrm{D}{+}t$ Reaction-Diffusion Systems
Authors: William Lavery, Jodie A. Cochrane, Christian Olesen, Dagim S. Tadele, John T. Nardini, Sara Hamis
First: 2026-04-20T17:40:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:40:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a powerful framework for learning governing equations of dynamical systems from data. Biologically-informed neural networks (BINNs) are a variant of PINNs that preserve the known differential operator structure (e.g., reaction-diffusion) while learning constitutive terms via trainable neural subnetworks, enforced through soft residual penalties. Existing BINN studies are limited to $1\mathrm{D}{+}t$ reaction-diffusion systems and focus on forward prediction, using the governing partial differential equation as a regulariser rather than an explicit identification target. Here, we extend BINNs to $2\mathrm{D}{+}t$ systems within a PINN framework that combines data preprocessing, BINN-based equation learning, and symbolic regression post-processing for closed-form equation discovery. We demonstrate the framework's real-world applicability by learning the governing equations of lung cancer cell population dynamics from time-lapse microscopy data, recovering $2\mathrm{D}{+}t$ reaction-diffusion models from experimental observations. The proposed framework is readily applicable to other spatio-temporal systems, providing a practical and interpretable tool for fast analytic equation discovery from data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于生物二维时反应扩散系统的物理信息神经网络
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）为从数据中学习动态系统的控制方程提供了一个强大框架。生物信息神经网络（BINNs）是PINNs的一种变体，它在保持已知微分算子结构（如反应扩散）的同时，通过可训练的神经子网络学习本构项，并通过软残差惩罚加以约束。现有BINN研究仅限于一维时反应扩散系统，且侧重于前向预测，将控制偏微分方程作为正则化项而非显式识别目标。本文在PINN框架内扩展BINNs至二维时系统，结合数据预处理、基于BINN的方程学习及符号回归后处理，实现闭式方程发现。我们通过从延时显微镜数据中学习肺癌细胞种群动力学的控制方程，从实验观测中恢复二维时反应扩散模型，展示了该框架的实际应用价值。该框架易于推广至其他时空系统，为从数据中快速发现可解释的解析方程提供实用工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to identify governing equations for complex biological systems from experimental data, this work extends biologically-informed neural networks (BINNs) to two-dimensional spatio-temporal reaction-diffusion systems, enabling equation discovery beyond the 1D+time scope of prior studies. The method integrates data preprocessing, BINN-based learning of constitutive terms with soft residual penalties enforcing known differential operators, and symbolic regression for deriving closed-form equations. Experiments on lung cancer cell dynamics from time-lapse microscopy successfully recovered accurate 2D+time reaction-diffusion models, demonstrating the framework’s practicality and interpretability for real-world biological data.
为从稀疏实验数据中发现复杂生物系统的控制方程，本研究将生物启发式神经网络（BINNs）扩展至二维时空反应-扩散系统，突破此前仅限于一维时间系统的局限。方法结合数据预处理、基于BINN的可训练子网络学习构型项（通过软残差惩罚强制满足已知微分算子结构），以及符号回归以推导闭式方程。在肺癌细胞动力学的显微成像数据实验中，成功恢复出准确的二维时空反应-扩散模型，证明该框架可有效应用于真实生物系统，并具备快速、可解释的方程发现能力，适用于其他时空系统。
FUSE: Ensembling Verifiers with Zero Labeled Data
Authors: Joonhyuk Lee, Virginia Ma, Sarah Zhao, Yash Nair, Asher Spector, Regev Cohen, Emmanuel J. Candès
First: 2026-04-20T17:40:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:40:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Verification of model outputs is rapidly emerging as a key primitive for both training and real-world deployment of large language models (LLMs). In practice, this often involves using imperfect LLM judges and reward models since ground truth acquisition can be time-consuming and expensive. We introduce Fully Unsupervised Score Ensembling (FUSE), a method for improving verification quality by ensembling verifiers without access to ground truth correctness labels. The key idea behind FUSE is to control conditional dependencies between verifiers in a manner that improves the unsupervised performance of a class of spectral algorithms from the ensembling literature. Despite requiring zero ground truth labels, FUSE typically matches or improves upon semi-supervised alternatives in test-time scaling experiments with diverse sets of generator models, verifiers, and benchmarks. In particular, we validate our method on both conventional academic benchmarks such as GPQA Diamond and on frontier, unsaturated benchmarks such as Humanity's Last Exam and IMO Shortlist questions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FUSE：无需标注数据的验证器集成
模型输出的验证正迅速成为大型语言模型（LLMs）训练和实际部署的关键基础。实践中，由于获取真实标签耗时且昂贵，常使用不完美的LLM评判器和奖励模型。我们提出了完全无监督评分集成（FUSE）方法，通过集成验证器提升验证质量，且无需真实正确性标签。FUSE的核心思想是控制验证器间的条件依赖关系，从而提升文献中一类谱算法的无监督性能。尽管无需真实标签，FUSE在测试时缩放实验中，通常能与半监督方法相当或更优，适用于多样化的生成器模型、验证器和基准测试。我们特别在传统学术基准（如GPQA Diamond）和前沿、未饱和基准（如Humanity's Last Exam及IMO短名单题目）上验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of verifying large language model outputs without ground truth labels, FUSE introduces an unsupervised ensembling method that improves verification quality by controlling conditional dependencies among verifiers, leveraging spectral algorithms. The method aggregates verifier scores without labeled correctness data, and experimental results show it matches or outperforms semi-supervised baselines across diverse generator models, verifier types, and benchmarks including GPQA Diamond and frontier challenges like Humanity's Last Exam and IMO Shortlist.
为了解决在无标签数据情况下验证大语言模型输出的难题，FUSE提出一种无监督集成方法，通过控制验证器间的条件依赖关系，结合谱算法提升验证质量。该方法无需正确性标签即可聚合验证器评分，实验表明其在多种生成器模型、验证器类型和基准（如GPQA Diamond、Humanity's Last Exam及IMO Shortlist）上表现与半监督方法相当或更优。
Empowering Multi-Turn Tool-Integrated Agentic Reasoning with Group Turn Policy Optimization
Authors: Yifeng Ding, Hung Le, Songyang Han, Kangrui Ruan, Zhenghui Jin, Varun Kumar, Zijian Wang, Anoop Deoras
First: 2025-11-18T19:01:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:40:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training Large Language Models (LLMs) for multi-turn Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) - where models iteratively reason, generate code, and verify through execution - remains challenging for existing reinforcement learning (RL) approaches. Current RL methods, exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), suffer from coarse-grained, trajectory-level rewards that provide insufficient learning signals for complex multi-turn interactions, leading to training stagnation. To address this issue, we propose Group Turn Policy Optimization (GTPO), a novel RL algorithm specifically designed for training LLMs on multi-turn TIR tasks. GTPO introduces three key innovations: (1) turn-level reward assignment that provides fine-grained feedback for individual turns, (2) return-based advantage estimation where normalized discounted returns are calculated as advantages, and (3) self-supervised reward shaping that exploits self-supervision signals from generated code to densify sparse binary outcome-based rewards. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that GTPO outperforms GRPO by 3.0% across diverse math reasoning benchmarks, establishing its effectiveness. GTPO also improves GRPO by 3.9% on commonsense reasoning and program synthesis tasks, demonstrating its generalizability to non-math domains. Importantly, GTPO incurs negligible overhead, ensuring its practicality for real-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过组轮策略优化赋能多轮工具集成代理推理
训练大型语言模型（LLM）进行多轮工具集成推理（TIR）——即模型迭代推理、生成代码并通过执行验证——对现有强化学习（RL）方法仍具挑战性。当前RL方法（如组相对策略优化GRPO）存在粗粒度、轨迹级奖励的问题，难以为复杂的多轮交互提供充分的学习信号，导致训练停滞。为解决此问题，我们提出组轮策略优化（GTPO），一种专为多轮TIR任务训练LLM设计的新型RL算法。GTPO包含三项关键创新：（1）轮级奖励分配，为每轮提供细粒度反馈；（2）基于回报的优势估计，通过归一化折扣回报计算优势；（3）自监督奖励塑造，利用生成代码的自监督信号将稀疏的二元结果奖励稠密化。全面评估表明，GTPO在多种数学推理基准上比GRPO提升3.0%，验证其有效性；在常识推理与程序合成任务上，GTPO比GRPO提升3.9%，证明其在非数学领域的泛化能力。重要的是，GTPO几乎无额外开销，确保其在实际场景中的实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing RL methods like GRPO in training LLMs for multi-turn Tool-Integrated Reasoning, this work introduces Group Turn Policy Optimization (GTPO), a novel RL algorithm that enhances learning signals through turn-level reward assignment, return-based advantage estimation, and self-supervised reward shaping. Experimental results show GTPO outperforms GRPO by 3.0% on math reasoning benchmarks and by 3.9% on commonsense reasoning and program synthesis tasks, while maintaining negligible computational overhead, demonstrating its effectiveness and practicality across diverse domains.
为解决现有强化学习方法（如GRPO）在训练大语言模型进行多轮工具集成推理时因奖励粒度粗、信号不足导致训练停滞的问题，本文提出Group Turn Policy Optimization（GTPO）算法，通过引入回合级奖励分配、基于回报的优势估计和自监督奖励塑造三大创新。实验表明，GTPO在数学推理基准上比GRPO提升3.0%，在常识推理与程序合成任务上提升3.9%，且计算开销可忽略，验证其有效性与泛化能力。
Efficient Kernel Learning from Side Information Using ADMM
Authors: En-Liang Hu
First: 2018-11-03T12:15:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:39:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Side information is highly useful in the learning of a nonparametric kernel matrix. However, this often leads to an expensive semidefinite program (SDP). In recent years, a number of dedicated solvers have been proposed. Though much better than off-the-shelf SDP solvers, they still cannot scale to large data sets. In this paper, we propose a novel solver based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). The key idea is to use a low-rank decomposition of the kernel matrix $\K = \V^\top \U$, with the constraint that $\V=\U$. The resultant optimization problem, though non-convex, has favorable convergence properties and can be efficiently solved without requiring eigen-decomposition in each iteration. Experimental results on a number of real-world data sets demonstrate that the proposed method is as accurate as directly solving the SDP, but can be one to two orders of magnitude faster.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用ADMM从旁观信息高效学习核矩阵
旁观信息在非参数核矩阵的学习中非常有用，但通常会导致昂贵的半定规划（SDP）。近年来，已提出多种专用求解器，尽管比现成的SDP求解器更优，但仍无法扩展到大规模数据集。本文提出了一种基于交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的新求解器。核心思想是利用核矩阵 $\K = \V^\top \U$ 的低秩分解，且满足 $\V=\U$ 的约束。所得优化问题虽非凸，但具有良好的收敛性质，且无需在每次迭代中进行特征分解即可高效求解。在多个真实数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法精度与直接求解SDP相当，但速度可快一个至两个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of semidefinite programming for kernel learning with side information, this paper introduces an ADMM-based solver that decomposes the kernel matrix into low-rank components under a symmetric constraint. The method avoids expensive eigen-decompositions per iteration and leverages the favorable convergence properties of the non-convex formulation. Experiments on real-world datasets show that the proposed approach achieves accuracy comparable to direct SDP solvers while being one to two orders of magnitude faster.
为了解决利用侧信息学习核矩阵时求解半定规划计算成本过高的问题，本文提出一种基于ADMM的求解器，通过将核矩阵分解为低秩形式并约束其左右因子相等。该方法避免了每轮迭代的特征值分解，虽为非凸问题但具有良好的收敛性。实验表明，该方法在保持与直接求解SDP相当精度的同时，速度提升一个至两个数量级。
Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Risk-Sensitive Estimation via Conditional Value-at-Risk
Authors: Feras Al Taha, Eilyan Bitar
First: 2026-04-20T17:39:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:39:18+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a distributionally robust approach to risk-sensitive estimation of an unknown signal x from an observed signal y. The unknown signal and observation are modeled as random vectors whose joint probability distribution is unknown, but assumed to belong to a given type-2 Wasserstein ball of distributions, termed the ambiguity set. The performance of an estimator is measured according to the conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) of the squared estimation error. Within this framework, we study the problem of computing affine estimators that minimize the worst-case CVaR over all distributions in the given ambiguity set. As our main result, we show that, when the nominal distribution at the center of the Wasserstein ball is finitely supported, such estimators can be exactly computed by solving a tractable semidefinite program. We evaluate the proposed estimators on a wholesale electricity price forecasting task using real market data and show that they deliver lower out-of-sample CVaR of squared error compared to existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过条件风险价值进行Wasserstein分布鲁棒风险敏感估计
我们提出了一种分布鲁棒的风险敏感估计方法，用于从观测信号y估计未知信号x。未知信号和观测值建模为随机向量，其联合概率分布未知，但假设属于给定的类型2 Wasserstein分布球（称为模糊集）。估计器的性能根据平方估计误差的条件风险价值（CVaR）进行衡量。在此框架下，我们研究了计算仿射估计器的问题，以最小化模糊集内所有分布的最坏情况CVaR。作为主要结果，我们证明，当Wasserstein球中心的名义分布具有有限支撑时，此类估计器可通过求解一个可处理的半定规划精确计算。我们在使用真实市场数据的批发电力价格预测任务中评估了所提出的估计器，结果表明其平方误差的样本外CVaR低于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust risk-sensitive estimation under distributional uncertainty, this paper introduces a distributionally robust framework using the conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) to measure estimator performance. It formulates the problem as minimizing the worst-case CVaR of squared estimation error over a Wasserstein ball of distributions centered on a nominal model. The key method involves deriving affine estimators that can be exactly computed via a tractable semidefinite program when the nominal distribution is finitely supported. Experimental results on real-world wholesale electricity price forecasting demonstrate that these estimators achieve lower out-of-sample CVaR compared to existing approaches.
为应对分布不确定性下的风险敏感估计需求，本文提出一种分布鲁棒框架，通过在Wasserstein球形分布集合上最小化平方估计误差的最坏情况条件风险价值（CVaR）来构建估计器。当中心名义分布为有限支撑时，该方法通过求解一个可处理的半定规划精确计算仿射估计器。在真实电力批发价格预测数据上的实验表明，所提方法在样本外CVaR指标上优于现有方法，验证了其在分布不确定性下的鲁棒性和有效性。
ClawEnvKit: Automatic Environment Generation for Claw-Like Agents
Authors: Xirui Li, Ming Li, Derry Xu, Wei-Lin Chiang, Ion Stoica, Cho-Jui Hsieh, Tianyi Zhou
First: 2026-04-20T17:36:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:36:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constructing environments for training and evaluating claw-like agents remains a manual, human-intensive process that does not scale. We argue that what is needed is not just a dataset, but an automated pipeline capable of generating diverse, verified environments on demand. To this end, we introduce ClawEnvKit, an autonomous generation pipeline that instantiates this formalism from natural language descriptions. The pipeline comprises three modules: (1) a parser that extracts structured generation parameters from natural language input; (2) a generator that produces the task specification, tool interface, and scoring configuration; and (3) a validator that enforces feasibility, diversity, structural validity, and internal consistency across the generated environments. Using ClawEnvKit, we construct Auto-ClawEval, the first large-scale benchmark for claw-like agents, comprising 1,040 environments across 24 categories. Empirically, Auto-ClawEval matches or exceeds human-curated environments on coherence and clarity at 13,800x lower cost. Evaluated across 4 model families and 8 agent harness frameworks, we find that harness engineering boosts performance by up to 15.7 percentage points over a bare ReAct baseline, completion remains the primary axis of variation with no model saturating the benchmark, and automated generation enables evaluation at a scale previously infeasible. Beyond static benchmarking, ClawEnvKit enables live evaluation: users describe a desired capability in natural language and obtain a verified environment on demand, turning evaluation into a continuous, user-driven process. The same mechanism serves as an on-demand training environment generator, producing task distributions that adapt to an agent's current weaknesses rather than being bounded by existing user logs.
中文标题/摘要

Motivated by the inefficiency and scalability limitations of manually constructing environments for claw-like agents, the authors introduce ClawEnvKit, an automated pipeline that generates diverse, verified environments from natural language descriptions. The system comprises three modules: a parser to extract structured parameters, a generator to produce task specs, tool interfaces, and scoring configurations, and a validator to ensure feasibility, diversity, and consistency. Evaluated via Auto-ClawEval—a 1,040-environment benchmark across 24 categories—ClawEnvKit achieves performance matching or exceeding human-curated environments at 13,800x lower cost. Across four model families and eight agent frameworks, harness engineering improved performance by up to 15.7 percentage points over a ReAct baseline, completion remained the dominant performance axis, and automated generation enabled scalable, on-demand evaluation and adaptive training environment creation.
为解决爪状智能体环境构建人工密集、难以扩展的问题，作者提出ClawEnvKit，一个从自然语言描述自动生成多样且验证过的环境的自动化流程。该系统包含三个模块：解析器提取结构化参数，生成器构建任务规范与评分配置，验证器确保环境可行性、多样性与一致性。通过Auto-ClawEval（24类共1040个环境）的实验证明，该流程在成本仅为人工环境1/13800的情况下，表现匹配甚至优于人工构建环境。在四类模型与八种框架下，框架工程提升性能最高达15.7个百分点（优于ReAct基线），完成度是主要性能差异来源，无模型饱和整个基准。ClawEnvKit还支持按需用户驱动评估与自适应训练环境生成。
Duality for the Adversarial Total Variation
Authors: Leon Bungert, Lucas Schmitt
First: 2026-04-20T17:34:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:34:31+00:00
Comments: 39 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adversarial training of binary classifiers can be reformulated as regularized risk minimization involving a nonlocal total variation. Building on this perspective, we establish a characterization of the subdifferential of this total variation using duality techniques. To achieve this, we derive a dual representation of the nonlocal total variation and a related integration of parts formula, involving a nonlocal gradient and divergence. We provide such duality statements both in the space of continuous functions vanishing at infinity on proper metric spaces and for the space of essentially bounded functions on Euclidean domains. Furthermore, under some additional conditions we provide characterizations of the subdifferential in these settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗总变差的对偶性
二元分类器的对抗训练可重新表述为涉及非局部总变差的正则化风险最小化。基于此视角，我们利用对偶技术建立了该总变差次梯度的刻画。为此，我们推导了非局部总变差的对偶表示及相关积分部分公式，涉及非局部梯度与散度。我们同时在恰当度量空间上连续且在无穷远处趋于零的函数空间，以及欧几里得域上本质有界函数空间中提供了此类对偶陈述。此外，在某些附加条件下，我们还在这两种情形下给出了次梯度的刻画。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by reformulating adversarial training of binary classifiers as regularized risk minimization involving nonlocal total variation, this work establishes duality-based characterizations of its subdifferential. The method derives a dual representation of the nonlocal total variation and an integration by parts formula using nonlocal gradient and divergence operators. Experimental results are not presented as this is a theoretical paper; however, the duality results are rigorously proven in both continuous function spaces vanishing at infinity on proper metric spaces and in essentially bounded function spaces on Euclidean domains, with subdifferential characterizations provided under additional conditions.
为将二分类器的对抗训练重述为涉及非局部总变差的正则化风险最小化问题，本文通过对偶方法建立了该总变差次梯度的刻画。方法推导出非局部总变差的对偶表示及涉及非局部梯度与散度的积分分部公式，适用于度量空间上消失于无穷的连续函数空间和欧氏域上本质有界函数空间。虽无实验结果，但在附加条件下提供了这些空间中次梯度的理论刻画，揭示了其数学结构。
Transition-Matrix Regularization for Next Dialogue Act Prediction in Counselling Conversations
Authors: Eric Rudolph, Philipp Steigerwald, Jens Albrecht
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-04-20T17:33:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:33:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted as ACL findings paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper studies how empirical dialogue-flow statistics can be incorporated into Next Dialogue Act Prediction (NDAP). A KL regularization term is proposed that aligns predicted act distributions with corpus-derived transition patterns. Evaluated on a 60-class German counselling taxonomy using 5-fold cross-validation, this improves macro-F1 by 9--42% relative depending on encoder and substantially improves dialogue-flow alignment. Cross-dataset validation on HOPE suggests that improvements transfer across languages and counselling domains. In systematic ablations across pretrained encoders and architectures, the findings indicate that transition regularization provides consistent gains and disproportionately benefits weaker baseline models. The results suggest that lightweight discourse-flow priors complement pretrained encoders, especially in fine-grained, data-sparse dialogue tasks.

标题：用于咨询对话中下一轮对话行为预测的转移矩阵正则化

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve dialogue act prediction in counselling conversations using empirical flow patterns, this study introduces a KL divergence-based regularization term that aligns predicted dialogue acts with corpus-derived transition matrices. Evaluated on a 60-class German counselling dataset via 5-fold cross-validation, the method consistently boosts macro-F1 by 9–42% relative to baselines, depending on encoder type, and significantly enhances dialogue-flow alignment. 为提升咨询对话中下一对话行为预测的性能，该研究提出一种基于KL散度的正则化方法，使预测的行为分布与语料库中的对话转移模式对齐。在60类德语咨询语料上进行5折交叉验证，该方法相较基线模型在宏F1分数上提升9–42%，并显著增强对话流程一致性。跨语料验证表明其效果可迁移至其他语言和领域，消融实验显示该正则化对弱基线模型收益更大，表明轻量级话语先验能有效补充预训练编码器，尤其适用于数据稀疏、细粒度的对话任务。
Fast and Forgettable: A Controlled Study of Novices' Performance, Learning, Workload, and Emotion in AI-Assisted and Human Pair Programming Paradigms
Authors: Nicholas Gardella, James Prather, Juho Leinonen, Paul Denny, Raymond Pettit, Sara L. Riggs
First: 2026-04-20T17:33:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:33:13+00:00
Comments: for online appendices, see https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19665253
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Code-generating Artificial Intelligence has gained popularity within both professional and educational programming settings over the past several years. While research and pedagogy are beginning to cope with this change, computing students are left to bear the unforeseen consequences of AI amidst a dearth of empirical evidence about its effects. Though pair programming between students is well studied and known to be beneficial to self-efficacy and academic achievement, it remains underutilized and further threatened by the proposition that AI can replace a human programming partner. In this paper, we present a controlled pair programming study with 22 participants who wrote Python code under time pressure in teams of two and individually with GitHub Copilot for 20 minutes each. They were incentivized by bonus compensation to balance performance with understanding and were retested individually on the programming tasks after a retention interval of one week. Subjective measures of workload and emotion as well as objective measures of performance and learning (retest performance) were collected. Results showed that participants performed significantly better with GitHub Copilot than their human teammate, and several dimensions of their workload were significantly reduced. However, the emotional effect of the human teammate was significantly more positive and arousing as compared to working with Copilot. Furthermore, there was a nonsignificant absolute retest performance reduction in the AI condition and a larger retest performance decrement in the AI condition. We recommend that educators strongly consider revisiting pair programming as an educational tool in addition to embracing modern AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：快速而易忘：一项关于初学者在AI辅助与人类配对编程范式中表现、学习、工作负荷与情绪的对照研究
过去几年，代码生成型人工智能在专业和教育编程环境中日益流行。尽管研究与教学正开始应对这一变化，但计算专业学生却不得不独自承担AI带来的未知后果，而缺乏关于其影响的实证证据。虽然学生间的配对编程已被广泛研究，且已知能提升自我效能感和学业成绩，但其应用仍不充分，且面临AI可能取代人类编程搭档的威胁。本文呈现了一项对照配对编程研究，22名参与者在20分钟内以两人小组或单独使用GitHub Copilot编写Python代码，且通过奖金激励平衡表现与理解。一周后，他们被单独重测编程任务。收集了主观的工作负荷与情绪数据，以及客观的表现与学习（重测表现）数据。结果显示，参与者使用GitHub Copilot时表现显著优于人类搭档，且多项工作负荷维度显著降低。然而，与人类搭档合作的情绪效应显著更积极且更具唤醒性。此外，AI条件下重测表现绝对值无显著下降，但下降幅度更大。我们建议教育工作者应重新考虑将配对编程作为教育工具，同时拥抱现代AI。
