Motivated by the growing use of AI in programming education and the lack of empirical evidence on its impact, this study compared AI-assisted (GitHub Copilot) and human pair programming for novice learners. Twenty-two participants completed 20-minute coding tasks individually and in pairs, with performance, learning, workload, and emotion measured. Results showed AI outperformed human partners in immediate task performance and reduced workload, but human partners elicited more positive and arousing emotional responses. Notably, AI users showed a larger decline in retest performance after one week, suggesting diminished learning retention compared to human collaboration.
鉴于AI编程工具如GitHub Copilot在编程教育中的普及与实证研究的匮乏，本研究在控制条件下对比了AI辅助与人类配对编程的效果。22名参与者在限时压力下分别以两人协作和AI辅助方式编写Python代码，一周后进行复测。结果显示，AI辅助显著提升客观表现并降低认知负荷，但人类搭档引发更积极的情绪体验。值得注意的是，尽管AI组初始表现更优，其复测成绩下降幅度更大，表明长期学习效果可能不如人类配对。
MetaCloak-JPEG: JPEG-Robust Adversarial Perturbation for Preventing Unauthorized DreamBooth-Based Deepfake Generation
Authors: Tanjim Rahaman Fardin, S M Zunaid Alam, Mahadi Hasan Fahim, Md Faysal Mahfuz
First: 2026-04-20T17:32:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:32:48+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid progress of subject-driven text-to-image synthesis, and in particular DreamBooth, has enabled a consent-free deepfake pipeline: an adversary needs only 4-8 publicly available face images to fine-tune a personalized diffusion model and produce photorealistic harmful content. Current adversarial face-protection systems -- PhotoGuard, Anti-DreamBooth, and MetaCloak -- perturb user images to disrupt surrogate fine-tuning, but all share a structural blindness: none backpropagates gradients through the JPEG compression pipeline that every major social-media platform applies before adversary access. Because JPEG quantization relies on round(), whose derivative is zero almost everywhere, adversarial energy concentrates in high-frequency DCT bands that JPEG discards, eliminating 60-80% of the protective signal. We introduce MetaCloak-JPEG, which closes this gap by inserting a Differentiable JPEG (DiffJPEG) layer built on the Straight-Through Estimator (STE): the forward pass applies standard JPEG compression, while the backward pass replaces round() with the identity. DiffJPEG is embedded in a JPEG-aware EOT distribution (~70% of augmentations include DiffJPEG) and a curriculum quality-factor schedule (QF: 95 to 50) inside a bilevel meta-learning loop. Under an l-inf perturbation budget of eps=8/255, MetaCloak-JPEG attains 32.7 dB PSNR, a 91.3% JPEG survival rate, and outperforms PhotoGuard on all 9 evaluated JPEG quality factors (9/9 wins, mean denoising-loss gain +0.125) within a 4.1 GB training-memory budget.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetaCloak-JPEG：用于防止未经授权的DreamBooth深度伪造生成的JPEG鲁棒对抗扰动
主体驱动的文本到图像合成技术，特别是DreamBooth的快速发展，使得攻击者仅需4-8张公开人脸图像即可微调个性化扩散模型，生成逼真有害内容，形成无需授权的深度伪造流程。当前对抗性人脸保护系统——PhotoGuard、Anti-DreamBooth和MetaCloak——通过扰动用户图像以干扰代理微调，但均存在结构盲区：均未将梯度反向传播通过所有主流社交媒体平台在攻击者访问前应用的JPEG压缩流程。由于JPEG量化依赖round()函数，其导数几乎处处为零，导致对抗能量集中在JPEG丢弃的高频DCT频带，使60-80%的保护信号失效。我们提出MetaCloak-JPEG，通过引入基于Straight-Through Estimator（STE）的可微JPEG（DiffJPEG）层弥补此缺陷：前向传播执行标准JPEG压缩，反向传播则将round()替换为恒等函数。DiffJPEG嵌入于JPEG感知的EOT分布（约70%增强包含DiffJPEG）及双层元学习循环内的课程式质量因子调度（QF：95至50）。在l-inf扰动预算eps=8/255下，MetaCloak-JPEG实现32.7 dB PSNR，JPEG存活率91.3%，并在4.1 GB训练内存预算内于9个评估JPEG质量因子中全面超越PhotoGuard（9/9胜，平均去噪损失提升+0.125）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of current adversarial face-protection systems to JPEG compression, which discards high-frequency perturbations critical for defense, the authors introduce MetaCloak-JPEG: a JPEG-aware adversarial perturbation method that integrates a Differentiable JPEG (DiffJPEG) layer using Straight-Through Estimator to backpropagate gradients through the compression pipeline. The method is trained within a bilevel meta-learning loop with a curriculum-based quality factor schedule and embedded in an EOT distribution that includes DiffJPEG in 70% of augmentations. Under an l-inf budget of 8/255, MetaCloak-JPEG achieves 32.7 dB PSNR, a 91.3% JPEG survival rate, and outperforms existing methods on all 9 tested JPEG quality factors, with a mean denoising-loss gain of +0.125, while operating within a 4.1 GB memory constraint.
针对现有对抗性人脸保护系统在JPEG压缩下失效的问题——因JPEG量化丢弃高频扰动导致防御信号损失60-80%，作者提出MetaCloak-JPEG方法，通过引入基于Straight-Through Estimator的可微JPEG（DiffJPEG）层，使梯度能反向传播至压缩流程。该方法在双层元学习框架中结合渐进式质量因子调度（QF: 95→50）和70%的EOT增强包含DiffJPEG。在l-inf扰动预算8/255下，其PSNR达32.7dB，JPEG存活率91.3%，在9种JPEG质量因子下全面超越PhotoGuard，内存占用仅4.1GB。
Symbolic Synthesis for LTLf+ Obligations
Authors: Giuseppe De Giacomo, Christian Hagemeier, Daniel Hausmann, Nir Piterman
First: 2026-04-20T17:27:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:27:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study synthesis for obligation properties expressed in LTLfp, the extension of LTLf to infinite traces. Obligation properties are positive Boolean combinations of safety and guarantee (co-safety) properties and form the second level of the temporal hierarchy of Manna and Pnueli. Although obligation properties are expressed over infinite traces, they retain most of the simplicity of LTLf. In particular, we show that they admit a translation into symbolically represented deterministic weak automata (DWA) obtained directly from the symbolic deterministic finite automata (DFA) for the underlying LTLf properties on trace prefixes. DWA inherit many of the attractive algorithmic features of DFA, including Boolean closure and polynomial-time minimization. Moreover, we show that synthesis for LTLfp obligation properties is theoretically highly efficient - solvable in linear time once the DWA is constructed. We investigate several symbolic algorithms for solving DWA games that arise in the synthesis of obligation properties and evaluate their effectiveness experimentally. Overall, the results indicate that synthesis for LTLfp obligation properties can be performed with virtually the same effectiveness as LTLf synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LTLf+义务属性的符号综合
我们研究在无限轨迹上表达的义务属性的综合问题，这些属性使用LTLfp（LTLf的扩展）表示。义务属性是安全性和保证（共安全）属性的正布尔组合，属于Manna和Pnueli时序层次结构的第二层。尽管义务属性作用于无限轨迹，但它们保留了LTLf的大部分简洁性。特别是，我们证明它们可翻译为从底层LTLf属性的轨迹前缀的符号确定有限自动机（DFA）直接获得的符号化确定弱自动机（DWA）。DWA继承了DFA的许多吸引人的算法特性，包括布尔闭包和多项式时间最小化。此外，我们证明LTLfp义务属性的综合在理论上高度高效——一旦构造出DWA，即可在线性时间内求解。我们研究了用于解决义务属性综合中出现的DWA博弈的若干符号化算法，并通过实验评估其有效性。总体而言，结果表明LTLfp义务属性的综合可达到与LTLf综合几乎相同的效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to synthesize systems satisfying obligation properties — which are positive combinations of safety and guarantee (co-safety) properties in the second level of Manna and Pnueli’s temporal hierarchy — this work presents a symbolic synthesis approach for LTLfp, an extension of LTLf to infinite traces. The method translates obligation properties into deterministic weak automata (DWA) derived directly from symbolic DFAs of underlying LTLf properties on trace prefixes, preserving algorithmic advantages like Boolean closure and polynomial minimization. Experimental evaluation of symbolic DWA game solvers demonstrates that synthesis for LTLfp obligations is nearly as efficient as for LTLf, with linear-time solvability once the DWA is constructed.
为满足义务性质（即Manna和Pnueli时序层次第二层的正合取安全与保证性质）的系统合成需求，本文提出针对LTLfp（LTLf对无限轨迹的扩展）的符号化合成方法。该方法将义务性质直接转换为从轨迹前缀LTLf性质的符号化DFA导出的确定性弱自动机（DWA），继承了DFA的算法优势如布尔闭包与多项式最小化。实验评估表明，一旦构建DWA，LTLfp义务性质的合成可在线性时间内完成，其效率几乎与LTLf合成相当。
ConMeZO: Adaptive Descent-Direction Sampling for Gradient-Free Finetuning of Large Language Models
Authors: Lejs Deen Behric, Liang Zhang, Bingcong Li, Kiran Koshy Thekumparampil
First: 2025-11-04T17:35:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:27:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zeroth-order or derivative-free optimization (MeZO) is an attractive strategy for finetuning large language models (LLMs) because it eliminates the memory overhead of backpropagation. However, it converges slowly due to the inherent curse of dimensionality when searching for descent directions in the high-dimensional parameter space of billion-scale LLMs. We propose ConMeZO, a novel zeroth-order optimizer that accelerates convergence by adaptive directional sampling. Instead of drawing the direction uniformly at random, ConMeZO restricts the sampling to a cone centered around a momentum estimate. This concentrates the search in directions where the true gradient is more likely to lie and thus reduces the effect of high dimensions. We prove that ConMeZO achieves the same worst-case convergence rate as MeZO. Empirically, when finetuning LLMs on natural language tasks, ConMeZO is up to 2X faster than MeZO while retaining the low-memory footprint of zeroth-order methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ConMeZO：用于大语言模型无梯度微调的自适应下降方向采样
零阶或无导数优化（MeZO）是微调大语言模型（LLMs）的一种有吸引力的策略，因为它消除了反向传播的内存开销。然而，由于在数十亿参数的高维空间中搜索下降方向时固有的维度诅咒，其收敛速度较慢。我们提出了ConMeZO，一种新型零阶优化器，通过自适应方向采样加速收敛。与均匀随机采样方向不同，ConMeZO将采样限制在一个以动量估计为中心的锥体内。这使搜索集中在真实梯度更可能存在的方向上，从而减轻高维影响。我们证明ConMeZO在最坏情况下的收敛速度与MeZO相同。实验证明，在自然语言任务上微调LLMs时，ConMeZO比MeZO快达2倍，同时保持零阶方法的低内存开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory efficiency of zeroth-order optimization but its slow convergence in high-dimensional LLM parameter spaces, the authors propose ConMeZO, a method that adaptively samples descent directions within a cone centered on a momentum estimate to concentrate search toward likely gradient directions. This reduces the impact of the curse of dimensionality while maintaining the memory benefits of derivative-free methods. Experiments show ConMeZO achieves up to 2X faster convergence than MeZO during LLM fine-tuning on natural language tasks without sacrificing memory efficiency.
为解决零阶优化在百亿参数大语言模型中收敛缓慢的问题，作者提出ConMeZO方法，通过在动量估计为中心的锥形区域内采样下降方向，集中搜索更可能包含真实梯度的方向。理论证明其最坏情况收敛速率与MeZO相同，实验表明其在自然语言任务上的微调速度比MeZO快一倍，同时保持零阶方法的低内存开销。
OGER: A Robust Offline-Guided Exploration Reward for Hybrid Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Xinyu Ma, Mingzhou Xu, Xuebo Liu, Chang Jin, Qiang Wang, Derek F. Wong, Min Zhang
First: 2026-04-20T17:26:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:26:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advancements in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) have significantly improved Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, yet models often struggle to explore novel trajectories beyond their initial latent space. While offline teacher guidance and entropy-driven strategies have been proposed to address this, they often lack deep integration or are constrained by the model's inherent capacity. In this paper, we propose OGER, a novel framework that unifies offline teacher guidance and online reinforcement learning through a specialized reward modeling lens. OGER employs multi-teacher collaborative training and constructs an auxiliary exploration reward that leverages both offline trajectories and the model's own entropy to incentivize autonomous exploration. Extensive experiments across mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OGER significantly outperforms competitive baselines, achieving substantial gains in mathematical reasoning while maintaining robust generalization to out-of-domain tasks. We provide a comprehensive analysis of training dynamics and conduct detailed ablation studies to validate the effectiveness of our entropy-aware reward modulation. Our code is available at https://github.com/ecoli-hit/OGER.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OGER：一种用于混合强化学习的鲁棒离线引导探索奖励
近期在可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）方面的进展显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力，但模型往往难以探索其初始潜在空间之外的新轨迹。尽管已有离线教师引导和熵驱动策略被提出以解决此问题，但它们通常缺乏深度整合或受限于模型固有容量。本文提出OGER，一种通过专门奖励建模视角统一离线教师引导与在线强化学习的新框架。OGER采用多教师协同训练，并构建一个辅助探索奖励，结合离线轨迹与模型自身熵，激励自主探索。在数学与通用推理基准上的大量实验表明，OGER显著优于竞争基线，在数学推理方面取得显著提升，同时保持对域外任务的鲁棒泛化能力。我们对训练动态进行了全面分析，并通过详细消融实验验证了熵感知奖励调制的有效性。代码开源于https://github.com/ecoli-hit/OGER.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of LLMs in exploring novel trajectories beyond their initial latent space, this paper introduces OGER, a framework that combines offline teacher guidance with online reinforcement learning via a reward modeling approach. OGER employs multi-teacher collaborative training and constructs an auxiliary exploration reward that integrates offline trajectories and model entropy to encourage autonomous exploration. Experimental results across mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks show OGER consistently outperforms existing baselines, achieving significant gains in mathematical reasoning while preserving strong generalization to out-of-domain tasks.
为解决大语言模型在初始潜在空间外探索能力不足的问题，本文提出OGER框架，通过奖励建模视角融合离线教师引导与在线强化学习。该框架采用多教师协同训练，构建结合离线轨迹与模型熵的辅助探索奖励，以激励自主探索。在数学与通用推理基准上的实验表明，OGER显著优于现有基线方法，在数学推理上取得显著提升，同时保持对域外任务的良好泛化能力。
Neural Surface Reconstruction from Sparse Views Using Epipolar Geometry
Authors: Xinhai Chang, Kaichen Zhou
First: 2024-06-06T17:47:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:21:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing accurate surfaces from sparse multi-view images remains challenging due to severe geometric ambiguity and occlusions. Existing generalizable neural surface reconstruction methods primarily rely on cost volumes that summarize multi-view features using simple statistics (e.g., mean and variance), which discard critical view-dependent geometric structure and often lead to over-smoothed reconstructions. We propose EpiS, a generalizable neural surface reconstruction framework that explicitly leverages epipolar geometry for sparse-view inputs. Instead of directly regressing geometry from cost-volume statistics, EpiS uses coarse cost-volume features to guide the aggregation of fine-grained epipolar features sampled along corresponding epipolar lines across source views. An epipolar transformer fuses multi-view information, followed by ray-wise aggregation to produce SDF-aware features for surface estimation. To further mitigate information loss under sparse views, we introduce a geometry regularization strategy that leverages a pretrained monocular depth model through scale-invariant global and local constraints. Extensive experiments on DTU and BlendedMVS demonstrate that EpiS significantly outperforms state-of-the-art generalizable surface reconstruction methods under sparse-view settings, while maintaining strong generalization without per-scene optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用极线几何从稀疏视图重建神经表面
由于严重的几何歧义和遮挡，从稀疏多视角图像中重建精确表面仍具挑战性。现有可泛化的神经表面重建方法主要依赖于成本体积，通过简单统计（如均值和方差）总结多视角特征，这会丢弃关键的视角依赖几何结构，常导致过度平滑的重建结果。我们提出EpiS，一种针对稀疏视图输入明确利用极线几何的可泛化神经表面重建框架。EpiS不直接从成本体积统计回归几何，而是利用粗略成本体积特征引导沿源视图对应极线采样的细粒度极线特征聚合。极线变换器融合多视角信息，随后按射线聚合生成SDF感知特征以进行表面估计。为进一步缓解稀疏视图下的信息损失，我们引入几何正则化策略，通过尺度不变的全局和局部约束利用预训练的单目深度模型。在DTU和BlendedMVS上的大量实验表明，EpiS在稀疏视图设置下显著优于现有最先进的可泛化表面重建方法，同时无需每场景优化即可保持强泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing accurate surfaces from sparse multi-view images due to geometric ambiguity and occlusions, this paper introduces EpiS, a neural surface reconstruction framework that explicitly incorporates epipolar geometry. Instead of relying on cost-volume statistics that discard view-dependent structure, EpiS uses coarse cost-volume features to guide the aggregation of fine-grained epipolar features sampled along epipolar lines across views, then fuses them via an epipolar transformer and aggregates ray-wise to generate SDF-aware surface features. To handle sparse-view limitations, it employs a geometry regularization strategy using a pretrained monocular depth model for scale-invariant global and local constraints. Experiments on DTU and BlendedMVS show EpiS achieves significantly better reconstruction quality than prior generalizable methods under sparse views, while maintaining strong generalization without per-scene tuning.
为了解决稀疏多视角图像中因几何歧义和遮挡导致的表面重建难题，本文提出EpiS框架，显式利用极线几何信息。该方法不依赖仅含简单统计量（如均值和方差）的成本体积，而是用粗略成本体积特征引导从多视角极线采样细粒度极线特征，通过极线Transformer融合多视角信息，再沿射线聚合生成SDF感知特征。为缓解稀疏视角下的信息损失，引入基于预训练单目深度模型的几何正则化策略，结合尺度不变的全局与局部约束。在DTU和BlendedMVS数据集上的实验表明，EpiS在稀疏视角下显著优于现有通用表面重建方法，且无需场景特定调优即可保持强泛化能力。
Rays as Pixels: Learning A Joint Distribution of Videos and Camera Trajectories
Authors: Wonbong Jang, Shikun Liu, Soubhik Sanyal, Juan Camilo Perez, Kam Woh Ng, Sanskar Agrawal, Juan-Manuel Perez-Rua, Yiannis Douratsos, Tao Xiang
First: 2026-04-10T15:47:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:19:39+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://wbjang.github.io/raysaspixels/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recovering camera parameters from images and rendering scenes from novel viewpoints have been treated as separate tasks in computer vision and graphics. This separation breaks down when image coverage is sparse or poses are ambiguous, since each task depends on what the other produces. We propose Rays as Pixels, a Video Diffusion Model (VDM) that learns a joint distribution over videos and camera trajectories. To our knowledge, this is the first model to predict camera poses and do camera-controlled video generation within a single framework. We represent each camera as dense ray pixels (raxels), a pixel-aligned encoding that lives in the same latent space as video frames, and denoise the two jointly through a Decoupled Self-Cross Attention mechanism. A single trained model handles three tasks: predicting camera trajectories from video, generating video from input images along a pre-defined trajectory, and jointly synthesizing video and trajectory from input images. We evaluate on pose estimation and camera-controlled video generation, and introduce a closed-loop self-consistency test showing that the model's predicted poses and its renderings conditioned on those poses agree. Ablations against Plücker embeddings confirm that representing cameras in a shared latent space with video is subtantially more effective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：光线即像素：学习视频与相机轨迹的联合分布
在计算机视觉和图形学中，从图像恢复相机参数和从新视角渲染场景通常被视为独立任务。然而，当图像覆盖稀疏或姿态模糊时，这种分离失效，因为每个任务都依赖于另一个任务的输出。我们提出“光线即像素”（Rays as Pixels），一种视频扩散模型（VDM），用于学习视频与相机轨迹的联合分布。据我们所知，这是首个在单一框架内同时预测相机姿态并实现相机控制视频生成的模型。我们将每个相机表示为密集的光线像素（raxels），这是一种与视频帧对齐的编码，存在于与视频帧相同的潜在空间中，并通过解耦的自交叉注意力机制联合去噪。单个训练模型可处理三项任务：从视频预测相机轨迹、沿预定义轨迹从输入图像生成视频，以及从输入图像联合合成视频与轨迹。我们在姿态估计和相机控制视频生成任务上进行评估，并引入闭环自一致性测试，表明模型预测的姿态与其基于该姿态条件渲染的结果一致。与Plücker嵌入的消融实验验证了在共享潜在空间中表示相机与视频相比，显著更有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the interdependence between camera pose estimation and novel-view video synthesis, this work introduces Rays as Pixels, a Video Diffusion Model that jointly learns the distribution of videos and camera trajectories. The method encodes each camera as dense ray pixels (raxels) aligned with video frames in a shared latent space, and employs a Decoupled Self-Cross Attention mechanism to jointly denoise both modalities. Experiments show the model successfully handles three tasks: trajectory prediction from video, video generation along predefined paths from images, and joint synthesis of both video and trajectory from images. A closed-loop self-consistency test confirms that predicted poses and rendered videos agree, and ablation studies demonstrate that shared latent representation outperforms Plücker embeddings.
为解决相机姿态估计与新视角视频合成任务间相互依赖的问题，该研究提出Rays as Pixels方法，构建首个联合学习视频与相机轨迹分布的视频扩散模型。方法将相机表示为与视频帧对齐的密集射线像素（raxels），并置于与视频帧共享的潜在空间中，通过解耦自注意力与交叉注意力机制联合去噪。实验表明，该模型可高效完成三项任务——从视频预测轨迹、从输入图像沿预设路径生成视频、从图像联合合成视频与轨迹，且在姿态估计与闭环自一致性测试中表现优异，消融实验验证共享潜在空间表示显著优于Plücker嵌入方法。
Integrating Feature Selection and Machine Learning for Nitrogen Assessment in Grapevine Leaves using In-Field Hyperspectral Imaging
Authors: Atif Bilal Asad, Achyut Paudel, Safal Kshetri, Chenchen Kang, Salik Ram Khanal, Nataliya Shcherbatyuk, Pierre Davadant, R. Paul Schreiner, Santosh Kalauni, Manoj Karkee, Markus Keller
First: 2025-07-23T18:53:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:18:50+00:00
Comments: Major Revision
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Nitrogen (N) is one of the most critical nutrients in winegrape production, influencing vine vigor, fruit composition, and wine quality. Because soil N availability varies spatially and temporally, accurate estimation of leaf N concentration is essential for optimizing fertilization at the individual plant level. In this study, in-field hyperspectral images (400-1000 nm) were collected from four grapevine cultivars (Chardonnay, Pinot Noir, Concord, and Syrah) across two growth stages (bloom and veraison) during the 2022 and 2023 growing seasons at both the leaf and canopy levels. An ensemble feature selection framework was developed to identify the most informative spectral bands for N estimation within individual cultivars, effectively reducing redundancy and selecting compact, physiologically meaningful band combinations spanning the visible, red-edge, and near-infrared regions. At the leaf level, models achieved the highest predictive accuracy for Chardonnay (R^2 = 0.82, RMSE = 0.19 %DW) and Pinot Noir (R^2 = 0.69, RMSE = 0.20 %DW). Canopy-level predictions also performed well, with R^2 values of 0.65, 0.72, and 0.70 for Chardonnay, Concord, and Syrah, respectively. White cultivars exhibited balanced spectral contributions across the visible, red-edge, and near-infrared regions, whereas red cultivars relied more heavily on visible bands due to anthocyanin-chlorophyll interactions. Leaf-level N-sensitive bands selected for Chardonnay and Pinot Noir were successfully transferred to the canopy level, improving or maintaining prediction accuracy across cultivars. These results confirm that ensemble feature selection captures spectrally robust, scale-consistent bands transferable across measurement levels and cultivars, demonstrating the potential of integrating in-field hyperspectral imaging with machine learning for vineyard N status monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用田间高光谱成像结合特征选择与机器学习评估葡萄叶氮含量
氮是酿酒葡萄生产中最关键的营养元素之一，影响植株活力、果实组成和葡萄酒品质。由于土壤氮的可利用性在空间和时间上存在差异，准确估算叶片氮浓度对优化单株施肥至关重要。本研究在2022和2023年生长季，于开花和转色两个生长阶段，对四种葡萄品种（霞多丽、黑皮诺、康科德、西拉）在叶片和冠层水平采集了400-1000 nm波段的田间高光谱图像。开发了一种集成特征选择框架，以识别各品种内对氮估算最有效的光谱波段，有效减少冗余并选择涵盖可见光、红边和近红外区域的紧凑、具有生理意义的波段组合。在叶片水平，霞多丽（R²=0.82，RMSE=0.19%DW）和黑皮诺（R²=0.69，RMSE=0.20%DW）模型预测精度最高。冠层水平预测表现良好，霞多丽、康科德和西拉的R²值分别为0.65、0.72和0.70。白葡萄品种在可见光、红边和近红外区域的光谱贡献均衡，而红葡萄品种则因花青素-叶绿素相互作用更依赖可见光波段。为霞多丽和黑皮诺选择的叶片氮敏感波段成功应用于冠层水平，提升了或维持了跨品种的预测精度。结果证实，集成特征选择能捕捉到在不同测量尺度和品种间可转移的光谱稳健波段，展示了将田间高光谱成像与机器学习结合用于葡萄园氮状况监测的潜力。
Summary / 总结
To address the need for precise nitrogen assessment in grapevines for optimized fertilization, this study used in-field hyperspectral imaging (400-1000 nm) across four cultivars and two growth stages to develop an ensemble feature selection method that identifies physiologically meaningful spectral bands. The method reduced redundancy and selected compact band combinations spanning visible, red-edge, and near-infrared regions, which were then used to train machine learning models. Results showed leaf-level models achieved highest accuracy for Chardonnay (R²=0.82, RMSE=0.19%DW) and Pinot Noir (R²=0.69, RMSE=0.20%DW), while canopy-level predictions remained strong (R²=0.65–0.72). White cultivars utilized bands across all spectral regions, while red cultivars relied more on visible bands due to pigment interactions. Transferability of leaf-selected bands to canopy level maintained or improved accuracy across cultivars, demonstrating the method’s scalability and robustness for vineyard nitrogen monitoring.
为实现葡萄园氮素精准评估以优化施肥，本研究在田间采集了四个品种在两个生长期的高光谱图像（400-1000 nm），并构建了集成特征选择框架，筛选出具有生理意义的光谱波段组合，减少冗余并覆盖可见光、红边与近红外区域。叶面模型中，霞多丽（R²=0.82，RMSE=0.19%DW）和黑皮诺（R²=0.69，RMSE=0.20%DW）表现最佳，冠层预测亦稳健，分别达0.65、0.72和0.70。白葡萄品种光谱贡献分布均衡，红葡萄品种则因花青素与叶绿素相互作用更依赖可见光波段。叶面筛选出的关键波段成功迁移至冠层，跨品种保持或提升预测精度，验证了该方法在不同测量尺度与品种间的可迁移性与鲁棒性。
IDOBE: Infectious Disease Outbreak forecasting Benchmark Ecosystem
Authors: Aniruddha Adiga, Jingyuan Chou, Anshul Chiranth, Bryan Lewis, Ana I. Bento, Shaun Truelove, Geoffrey Fox, Madhav Marathe, Harry Hochheiser, Srini Venkatramanan
First: 2026-04-20T17:18:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:18:18+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Epidemic forecasting has become an integral part of real-time infectious disease outbreak response. While collaborative ensembles composed of statistical and machine learning models have become the norm for real-time forecasting, standardized benchmark datasets for evaluating such methods are lacking. Further, there is limited understanding on performance of these methods for novel outbreaks with limited historical data. In this paper, we propose IDOBE, a curated collection of epidemiological time series focused on outbreak forecasting. IDOBE compiles from multiple data repositories spanning over a century of surveillance and across U.S. states and global locations. We perform derivative-based segmentation to generate over 10,000 outbreaks covering multiple outcomes such as cases and hospitalizations for 13 diseases. We consider a variety of information-theoretic and distributional measures to quantify the epidemiological diversity of the dataset. Finally, we perform multi-horizon short-term forecasting (1- to 4-week-ahead) through the progression of the outbreak using 11 baseline models and report on their performance. In addition to standard metrics such as NMSE and MAPE for point forecasts, we include probabilistic scoring rules such as Normalized Weighted Interval Score (NWIS) to quantify the performance. We find that MLP-based methods have the most robust performance, with statistical methods having a slight edge during the pre-peak phase. IDOBE dataset along with baselines are released publicly on https://github.com/NSSAC/IDOBE to enable standardized, reproducible benchmarking of outbreak forecasting methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IDOBE：传染病爆发预测基准生态系统
流行病预测已成为实时应对传染病爆发的重要组成部分。尽管由统计模型和机器学习模型组成的协同集成方法已成为实时预测的常规做法，但用于评估此类方法的标准基准数据集仍缺乏。此外，对于历史数据有限的新爆发事件，这些方法的性能尚缺乏充分理解。本文提出IDOBE，一个专注于爆发预测的流行病学时间序列精选数据集。IDOBE整合了跨越一个世纪、覆盖美国各州及全球多个地点的多个数据源。我们通过基于导数的分段方法生成了超过10,000次爆发事件，涵盖13种疾病的多种结局（如病例数和住院人数）。我们采用多种信息论和分布度量来量化数据集的流行病学多样性。最后，我们通过11个基线模型对爆发进程进行多时间尺度短期预测（1至4周），并报告其性能。除NMSE和MAPE等标准点预测指标外，我们还引入了归一化加权区间得分（NWIS）等概率评分规则以量化性能。结果表明，基于MLP的方法表现最为稳健，统计方法在爆发前峰阶段略占优势。IDOBE数据集及基线模型已公开发布于https://github.com/NSSAC/IDOBE，以支持爆发预测方法的标准化、可重复基准测试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized benchmarks to evaluate outbreak forecasting methods and to better understand model performance during novel outbreaks with limited historical data, this paper introduces IDOBE, a curated dataset of epidemiological time series spanning over a century and across U.S. states and global locations. The dataset is constructed through derivative-based segmentation to generate over 10,000 outbreaks for 13 diseases, covering outcomes like cases and hospitalizations, and is characterized using information-theoretic and distributional measures. The authors evaluate 11 baseline models using multi-horizon short-term forecasting (1–4 weeks ahead), reporting performance via standard metrics like NMSE and MAPE, as well as probabilistic scores such as NWIS. Results show MLP-based models achieve the most robust performance overall, while statistical methods slightly outperform others during the pre-peak phase.
为解决流行病预测方法缺乏标准化基准评估的问题，并理解在历史数据有限的新疫情下模型表现，作者构建了IDOBE数据集，该数据集由跨越百年、覆盖美国各州及全球的流行病学时间序列组成，通过导数分割生成13种疾病超1万次疫情爆发的病例与住院数据。他们采用信息论方法量化数据多样性，并对11种基线模型在1至4周预测窗口内的表现进行评估，使用点预测指标（NMSE、MAPE）和概率评分（NWIS），结果表明MLP类模型整体表现最稳健，统计方法在疫情上升前阶段略占优势。
Unleashing Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models via Textual Representation Guided Reasoning
Authors: Jiacheng Hua, Yishu Yin, Yuhang Wu, Tai Wang, Yifei Huang, Miao Liu
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-03-24T16:38:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:18:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026. 22 pages, 6 figures, 10 tables. Project page: https://trace-reasoning.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) struggle with 3D spatial reasoning, as they fail to construct structured abstractions of the 3D environment depicted in video inputs. To bridge this gap, drawing inspiration from cognitive theories of allocentric spatial reasoning, we investigate how to enable MLLMs to model and reason over text-based spatial representations of video. Specifically, we introduce Textual Representation of Allocentric Context from Egocentric Video (TRACE), a prompting method that induces MLLMs to generate text-based representations of 3D environments as intermediate reasoning traces for more accurate spatial question answering. TRACE encodes meta-context, camera trajectories, and detailed object entities to support structured spatial reasoning over egocentric videos. Extensive experiments on VSI-Bench and OST-Bench demonstrate that TRACE yields notable and consistent improvements over prior prompting strategies across a diverse range of MLLM backbones, spanning different parameter scales and training schemas. We further present ablation studies to validate our design choices, along with detailed analyses that probe the bottlenecks of 3D spatial reasoning in MLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过文本表示引导的推理在多模态大语言模型中释放空间推理能力
现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在3D空间推理方面存在困难，因为它们无法构建视频输入中3D环境的结构化抽象。为弥合这一差距，我们借鉴认知理论中的 allocentric 空间推理思想，研究如何使MLLMs能够建模和推理视频的基于文本的空间表示。具体而言，我们提出了“从第一人称视频中提取allocentric上下文的文本表示”（TRACE）方法，该方法通过引导MLLMs生成3D环境的文本表示作为中间推理轨迹，以提高空间问答的准确性。TRACE编码元上下文、摄像机轨迹和详细物体实体，以支持对第一人称视频的结构化空间推理。在VSI-Bench和OST-Bench上的大量实验表明，TRACE在多种MLLM架构（涵盖不同参数规模和训练方案）上均显著且稳定地优于现有提示策略。我们进一步通过消融研究验证设计选择，并辅以详细分析，探讨MLLMs在3D空间推理中的瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in 3D spatial reasoning, this work introduces TRACE, a prompting method inspired by allocentric spatial cognition, to guide MLLMs in generating text-based spatial representations from egocentric videos. TRACE encodes meta-context, camera trajectories, and object entities to enable structured reasoning, serving as intermediate reasoning traces for spatial question answering. Experiments on VSI-Bench and OST-Bench show consistent performance gains across diverse MLLM backbones, with ablation studies confirming the effectiveness of its design components.
为解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在三维空间推理中的不足，本文提出TRACE方法，通过引导模型从第一人称视频中生成文本形式的空间表示，以支持结构化推理。TRACE编码元上下文、相机轨迹和物体实体，构建以观察者为中心的空间抽象作为中间推理轨迹。在VSI-Bench和OST-Bench上的实验表明，该方法在多种模型架构、参数规模和训练范式下均取得显著且稳定的性能提升，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
LLM Safety From Within: Detecting Harmful Content with Internal Representations
Authors: Difan Jiao, Yilun Liu, Ye Yuan, Zhenwei Tang, Linfeng Du, Haolun Wu, Ashton Anderson
First: 2026-04-20T17:17:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:17:07+00:00
Comments: 17 pages,10 figures,6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Guard models are widely used to detect harmful content in user prompts and LLM responses. However, state-of-the-art guard models rely solely on terminal-layer representations and overlook the rich safety-relevant features distributed across internal layers. We present SIREN, a lightweight guard model that harnesses these internal features. By identifying safety neurons via linear probing and combining them through an adaptive layer-weighted strategy, SIREN builds a harmfulness detector from LLM internals without modifying the underlying model. Our comprehensive evaluation shows that SIREN substantially outperforms state-of-the-art open-source guard models across multiple benchmarks while using 250 times fewer trainable parameters. Moreover, SIREN exhibits superior generalization to unseen benchmarks, naturally enables real-time streaming detection, and significantly improves inference efficiency compared to generative guard models. Overall, our results highlight LLM internal states as a promising foundation for practical, high-performance harmfulness detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从内部保障大语言模型安全：利用内部表征检测有害内容
守卫模型广泛用于检测用户提示和大语言模型响应中的有害内容。然而，当前最先进的守卫模型仅依赖终端层表征，忽视了分布在内部各层中的丰富安全相关特征。我们提出了SIREN，一种轻量级守卫模型，利用这些内部特征。通过线性探测识别安全神经元，并结合自适应层加权策略，SIREN无需修改底层模型即可从大语言模型内部构建有害性检测器。全面评估表明，SIREN在多个基准测试中显著优于当前最先进的开源守卫模型，且参数量仅为后者的1/250。此外，SIREN在未见基准测试中表现出更强的泛化能力，天然支持实时流式检测，并显著提升推理效率，相较生成式守卫模型更具优势。总体而言，我们的结果凸显大语言模型内部状态作为实用、高性能有害性检测基础的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing guard models that rely only on terminal-layer representations, the authors propose SIREN, a lightweight detector that leverages internal layer features of LLMs to identify harmful content. SIREN identifies safety-relevant neurons via linear probing and combines them using an adaptive layer-weighted strategy, enabling detection without modifying the base model. Experimental results show SIREN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art open-source guard models across multiple benchmarks, uses 250 times fewer trainable parameters, generalizes well to unseen datasets, supports real-time streaming, and improves inference efficiency compared to generative guard models.
鉴于现有守卫模型仅依赖模型最后一层表示而忽略内部层中丰富的安全相关特征，作者提出SIREN，一种轻量级检测器，通过线性探测识别安全神经元，并采用自适应层加权策略组合这些特征，无需修改基础模型。实验表明，SIREN在多个基准测试中显著优于当前最先进的开源守卫模型，参数量仅为后者的1/250，对未见数据集泛化能力更强，支持实时流式检测，并在推理效率上优于生成式守卫模型。
UDM-GRPO: Stable and Efficient Group Relative Policy Optimization for Uniform Discrete Diffusion Models
Authors: Jiaqi Wang, Haoge Deng, Ting Pan, Yang Liu, Chengyuan Wang, Fan Zhang, Yonggang Qi, Xinlong Wang
First: 2026-04-20T17:16:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:16:50+00:00
Comments: Code:\href{https://github.com/Yovecent/UDM-GRPO}{this https URL}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Uniform Discrete Diffusion Model (UDM) has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for discrete generative modeling; however, its integration with reinforcement learning remains largely unexplored. We observe that naively applying GRPO to UDM leads to training instability and marginal performance gains. To address this, we propose \Ours, the first framework to integrate UDM with RL. Our method is guided by two key insights: (i) treating the final clean sample as the action provides more accurate and stable optimization signals; and (ii) reconstructing trajectories via the diffusion forward process better aligns probability paths with the pretraining distribution. Additionally, we introduce two strategies, Reduced-Step and CFG-Free, to further improve training efficiency. \Ours significantly improves base model performance across multiple T2I tasks. Notably, GenEval accuracy improves from $69\%$ to $96\%$ and PickScore increases from $20.46$ to $23.81$, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both continuous and discrete settings. On the OCR benchmark, accuracy rises from $8\%$ to $57\%$, further validating the generalization ability of our method. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Yovecent/UDM-GRPO}{https://github.com/Yovecent/UDM-GRPO}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UDM-GRPO：用于均匀离散扩散模型的稳定高效组相对策略优化
均匀离散扩散模型（UDM）最近作为一种有前景的离散生成建模范式崭露头角；然而，其与强化学习的结合仍鲜有探索。我们观察到，直接将GRPO应用于UDM会导致训练不稳定且性能提升有限。为此，我们提出\Ours，首个将UDM与RL结合的框架。我们的方法基于两个关键见解：(i) 将最终干净样本视为动作可提供更准确稳定的优化信号；(ii) 通过扩散前向过程重建轨迹能更好地使概率路径与预训练分布对齐。此外，我们引入两种策略——减少步数和无CFG——进一步提升训练效率。\Ours在多个T2I任务中显著提升基础模型性能。值得注意的是，GenEval准确率从69%提升至96%，PickScore从20.46提升至23.81，在连续和离散设置中均达到最先进水平。在OCR基准测试中，准确率从8%提升至57%，进一步验证了方法的泛化能力。代码见\href{https://github.com/Yovecent/UDM-GRPO}{https://github.com/Yovecent/UDM-GRPO}。

Motivated by the need to integrate reinforcement learning with Uniform Discrete Diffusion Models (UDM) for improved discrete generative modeling, this work proposes UDM-GRPO, the first framework to combine UDM with RL. The method leverages two key insights: treating the final clean sample as the action for stable optimization and reconstructing trajectories via the diffusion forward process to align probability paths with pretraining distributions. It further enhances efficiency with Reduced-Step and CFG-Free strategies. Experimental results show significant performance gains across T2I tasks, with GenEval accuracy rising from 69% to 96% and PickScore from 20.46 to 23.81, achieving state-of-the-art results in both continuous and discrete settings, and OCR accuracy improving from 8% to 57%, demonstrating strong generalization.

An LLM-Guided Query-Aware Inference System for GNN Models on Large Knowledge Graphs
Authors: Waleed Afandi, Hussein Abdallah, Ashraf Aboulnaga, Essam Mansour
First: 2026-03-04T19:30:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:16:33+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficient inference for graph neural networks (GNNs) on large knowledge graphs (KGs) is essential for many real-world applications. GNN inference queries are computationally expensive and vary in complexity, as each involves a different number of target nodes linked to subgraphs of diverse densities and structures. Existing acceleration methods, such as pruning, quantization, and knowledge distillation, instantiate smaller models but do not adapt them to the structure or semantics of individual queries. They also store models as monolithic files that must be fully loaded, and miss the opportunity to retrieve only the neighboring nodes and corresponding model components that are semantically relevant to the target nodes. These limitations lead to excessive data loading and redundant computation on large KGs. This paper presents KG-WISE, a task-driven inference paradigm for large KGs. KG-WISE decomposes trained GNN models into fine-grained components that can be partially loaded based on the structure of the queried subgraph. It employs large language models (LLMs) to generate reusable query templates that extract semantically relevant subgraphs for each task, enabling query-aware and compact model instantiation. We evaluate KG-WISE on six large KGs with up to 42 million nodes and 166 million edges. KG-WISE achieves up to 28x faster inference and 98% lower memory usage than state-of-the-art systems while maintaining or improving accuracy across both commercial and open-weight LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大型知识图谱的GNN模型LLM引导的查询感知推理系统
在大型知识图谱（KGs）上对图神经网络（GNNs）进行高效推理，对许多现实应用至关重要。GNN推理查询计算成本高且复杂度各异，因为每个查询涉及不同数量的目标节点，且与结构密度和多样性各异的子图相连。现有加速方法（如剪枝、量化和知识蒸馏）虽能生成较小模型，但无法根据单个查询的结构或语义进行自适应调整，且将模型存储为单体文件，必须完全加载，未能利用仅检索与目标节点语义相关的邻接节点及对应模型组件的机会。这些限制导致在大型KG上产生大量数据加载和冗余计算。本文提出KG-WISE，一种面向大型KG的任务驱动推理范式。KG-WISE将训练好的GNN模型分解为细粒度组件，可根据查询子图结构部分加载。它利用大语言模型（LLMs）生成可复用的查询模板，提取每个任务的语义相关子图，实现查询感知且紧凑的模型实例化。我们在六个最大含4200万节点和1.66亿边的大型KG上评估KG-WISE。相比现有最先进系统，KG-WISE推理速度提升最高达28倍，内存使用降低98%，同时在商业和开源LLM上保持或提升精度。

To address the inefficiency of GNN inference on large knowledge graphs, this paper introduces KG-WISE, a query-aware inference system that dynamically loads only the model components and neighboring nodes relevant to each query. It leverages large language models to generate reusable query templates that extract semantically aligned subgraphs, enabling fine-grained model instantiation without full model loading. Evaluated on six large KGs with up to 166 million edges, KG-WISE achieves up to 28x faster inference and 98% lower memory usage compared to existing methods, while preserving or improving accuracy across both commercial and open-weight LLMs.
为解决大型知识图谱上GNN推理效率低、现有方法无法适配查询结构与语义的问题，KG-WISE提出一种由大语言模型引导的查询感知推理系统。该系统将训练好的GNN模型分解为细粒度组件，根据查询子图结构动态加载相关部分，并利用大语言模型生成可复用模板以提取语义相关的邻居节点。在包含最多4200万节点的六个大型知识图谱上评估，KG-WISE相较现有方法推理速度提升最高达28倍，内存占用降低98%，同时保持或提升了准确率，适用于商业与开源大语言模型。
Scaling Recurrence-aware Foundation Models for Clinical Records via Next-Visit Prediction
Authors: Haresh Rengaraj Rajamohan, Xiang Gao, Weicheng Zhu, Shih-Lun Huang, Long Chen, Gabe Schulman, Huizhen Jin, Shengduo Li, Yixuan Wang, Huidi Yang, Kyunghyun Cho, Cem M. Deniz, Narges Razavian
First: 2026-03-25T17:42:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:16:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While large-scale pretraining has revolutionized language modeling, its potential remains underexplored in healthcare with structured electronic health records (EHRs). We present RAVEN, a novel generative pretraining strategy for sequential EHR data based on Recurrence-Aware next-Visit EveNt prediction. Leveraging a dataset of over one million unique individuals, our model learns to autoregressively generate tokenized clinical events for the next visit conditioned on patient history. We introduce regularization on predicting repeated events and highlight a key pitfall in EHR-based foundation model evaluations: repeated event tokens can inflate performance metrics when new onsets are not distinguished from subsequent occurrences. Furthermore, we empirically investigate the scaling behaviors in a data-constrained, compute-saturated regime, showing that simply increasing model size is suboptimal without commensurate increases in data volume. We evaluate our model via zero-shot prediction for forecasting the incidence of a diverse set of diseases, where it rivals fully fine-tuned representation-based Transformer models and outperforms both standard simulation-based next-token approaches and a prompted medical large language model baseline. Finally, without additional parameter updates, we show that RAVEN can generalize to an external patient cohort under lossy clinical code mappings and feature coverage gaps.

为解决医疗电子健康记录中大规模预训练应用不足的问题，作者提出RAVEN方法，基于患者历史自回归生成下一次就诊的临床事件。模型在超百万患者数据上训练，并引入重复事件正则化，揭示重复事件可能虚高评估指标。实验表明仅扩大模型规模而不增加数据量效果不佳，RAVEN在零样本疾病预测中表现优异，超越标准下个token生成和提示式医疗大模型基线，且无需额外参数调整即可在部分临床编码映射缺失的情况下泛化至外部患者群体。
TrianguLang: Geometry-Aware Semantic Consensus for Pose-Free 3D Localization
Authors: Bryce Grant, Aryeh Rothenberg, Atri Banerjee, Peng Wang
First: 2026-03-09T08:37:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:13:59+00:00
Comments: Tables updated with current results, typographical errors fixed
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Localizing objects and parts from natural language in 3D space is essential for robotics, AR, and embodied AI, yet existing methods face a trade-off between the accuracy and geometric consistency of per-scene optimization and the efficiency of feed-forward inference. We present TrianguLang, a feed-forward framework for 3D localization that requires no camera calibration at inference. Unlike prior methods that treat views independently, we introduce Geometry-Aware Semantic Attention (GASA), which utilizes predicted geometry to gate cross-view feature correspondence, suppressing semantically-plausible but geometrically-inconsistent matches without requiring ground-truth poses. Validated on five benchmarks including ScanNet++ and uCO3D, TrianguLang achieves state-of-the-art feed-forward text-guided segmentation and localization, reducing user effort from $O(N)$ clicks to a single text query. The model processes each frame at 1008x1008 resolution in $\sim$57ms ($\sim$18 FPS) without optimization, enabling practical deployment for interactive robotics and AR applications. Code and checkpoints are available at https://cwru-aism.github.io/triangulang/.

为实现机器人和AR场景中无需姿态校准的自然语言驱动3D定位，TrianguLang提出一种前馈框架，引入几何感知语义注意力（GASA）机制，利用预测几何信息筛选跨视图特征对应关系，抑制语义合理但几何不一致的匹配，无需真实姿态数据。在包括ScanNet++和uCO3D在内的五个基准上，该模型在前馈文本引导分割与定位任务中达到当前最优性能，将用户交互从O(N)次点击降至单次文本查询，且在1008x1008分辨率下无需优化即可实现约57毫秒（约18帧/秒）的处理速度。
SEARL: Joint Optimization of Policy and Tool Graph Memory for Self-Evolving Agents
Authors: Xinshun Feng, Xinhao Song, Lijun Li, Gongshen Liu, Jing Shao
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-09T04:38:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:11:17+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) have demonstrated significant potential in single-turn reasoning tasks. With the paradigm shift toward self-evolving agentic learning, models are increasingly expected to learn from trajectories by synthesizing tools or accumulating explicit experiences. However, prevailing methods typically rely on large-scale LLMs or multi-agent frameworks, which hinder their deployment in resource-constrained environments. The inherent sparsity of outcome-based rewards also poses a substantial challenge, as agents typically receive feedback only upon completion of tasks. To address these limitations, we introduce a Tool-Memory based self-evolving agentic framework SEARL. Unlike approaches that directly utilize interaction experiences, our method constructs a structured experience memory that integrates planning with execution. This provides a novel state abstraction that facilitates generalization across analogous contexts, such as tool reuse. Consequently, agents extract explicit knowledge from historical data while leveraging inter-trajectory correlations to densify reward signals. We evaluate our framework on knowledge reasoning and mathematics tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness in achieving more practical and efficient learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SEARL：自进化代理的策略与工具图记忆联合优化
近年来，可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）在单轮推理任务中展现出巨大潜力。随着自进化代理学习范式的转变，模型被期望通过合成工具或积累显式经验从轨迹中学习。然而，现有方法通常依赖大规模语言模型或多智能体框架，限制了其在资源受限环境中的部署。基于结果的奖励固有稀疏性也构成重大挑战，因为代理通常仅在任务完成后才获得反馈。为解决这些局限，我们提出基于工具记忆的自进化代理框架SEARL。与直接利用交互经验的方法不同，我们的方法构建结构化经验记忆，将规划与执行相结合，提供一种新颖的状态抽象，促进在类似情境（如工具复用）下的泛化。因此，代理从历史数据中提取显式知识，并利用轨迹间相关性增强奖励信号密度。我们在知识推理和数学任务上评估该框架，证明其在实现更实用、高效学习方面的有效性。

Motivated by the need for efficient self-evolving agents that can learn from sparse rewards without relying on large LLMs or multi-agent setups, SEARL introduces a tool-memory based framework that jointly optimizes policy and tool graph memory. Instead of directly using interaction experiences, it constructs a structured memory that integrates planning and execution, enabling novel state abstractions that support generalization across similar contexts like tool reuse. Experimental results on knowledge reasoning and mathematics tasks show that SEARL enables agents to extract explicit knowledge from historical trajectories and densify reward signals through inter-trajectory correlations, leading to more practical and efficient learning.
为了解决现有方法依赖大模型或多智能体框架、难以在资源受限环境下部署且奖励稀疏的问题，SEARL提出一种基于工具记忆的自进化智能体框架，通过构建融合规划与执行的结构化经验记忆，实现新颖的状态抽象以支持跨相似场景（如工具复用）的泛化能力。实验表明，该框架能从历史数据中提取显式知识并利用轨迹间关联强化奖励信号，在知识推理与数学任务上展现出更实用高效的自进化学习效果。
MaLoRA: Gated Modality LoRA for Key-Space Alignment in Multimodal LLM Fine-Tuning
Authors: Xinhan Zheng, Huyu Wu, Xueting Wang, Duo Su, Haiyun Jiang
First: 2025-10-30T17:22:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:11:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit a pronounced preference for textual inputs when processing vision-language data, limiting their ability to reason effectively from visual evidence. Unlike prior studies that attribute this text bias to external factors such as data imbalance or instruction tuning, we propose that the bias originates from the model's internal architecture. Specifically, we hypothesize that visual key vectors (Visual Keys) are out-of-distribution (OOD) relative to the text key space learned during language-only pretraining. Consequently, these visual keys receive systematically lower similarity scores during attention computation, leading to their under-utilization in the context representation. To validate this hypothesis, we extract key vectors from LLaVA and Qwen2.5-VL and analyze their distributional structures using qualitative (t-SNE) and quantitative (Jensen-Shannon divergence) methods. The results provide direct evidence that visual and textual keys occupy markedly distinct subspaces within the attention space. The inter-modal divergence is statistically significant, exceeding intra-modal variation by several orders of magnitude. These findings reveal that text bias arises from an intrinsic misalignment within the attention key space rather than solely from external data factors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MaLoRA：用于多模态大语言模型微调中关键空间对齐的门控模态LoRA
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在处理视觉-语言数据时表现出对文本输入的明显偏好，限制了其从视觉证据有效推理的能力。与以往将此文本偏见归因于数据不平衡或指令调优等外部因素不同，我们提出该偏见源于模型内部架构。具体而言，我们假设视觉键向量（视觉键）相对于语言预训练期间学习到的文本键空间属于分布外（OOD）。因此，在注意力计算中，这些视觉键会系统性地获得较低的相似度得分，导致其在上下文表示中被低估。为验证该假设，我们从LLaVA和Qwen2.5-VL中提取键向量，并使用定性（t-SNE）和定量（Jensen-Shannon散度）方法分析其分布结构。结果直接表明，视觉键与文本键在注意力空间中占据显著不同的子空间。跨模态差异具有统计显著性，其程度远超模态内差异数个数量级。这些发现揭示，文本偏见源于注意力键空间内的内在错位，而非仅由外部数据因素导致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that multimodal LLMs exhibit a strong preference for textual inputs over visual data, this study investigates whether the bias stems from internal architectural limitations rather than external data factors. The authors propose that visual key vectors are out-of-distribution relative to the text key space learned during language-only pretraining, leading to lower attention similarity scores and under-utilization of visual context. To validate this, they extract and analyze key vectors from LLaVA and Qwen2.5-VL using t-SNE and Jensen-Shannon divergence, revealing that visual and textual keys occupy distinct, statistically divergent subspaces in the attention space, with inter-modal divergence far exceeding intra-modal variation.
鉴于多模态大语言模型在处理视觉-语言数据时表现出对文本输入的明显偏好，本研究探讨该偏差是否源于模型内部架构限制而非外部数据失衡。作者假设视觉键向量相对于语言预训练阶段学习到的文本键空间属于分布外，导致注意力计算中相似度得分偏低，从而削弱视觉上下文的利用。通过从LLaVA和Qwen2.5-VL中提取键向量并使用t-SNE和Jensen-Shannon散度进行分析，结果表明视觉键与文本键在注意力键空间中占据显著不同的子空间，且跨模态差异远大于模态内差异。
MT-OSC: Path for LLMs that Get Lost in Multi-Turn Conversation
Authors: Jyotika Singh, Fang Tu, Miguel Ballesteros, Weiyi Sun, Sandip Ghoshal, Michelle Yuan, Yassine Benajiba, Sujith Ravi, Dan Roth
First: 2026-04-09T21:39:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:10:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) suffer significant performance degradation when user instructions and context are distributed over multiple conversational turns, yet multi-turn (MT) interactions dominate chat interfaces. The routine approach of appending full chat history to prompts rapidly exhausts context windows, leading to increased latency, higher computational costs, and diminishing returns as conversations extend. We introduce MT-OSC, a One-off Sequential Condensation framework that efficiently and automatically condenses chat history in the background without disrupting the user experience. MT-OSC employs a Condenser Agent that uses a few-shot inference-based Condenser and a lightweight Decider to selectively retain essential information, reducing token counts by up to 72% in 10-turn dialogues. Evaluated across 13 state-of-the-art LLMs and diverse multi-turn benchmarks, MT-OSC consistently narrows the multi-turn performance gap - yielding improved or preserved accuracy across datasets while remaining robust to distractors and irrelevant turns. Our results establish MT-OSC as a scalable solution for multi-turn chats, enabling richer context within constrained input spaces, reducing latency and operational cost, while balancing performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MT-OSC：解决大语言模型在多轮对话中迷失路径的方法
当用户指令和上下文分散在多轮对话中时，大语言模型（LLM）性能会显著下降，但多轮（MT）交互在聊天界面中占主导地位。将完整聊天历史附加到提示中会迅速耗尽上下文窗口，导致延迟增加、计算成本上升，且随着对话延长回报递减。我们提出MT-OSC，一种一次性顺序压缩框架，可在后台高效自动压缩聊天历史，且不影响用户体验。MT-OSC采用压缩代理，结合基于少量示例推理的压缩器和轻量级决策器，选择性保留关键信息，在10轮对话中可将token数量减少高达72%。在13个最先进的LLM和多样化的多轮基准测试中，MT-OSC持续缩小多轮性能差距——在各类数据集上保持或提升准确率，同时对干扰项和无关轮次具有鲁棒性。我们的结果确立MT-OSC为多轮聊天的可扩展解决方案，能在受限输入空间内提供更丰富的上下文，降低延迟和运营成本，同时平衡性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance degradation of LLMs in multi-turn conversations due to context window exhaustion, MT-OSC introduces a One-off Sequential Condensation framework that automatically condenses chat history in the background without affecting user experience. It employs a Condenser Agent with a few-shot inference-based Condenser and a lightweight Decider to selectively retain critical information, reducing token counts by up to 72% in 10-turn dialogues. Evaluated across 13 LLMs and diverse benchmarks, MT-OSC consistently closes the multi-turn performance gap, preserving or improving accuracy while remaining robust to irrelevant turns, and reduces latency and computational cost.
为了解决大语言模型在多轮对话中因上下文窗口耗尽导致性能下降的问题，MT-OSC提出了一种后台自动压缩对话历史的“一次性顺序压缩”框架。该框架通过一个包含少量示例推理的压缩器和轻量级决策器组成的压缩代理，选择性保留关键信息，使10轮对话的token数量减少高达72%。在13个主流大模型和多个多轮基准测试中，MT-OSC持续缩小多轮对话性能差距，保持或提升准确率，同时对无关轮次具有鲁棒性，并有效降低延迟与计算开销。
MedRedFlag: Investigating how LLMs Redirect Misconceptions in Real-World Health Communication
Authors: Sraavya Sambara, Yuan Pu, Ayman Ali, Vishala Mishra, Lionel Wong, Monica Agrawal
First: 2026-01-14T20:23:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:07:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-world health questions from patients often unintentionally embed false assumptions or premises. In such cases, safe medical communication typically involves redirection: addressing the implicit misconception and then responding to the underlying patient context, rather than the original question. While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used by lay users for medical advice, they have not yet been tested for this crucial competency. Therefore, in this work, we investigate how LLMs react to false premises embedded within real-world health questions. We develop a semi-automated pipeline to curate MedRedFlag, a dataset of 1100+ questions sourced from Reddit that require redirection. We then systematically compare responses from state-of-the-art LLMs to those from clinicians. Our analysis reveals that LLMs often fail to redirect problematic questions, even when the problematic premise is detected, and provide answers that could lead to suboptimal medical decision making. Our benchmark and results reveal a novel and substantial gap in how LLMs perform under the conditions of real-world health communication, highlighting critical safety concerns for patient-facing medical AI systems. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/srsambara-1/MedRedFlag.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedRedFlag：探究大语言模型如何在现实健康沟通中纠正误解
患者在现实世界中提出健康问题时，往往无意中包含错误的假设或前提。在这种情况下，安全的医疗沟通通常需要‘转向’：先处理隐含的误解，再回应患者的真实情境，而非直接回应原问题。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被普通用户用于获取医疗建议，但它们尚未经过此类关键能力的测试。因此，本研究探讨了LLMs如何应对嵌入在现实健康问题中的错误前提。我们开发了一个半自动流程，整理出MedRedFlag数据集，该数据集包含1100多个来自Reddit的需要转向处理的问题。随后，我们系统性地比较了当前最先进的LLMs与临床医生的回答。分析表明，即使检测到错误前提，LLMs往往未能有效转向问题，且提供的答案可能导致次优的医疗决策。我们的基准测试和结果揭示了LLMs在现实健康沟通场景下存在显著且新颖的性能差距，凸显了面向患者的医疗AI系统所面临的关键安全问题。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/srsambara-1/MedRedFlag获取。
