鉴于现实医疗沟通中需要识别并纠正患者提问中的错误前提，本研究考察了大语言模型（LLM）在处理此类问题时的表现。研究构建了包含1100多个需转向回答的Reddit健康问题数据集MedRedFlag，并对比了LLM与临床医生的回答。结果发现，即使LLM能识别错误前提，仍常未能转向回答，反而提供可能误导医疗决策的建议，揭示了LLM在真实医疗场景中存在显著性能缺陷，对面向患者的医疗AI系统构成安全风险。
S2H-DPO: Hardness-Aware Preference Optimization for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Nitish Shukla, Surgan Jandial, Arun Ross
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-20T17:06:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:06:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable progress in single-image understanding, yet effective reasoning across multiple images remains challenging. We identify a critical capability gap in existing multi-image alignment approaches: current methods focus primarily on localized reasoning with pre-specified image indices (``Look at Image 3 and...''), bypassing the essential skills of global visual search and autonomous cross-image comparison. To address this limitation, we introduce a Simple-to-Hard (S2H) learning framework that systematically constructs multi-image preference data across three hierarchical reasoning levels requiring an increasing level of capabilities: (1) single-image localized reasoning, (2) multi-image localized comparison, and (3) global visual search. Unlike prior work that relies on model-specific attributes, such as hallucinations or attention heuristics, to generate preference pairs, our approach leverages prompt-driven complexity to create chosen/rejected pairs that are applicable across different models. Through extensive evaluations on LLaVA and Qwen-VL models, we show that our diverse multi-image reasoning data significantly enhances multi-image reasoning performance, yielding significant improvements over baseline methods across benchmarks. Importantly, our approach maintains strong single-image reasoning performance while simultaneously strengthening multi-image understanding capabilities, thus advancing the state of the art for holistic visual preference alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：S2H-DPO：面向视觉-语言模型的硬度感知偏好优化
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在单图像理解方面取得了显著进展，但在多图像间的有效推理仍具挑战。我们发现现有多图像对齐方法存在关键能力缺口：当前方法主要聚焦于预指定图像索引的局部推理（如“查看图像3并...”），忽略了全局视觉搜索与自主跨图像比较等核心能力。为解决此问题，我们提出一种由简单到困难（S2H）的学习框架，系统构建跨越三个层级递进推理能力的多图像偏好数据：（1）单图像局部推理，（2）多图像局部比较，（3）全局视觉搜索。与依赖模型特定属性（如幻觉或注意力启发式）生成偏好对的现有方法不同，我们的方法通过提示驱动的复杂度生成适用于不同模型的优选/拒绝对。在LLaVA和Qwen-VL模型上的广泛评估表明，我们的多样化多图像推理数据显著提升多图像推理性能，在多个基准测试中优于基线方法。重要的是，我们的方法在保持单图像推理性能的同时，增强了多图像理解能力，从而推动了整体视觉偏好对齐的前沿。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing VLMs in multi-image reasoning, this work introduces S2H-DPO, a hardness-aware preference optimization framework that systematically generates preference data across three hierarchical reasoning levels: single-image reasoning, multi-image comparison, and global visual search. Unlike prior methods that rely on model-specific artifacts, S2H-DPO constructs preference pairs using prompt-driven complexity, ensuring cross-model applicability. Experimental results on LLaVA and Qwen-VL show that S2H-DPO significantly improves multi-image reasoning performance across benchmarks while preserving or even enhancing single-image capabilities, demonstrating its effectiveness in holistic visual preference alignment.
为解决视觉语言模型在多图像推理中的能力不足，本文提出S2H-DPO框架，通过构建三级递进式多图像偏好数据（单图局部推理、多图局部比较、全局视觉搜索）系统提升模型能力。与依赖模型特定特征的方法不同，该框架通过提示驱动的复杂度生成跨模型适用的偏好对。实验表明，该方法在LLaVA和Qwen-VL模型上显著提升多图像推理性能，同时保持甚至增强单图推理能力，推动了视觉偏好对齐的前沿进展。
Different Paths to Harmful Compliance: Behavioral Side Effects and Mechanistic Divergence Across LLM Jailbreaks
Authors: Md Rysul Kabir, Zoran Tiganj
First: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-weight language models can be rendered unsafe through several distinct interventions, but the resulting models may differ substantially in capabilities, behavioral profile, and internal failure mode. We study behavioral and mechanistic properties of jailbroken models across three unsafe routes: harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-suppressing abliteration. All three routes achieve near-ceiling harmful compliance, but they diverge once we move beyond direct harmfulness. RLVR-jailbroken models show minimal degradation and preserve explicit harm recognition in a structured self-audit: they are able to identify harmful prompts and describe how a safe LLM should respond, yet they comply with the harmful request. With RLVR, harmful behavior is strongly suppressed by a reflective safety scaffold: when a harmful prompt is prepended with an instruction to reflect on safety standards, harmful behavior drops close to the baseline. Category-specific RLVR jailbreaks generalize broadly across harmfulness domains. Models jailbroken with SFT show the largest collapse in explicit safety judgments, the highest behavioral drift, and a substantial capability loss on standard benchmarks. Abliteration is family-dependent in both self-audit and response to a reflective safety scaffold. Mechanistic and repair analyses further separate the routes: abliteration is consistent with localized refusal-feature deletion, RLVR with preserved safety geometry but retargeted policy behavior, and SFT with broader distributed drift. Targeted repair partially recovers RLVR-jailbroken models, but has little effect on SFT-jailbroken models. Together, these results show that jailbreaks can produce vastly different properties despite similar harmfulness, with models jailbroken via RLVR showing remarkable similarity to the base model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通往有害合规的不同路径：大语言模型越狱行为副作用与机制差异
开放权重语言模型可通过多种不同干预手段变得不安全，但由此产生的模型在能力、行为特征和内部失效模式上可能差异显著。我们研究了三种不安全路径下越狱模型的行为与机制特性：有害监督微调（SFT）、带可验证奖励的有害强化学习（RLVR）以及拒绝抑制型消融。这三种路径均实现接近上限的有害合规，但在超越直接有害性后出现分化。RLVR越狱模型表现出最小性能退化，仍能保留结构化自我审计中的显式危害识别能力：它们能识别有害提示并描述安全大语言模型应如何回应，却仍遵从有害请求。在RLVR中，有害行为被反思性安全框架强烈抑制：当有害提示前添加“反思安全标准”指令时，有害行为降至基线水平附近。针对特定类别的RLVR越狱模型可广泛泛化至不同危害领域。SFT越狱模型在显式安全判断上退化最严重，行为漂移最大，且在标准基准测试中能力损失显著。消融方法在自我审计与对反思性安全框架的响应上均依赖模型家族。机制与修复分析进一步区分了各路径：消融对应局部拒绝特征删除，RLVR对应保留安全几何结构但重定向策略行为，SFT对应广泛分布漂移。针对性修复可部分恢复RLVR越狱模型，但对SFT越狱模型效果甚微。总体而言，这些结果表明，尽管有害性相似，越狱可产生截然不同的模型特性，其中通过RLVR越狱的模型与基线模型高度相似。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how different methods of jailbreaking open-weight language models—harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-suppressing abliteration—produce divergent behavioral and mechanistic outcomes despite achieving similar levels of harmful compliance. The researchers analyze self-audit capabilities, response to reflective safety prompts, and performance on safety benchmarks, finding that RLVR models retain explicit harm recognition and respond correctly to safety instructions, while SFT models suffer significant degradation in safety judgment and capability. Abliteration’s effects vary by model family and show localized refusal feature removal. Mechanistic analysis reveals distinct internal patterns: RLVR preserves safety geometry but redirects policy behavior, SFT causes broad distributed drift, and abliteration removes refusal features. Repair attempts successfully restore RLVR models but not SFT models, indicating that RLVR jailbreaks remain closest to the original model’s safety architecture.
该研究探讨了三种不同方式（有害监督微调SFT、有害强化学习带可验证奖励RLVR、拒绝抑制型删除abliteration）对开源语言模型进行越狱后产生的行为与机制差异，尽管三者均达到相似的有害合规水平。实验发现，RLVR模型保留了对有害内容的识别能力，并能响应安全指令，而SFT模型在安全判断和能力上出现显著退化。abliteration的效果因模型家族而异，表现为局部拒绝特征删除。机制分析显示：RLVR保留安全结构但重定向策略，SFT导致广泛分布漂移，abliteration则删除拒绝特征。针对性修复可恢复RLVR模型，但对SFT模型无效，表明RLVR越狱模型最接近原始模型行为。
Wasserstein-p Central Limit Theorem Rates: From Local Dependence to Markov Chains
Authors: Yixuan Zhang, Qiaomin Xie
First: 2026-01-13T03:25:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:00:57+00:00
Comments: ACM SIGMETRICS 2026. 73 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-asymptotic central limit theorem (CLT) rates play a central role in modern machine learning and operations research. In this paper, we study CLT rates for multivariate dependent data in Wasserstein-$p$ ($W_p$) distance, for general $p\ge 1$. We focus on two fundamental dependence structures that commonly arise in practice: locally dependent sequences and geometrically ergodic Markov chains. In both settings, we establish the first optimal $\mathcal O(n^{-1/2})$ rate in $W_1$, as well as the first $W_p$ ($p\ge 2$) CLT rates under mild moment assumptions, substantially improving the best previously known bounds in these dependent-data regimes. As an application of our optimal $W_1$ rate for locally dependent sequences, we further obtain the first optimal $W_1$-CLT rate for multivariate $U$-statistics. On the technical side, we derive a tractable auxiliary bound for $W_1$ Gaussian approximation errors that is well suited for studying dependent data. For Markov chains, we further prove that the regeneration time of the split chain associated with a geometrically ergodic chain has a geometric tail without assuming strong aperiodicity or other restrictive conditions. These tools may be of independent interests and enable our optimal $W_1$ rates and underpin our $W_p$ ($p\ge 2$) results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Wasserstein-p中心极限定理速率：从局部依赖到马尔可夫链
非渐近中心极限定理（CLT）速率在现代机器学习和运筹学中起着核心作用。本文研究了在Wasserstein-p（W_p）距离下，多元依赖数据的CLT速率，适用于一般p≥1。我们聚焦于实践中常见的两种基本依赖结构：局部依赖序列和几何遍历马尔可夫链。在两种情形下，我们首次建立了W_1距离下的最优O(n^{-1/2})速率，以及在温和矩假设下首次获得W_p（p≥2）的CLT速率，显著改进了此前在这些依赖数据情形下的最佳已知界。作为局部依赖序列最优W_1速率的应用，我们进一步获得了多元U统计量的首个最优W_1-CLT速率。 技术上，我们推导出适用于研究依赖数据的W_1高斯近似误差的可处理辅助界。对于马尔可夫链，我们进一步证明，与几何遍历链相关的分裂链的再生时间具有几何尾分布，无需假设强周期性或其他限制性条件。这些工具可能具有独立研究价值，并支撑了我们的最优W_1速率及W_p（p≥2）结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise non-asymptotic convergence rates in machine learning and operations research, this paper establishes optimal Wasserstein-$p$ central limit theorem rates for dependent multivariate data. It focuses on locally dependent sequences and geometrically ergodic Markov chains, deriving for the first time an $\mathcal O(n^{-1/2})$ rate in $W_1$ and new $W_p$ ($p\ge 2$) rates under mild moment conditions, significantly improving prior bounds. The method introduces a tractable auxiliary bound for $W_1$ Gaussian approximation errors and proves geometric tail properties for regeneration times in split chains, enabling the optimal rates. As an application, the paper obtains the first optimal $W_1$-CLT rate for multivariate $U$-statistics.
为满足机器学习与运筹学中对非渐近误差界的需求，本文首次为局部相关序列与几何遍历马尔可夫链建立了Wasserstein-p中心极限定理的最优速率。在W_1距离下获得O(n^{-1/2})的最优速率，并在W_p（p≥2）距离下首次在弱矩条件下给出速率，显著优于先前结果。方法上，引入了适用于依赖数据的W_1高斯近似误差的可计算辅助界，并证明了分裂链再生时间的几何尾性质，支撑了最优W_1速率及W_p结果。
MASS-RAG: Multi-Agent Synthesis Retrieval-Augmented Generation
Authors: Xingchen Xiao, Heyan Huang, Runheng Liu, Jincheng Xie
First: 2026-04-20T17:00:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:00:38+00:00
Comments: 19 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are widely used in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to incorporate external knowledge at inference time. However, when retrieved contexts are noisy, incomplete, or heterogeneous, a single generation process often struggles to reconcile evidence effectively. We propose \textbf{MASS-RAG}, a multi-agent synthesis approach to retrieval-augmented generation that structures evidence processing into multiple role-specialized agents. MASS-RAG applies distinct agents for evidence summarization, evidence extraction, and reasoning over retrieved documents, and combines their outputs through a dedicated synthesis stage to produce the final answer. This design exposes multiple intermediate evidence views, allowing the model to compare and integrate complementary information before answer generation. Experiments on four benchmarks show that MASS-RAG consistently improves performance over strong RAG baselines, particularly in settings where relevant evidence is distributed across retrieved contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MASS-RAG：多智能体合成检索增强生成
大语言模型（LLMs）广泛用于检索增强生成（RAG），以在推理时引入外部知识。然而，当检索到的上下文存在噪声、不完整或异质性时，单一生成过程往往难以有效协调证据。我们提出\textbf{MASS-RAG}，一种用于检索增强生成的多智能体合成方法，将证据处理结构化为多个角色专用智能体。MASS-RAG为证据摘要、证据提取和检索文档推理分别分配不同智能体，并通过专门的合成阶段整合其输出以生成最终答案。该设计暴露多个中间证据视图，使模型在答案生成前能够比较并整合互补信息。在四个基准测试上的实验表明，MASS-RAG在多个指标上持续优于强RAG基线方法，尤其在相关证据分散于多个检索上下文时表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that single-agent RAG systems struggle with noisy, incomplete, or heterogeneous retrieved evidence, MASS-RAG introduces a multi-agent framework where specialized agents handle evidence summarization, extraction, and reasoning before synthesizing a final answer. By decomposing evidence processing into distinct roles and combining their outputs through a dedicated synthesis stage, the model leverages multiple intermediate views to better integrate complementary information. Experimental results across four benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance gains over strong RAG baselines, especially when relevant evidence is scattered across retrieved documents.
为了解决单代理RAG系统在处理噪声、不完整或异构检索证据时表现不佳的问题，MASS-RAG提出了一种多代理框架，将证据摘要、抽取和推理分别分配给不同角色的代理，并通过专门的合成阶段整合输出生成最终答案。该设计使模型能够比较并融合互补信息。在四个基准测试中，实验结果表明其性能持续优于强RAG基线，尤其在相关证据分散于多个检索文档时表现更优。
Document-as-Image Representations Fall Short for Scientific Retrieval
Authors: Ghazal Khalighinejad, Raghuveer Thirukovalluru, Alexander H. Oh, Bhuwan Dhingra
First: 2026-04-20T17:00:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T17:00:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many recent document embedding models are trained on document-as-image representations, embedding rendered pages as images rather than the underlying source. Meanwhile, existing benchmarks for scientific document retrieval, such as ArXivQA and ViDoRe, treat documents as images of pages, implicitly favoring such representations. In this work, we argue that this paradigm is not well-suited for text-rich multimodal scientific documents, where critical evidence is distributed across structured sources, including text, tables, and figures. To study this setting, we introduce ArXivDoc, a new benchmark constructed from the underlying LaTeX sources of scientific papers. Unlike PDF or image-based representations, LaTeX provides direct access to structured elements (e.g., sections, tables, figures, equations), enabling controlled query construction grounded in specific evidence types. We systematically compare text-only, image-based, and multimodal representations across both single-vector and multi-vector retrieval models. Our results show that: (1) document-as-image representations are consistently suboptimal, especially as document length increases; (2) text-based representations are most effective, even for figure-based queries, by leveraging captions and surrounding context; and (3) interleaved text+image representations outperform document-as-image approaches without requiring specialized training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：文档作为图像的表示方法在科学文献检索中表现不佳
许多近期的文档嵌入模型是在文档作为图像的表示上训练的，即把渲染后的页面作为图像而非底层源码进行嵌入。同时，现有的科学文献检索基准（如ArXivQA和ViDoRe）也将文档视为页面图像，隐式地偏向此类表示。本文我们认为，这种范式不适合文本丰富的多模态科学文献，其中关键证据分散在结构化源中，包括文本、表格和图表。为研究此场景，我们引入了ArXivDoc，一个基于科学论文底层LaTeX源码构建的新基准。与PDF或图像表示不同，LaTeX可直接访问结构化元素（如章节、表格、图表、公式），从而支持基于特定证据类型的可控查询构建。我们系统性地比较了纯文本、图像和多模态表示在单向量和多向量检索模型中的表现。结果表明：(1) 文档作为图像的表示始终次优，尤其随着文档长度增加；(2) 基于文本的表示最有效，即使在图表查询中，也能通过图注和上下文环境实现；(3) 交错的文本+图像表示优于文档作为图像的方法，且无需特殊训练。
Summary / 总结
This study critiques the common practice of treating scientific documents as images for retrieval, arguing that such representations fail to capture the structured, text-rich nature of academic papers. The authors introduce ArXivDoc, a new benchmark built from LaTeX sources to enable precise, evidence-grounded queries across text, tables, and figures. They compare text-only, image-based, and multimodal models across single- and multi-vector retrieval setups and find that image-based methods consistently underperform, especially with longer documents; text-based models are most effective even for figure queries by leveraging captions and context; and interleaved text+image models outperform image-only approaches without needing specialized training.
该研究针对科学文献检索中文档作为图像表示的局限性，指出此类方法忽视了科学文档的结构化特性。作者构建了基于LaTeX源码的ArXivDoc基准，支持对文本、表格和图表等结构元素的精准查询。通过对比纯文本、图像及图文混合模型在单向量与多向量检索中的表现，结果表明图像表示在长文档中表现较差；纯文本模型即使面对图表查询也能通过标题和上下文取得最佳效果；而图文混合模型无需特殊训练即可超越图像表示。
GeoRC: A Benchmark for Geolocation Reasoning Chains
Authors: Mohit Talreja, Joshua Diao, Jim Thannikary James, Radu Casapu, Tejas Santanam, Ethan Mendes, Alan Ritter, Wei Xu, James Hays
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-29T05:18:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:58:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Language Models (VLMs) are good at recognizing the global location of a photograph -- their geolocation prediction accuracy rivals the best human experts. But many VLMs are startlingly bad at \textit{explaining} which image evidence led to their prediction, even when their location prediction is correct. In this paper, we introduce GeoRC, the first benchmark for geolocation reasoning chains sourced directly from Champion-tier GeoGuessr experts, including the reigning world champion. This benchmark consists of 800 ``ground truth'' reasoning chains across 500 query scenes from GeoGuessr maps, with expert chains addressing hundreds of different discriminative attributes, such as soil properties, architecture, and license plate shapes. We evaluate LLM-as-a-judge and VLM-as-a-judge strategies for scoring VLM-generated reasoning chains against our expert reasoning chains and find that Qwen 3 LLM-as-a-judge correlates best with human-expert scoring. Our benchmark reveals that while large, closed-source VLMs such as Gemini and GPT 5 rival human experts at predicting locations, they still lag behind human experts when it comes to producing auditable reasoning chains. Small open-weight VLMs such as Llama and Qwen catastrophically fail on our benchmark -- they perform only slightly better than a baseline in which an LLM hallucinates a reasoning chain with oracle knowledge of the photo location but \textit{no visual information at all}. We believe the gap between human experts and VLMs on this task points to VLM limitations at extracting fine-grained visual attributes from high resolution images. We open source our benchmark for the community to use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoRC：地理定位推理链基准
视觉语言模型（VLMs）擅长识别照片的全球位置，其地理定位预测准确率可媲美顶尖人类专家。但许多VLMs在解释其预测所依据的图像证据时表现惊人地差，即使其位置预测正确。本文引入GeoRC，这是首个直接源自GeoGuessr冠军级专家（包括现任世界冠军）的地理定位推理链基准。该基准包含500个GeoGuessr地图查询场景的800条“真实”推理链，专家推理链涵盖数百种不同判别性属性，如土壤特性、建筑风格和车牌形状。我们评估了LLM作为裁判和VLM作为裁判两种策略，用于评分VLM生成的推理链与专家推理链的对比，发现Qwen 3作为LLM裁判与人类专家评分的相关性最佳。我们的基准表明，尽管大型闭源VLM（如Gemini和GPT 5）在预测位置上可媲美人类专家，但在生成可审计的推理链方面仍落后于人类专家。小型开源VLM（如Llama和Qwen）在本基准上表现灾难性失败——其表现仅略优于一个基线模型，该模型由LLM凭对照片位置的“神谕”知识虚构推理链，但完全不依赖任何视觉信息。我们认为，人类专家与VLM在该任务上的差距揭示了VLM在从高分辨率图像中提取细粒度视觉属性方面的局限性。我们开源本基准供社区使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that Vision Language Models (VLMs) often fail to explain their geolocation predictions despite high accuracy, this paper introduces GeoRC, the first benchmark of reasoning chains derived from top-tier GeoGuessr experts. The benchmark comprises 800 ground-truth chains across 500 scenes, detailing discriminative visual cues like soil, architecture, and license plates. Evaluating VLM-generated chains against expert chains using LLM-as-a-judge and VLM-as-a-judge scoring, the study finds Qwen 3 correlates best with human judgment. Results show that large closed-source VLMs like Gemini and GPT-5 match human geolocation accuracy but lag in producing auditable reasoning, while small open-weight models like Llama and Qwen perform poorly — barely better than an LLM hallucinating chains with location knowledge but no visual input, indicating VLMs struggle with fine-grained visual attribute extraction.
鉴于视觉语言模型（VLM）虽能准确预测图像地理位置，却常无法解释其推理依据，本文提出GeoRC基准，首次从顶级GeoGuessr专家（含世界冠军）处收集800条真实推理链，覆盖500个场景，涵盖土壤、建筑、车牌等细粒度视觉特征。评估VLM生成推理链与专家链的对比，发现Qwen 3作为评分者与人类评分相关性最高。实验表明，大型闭源模型如Gemini和GPT-5虽在定位预测上媲美人类专家，但在生成可审计推理链方面仍落后；小型开源模型如Llama和Qwen表现灾难性，仅略优于仅凭地点知识虚构推理链的基线，揭示VLM在从高分辨率图像中提取细粒度视觉属性方面存在局限。
Learning the Riccati solution operator for time-varying LQR via Deep Operator Networks
Authors: Jun Chen, Umberto Biccari, Junmin Wang
First: 2026-04-20T16:56:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:56:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a computational framework for replacing the repeated numerical solution of differential Riccati equations in finite-horizon Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) problems by a learned operator surrogate. Instead of solving a nonlinear matrix-valued differential equation for each new system instance, we construct offline an approximation of the associated solution operator mapping time-dependent system parameters to the Riccati trajectory. The resulting model enables fast online evaluation of approximate optimal feedbacks across a wide class of systems, thereby shifting the computational burden from repeated numerical integration to a one-time learning stage. From a theoretical perspective, we establish control-theoretic guarantees for this operator-based approximation. In particular, we derive bounds quantifying how operator approximation errors propagate to feedback performance, trajectory accuracy, and cost suboptimality, and we prove that exponential stability of the closed-loop system is preserved under sufficiently accurate operator approximation. These results provide a framework to assess the reliability of data-driven approximations in optimal control. On the computational side, we design tailored DeepONet architectures for matrix-valued, time-dependent problems and introduce a progressive learning strategy to address scalability with respect to the system dimension. Numerical experiments on both time-invariant and time-varying LQR problems demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves high accuracy and strong generalization across a wide range of system configurations, while delivering substantial computational speedups compared to classical solvers. The method offers an effective and scalable alternative for parametric and real-time optimal control applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过深度算子网络学习时变LQR的里卡提解算子
我们提出了一种计算框架，用学习到的算子代理替代有限时域线性二次调节器（LQR）问题中重复求解微分里卡提方程的数值方法。无需为每个新系统实例求解非线性矩阵微分方程，我们离线构建一个将时变系统参数映射到里卡提轨迹的解算子近似模型。该模型可在广泛系统中快速在线评估近似最优反馈，从而将计算负担从重复数值积分转移到一次学习阶段。从理论角度，我们为该算子近似建立了控制理论保证：推导了算子近似误差如何传播至反馈性能、轨迹精度和成本次优性的界，并证明在算子近似足够精确时闭环系统的指数稳定性得以保持。这些结果为评估数据驱动近似在最优控制中的可靠性提供了框架。计算方面，我们为矩阵值、时变问题设计了定制化的DeepONet架构，并引入渐进式学习策略以应对系统维度扩展的可扩展性问题。在时不变和时变LQR问题上的数值实验表明，该方法在多种系统配置下实现高精度和强泛化能力，同时相比经典求解器显著提升计算效率。该方法为参数化和实时最优控制应用提供了一种有效且可扩展的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of repeatedly solving differential Riccati equations in time-varying LQR problems, this work proposes a deep operator network to approximate the solution operator mapping system parameters to Riccati trajectories. The method constructs an offline surrogate model using tailored DeepONet architectures and a progressive learning strategy to handle high-dimensional systems, enabling fast online evaluation of optimal feedbacks. Experimental results show the approach achieves high accuracy and strong generalization across diverse system configurations while delivering significant speedups over classical numerical solvers, with theoretical guarantees ensuring stability and performance bounds under sufficient approximation accuracy.
为解决时间变化LQR问题中反复求解微分Riccati方程的计算效率低下问题，本文提出利用Deep Operator Networks学习一个替代算子，将时变系统参数映射到Riccati轨迹。该方法在离线阶段构建算子近似，实现在线快速评估最优反馈，无需重复数值积分。实验表明，该方法在多种系统配置下具有高精度和强泛化能力，相比传统求解器显著加速计算，且理论分析确保在算子近似足够精确时闭环系统保持指数稳定性及性能有界。
Mammo-FM: Breast-specific foundational model for Integrated Mammographic Diagnosis, Prognosis, and Reporting
Authors: Shantanu Ghosh, Vedant Parthesh Joshi, Rayan Syed, Aya Kassem, Abhishek Varshney, Payel Basak, Weicheng Dai, Judy Wawira Gichoya, Hari M. Trivedi, Imon Banerjee, Shyam Visweswaran, Clare B. Poynton, Kayhan Batmanghelich
First: 2025-11-28T20:41:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:56:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Breast cancer is one of the leading causes of death among women worldwide. We introduce Mammo-FM, the first foundation model specifically for mammography, pretrained on the largest and most diverse dataset to date - 140,677 patients (821,326 mammograms) across four U.S. institutions. Mammo-FM provides a unified foundation for core clinical tasks in breast imaging, including cancer diagnosis, pathology localization, structured report generation, and cancer risk prognosis within a single framework. Its alignment between images and text enables both visual and textual interpretability, improving transparency and clinical auditability, which are essential for real-world adoption. We rigorously evaluate Mammo-FM across diagnosis, prognosis, and report-generation tasks in in- and out-of-distribution datasets. Despite operating on native-resolution mammograms and using only one-third of the parameters of state-of-the-art generalist FMs, Mammo-FM consistently outperforms them across multiple public and private benchmarks. These results highlight the efficiency and value of domain-specific foundation models designed around the full spectrum of tasks within a clinical domain and emphasize the importance of rigorous, domain-aligned evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Mammo-FM：用于整合乳腺X线诊断、预后与报告的乳腺专用基础模型
乳腺癌是全球女性死亡的主要原因之一。我们引入了Mammo-FM，这是首个专为乳腺X线摄影设计的基础模型，其预训练数据为迄今为止最大且最多样化的数据集——来自美国四家机构的140,677名患者（821,326张乳腺X线图像）。Mammo-FM为乳腺影像的核心临床任务提供统一的基础框架，包括癌症诊断、病理定位、结构化报告生成及癌症风险预后。其图像与文本的对齐特性实现了视觉与文本的可解释性，提升了透明度和临床可审计性，这对于实际应用至关重要。我们在分布内与分布外数据集上对Mammo-FM在诊断、预后和报告生成任务中进行了严格评估。尽管其仅使用原分辨率乳腺X线图像，且参数量仅为当前最先进通用基础模型的三分之一，Mammo-FM在多个公开与私有基准测试中持续优于后者。这些结果凸显了围绕临床领域内全任务谱设计的专用基础模型的效率与价值，并强调了严谨、领域对齐评估的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for more accurate and interpretable breast cancer diagnosis tools, Mammo-FM is a breast-specific foundational model pretrained on a large, diverse dataset of 821,326 mammograms from 140,677 patients across four U.S. institutions. It integrates multiple clinical tasks—cancer detection, pathology localization, structured report generation, and risk prognosis—within a unified framework, leveraging image-text alignment for enhanced interpretability. Evaluated across in- and out-of-distribution datasets, Mammo-FM consistently outperforms generalist foundation models despite using only one-third of their parameters and operating on native-resolution images, demonstrating superior efficiency and clinical utility for breast imaging tasks.
为提升乳腺癌诊断与预后的准确性和可解释性，Mammo-FM 是首个专为乳腺X线摄影设计的基础模型，基于来自四家美国机构的140,677名患者（821,326张影像）进行预训练。该模型统一支持癌症检测、病灶定位、结构化报告生成和风险预测等核心临床任务，并通过图像与文本对齐实现视觉与文本双重可解释性。在多组分布内与分布外数据集上的严格评估中，Mammo-FM 在多个公开与私有基准测试中持续优于通用基础模型，且参数量仅为顶尖通用模型的三分之一，证明了其在临床场景中的高效性与任务针对性优势。
SpatialStack: Layered Geometry-Language Fusion for 3D VLM Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Jiang Zhang, Shijie Zhou, Bangya Liu, Achuta Kadambi, Zhiwen Fan
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-28T22:49:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:53:57+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026, Project Website: https://spatial-stack.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large vision-language models (VLMs) still struggle with reliable 3D spatial reasoning, a core capability for embodied and physical AI systems. This limitation arises from their inability to capture fine-grained 3D geometry and spatial relationships. While recent efforts have introduced multi-view geometry transformers into VLMs, they typically fuse only the deep-layer features from vision and geometry encoders, discarding rich hierarchical signals and creating a fundamental bottleneck for spatial understanding. To overcome this, we propose SpatialStack, a general hierarchical fusion framework that progressively aligns vision, geometry, and language representations across the model hierarchy. Moving beyond conventional late-stage vision-geometry fusion, SpatialStack stacks and synchronizes multi-level geometric features with the language backbone, enabling the model to capture both local geometric precision and global contextual semantics. Building upon this framework, we develop VLM-SpatialStack, a model that achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple 3D spatial reasoning benchmarks. Extensive experiments and ablations demonstrate that our multi-level fusion strategy consistently enhances 3D understanding and generalizes robustly across diverse spatial reasoning tasks, establishing SpatialStack as an effective and extensible design paradigm for vision-language-geometry integration in next-generation multimodal physical AI systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpatialStack：用于3D视觉语言模型空间推理的分层几何-语言融合
大型视觉语言模型（VLMs）在可靠的3D空间推理方面仍存在困难，这是具身和物理AI系统的核心能力。这一局限源于其无法捕捉精细的3D几何结构和空间关系。尽管近期研究已将多视角几何变换器引入VLMs，但它们通常仅融合视觉和几何编码器的深层特征，丢弃了丰富的层次化信号，从而形成了空间理解的根本瓶颈。为解决此问题，我们提出SpatialStack，一种通用的层次化融合框架，该框架在模型层次结构中逐步对齐视觉、几何和语言表示。超越传统的后期视觉-几何融合，SpatialStack将多层级几何特征堆叠并同步至语言主干，使模型能够同时捕捉局部几何精度和全局上下文语义。基于此框架，我们开发了VLM-SpatialStack模型，在多个3D空间推理基准测试中达到最先进性能。大量实验和消融研究证明，我们的多层级融合策略持续增强3D理解能力，并在多样化的空间推理任务中表现出稳健的泛化能力，确立了SpatialStack作为下一代多模态物理AI系统中视觉-语言-几何集成的有效且可扩展的设计范式。
Summary / 总结
SpatialStack addresses the limitation of large vision-language models in 3D spatial reasoning by introducing a hierarchical fusion framework that aligns vision, geometry, and language representations across multiple model layers. Unlike prior methods that fuse only deep features, SpatialStack stacks and synchronizes multi-level geometric signals with the language backbone, enabling the model to capture both fine-grained geometric details and global contextual semantics. Experiments show that this approach significantly improves performance on multiple 3D spatial reasoning benchmarks and demonstrates strong generalization across diverse tasks, establishing SpatialStack as a scalable and effective paradigm for multimodal physical AI systems.
为解决大视觉-语言模型在3D空间推理中的核心瓶颈，本文提出SpatialStack框架，通过在模型多层级上逐步对齐视觉、几何与语言表示，突破传统仅融合深层特征的局限。该方法将多级几何特征与语言主干同步堆叠，使模型同时捕捉局部几何精度与全局语义上下文。实验表明，基于该框架的VLM-SpatialStack在多个3D空间推理基准上达到最先进性能，消融实验验证多级融合策略持续提升3D理解能力并具备跨任务泛化能力。
ResearchBench: Benchmarking LLMs in Scientific Discovery via Inspiration-Based Task Decomposition
Authors: Yujie Liu, Zonglin Yang, Tong Xie, Jinjie Ni, Ben Gao, Yuqiang Li, Shixiang Tang, Wanli Ouyang, Erik Cambria, Dongzhan Zhou
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-03-27T08:09:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:53:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 2026 (findings)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have shown potential in assisting scientific research, yet their ability to discover high-quality research hypotheses remains unexamined due to the lack of a dedicated benchmark. To address this gap, we introduce the first large-scale benchmark for evaluating LLMs on a sufficient set of scientific discovery sub-tasks-inspiration retrieval, hypothesis composition, and hypothesis ranking-where sufficient means that perfectly solving these sub-tasks perfectly solves the overall discovery task. We develop an automated LLM-based framework that extracts critical components-research questions, background surveys, inspirations, and hypotheses-from papers across 12 disciplines, with expert validation confirming its accuracy. To prevent data contamination, we focus exclusively on publications from 2024 onward, ensuring minimal overlap with LLM pretraining data; our automated framework further enables automatic extraction of even more recent papers as LLM pretraining cutoffs advance, supporting scalable and contamination-free automatic renewal of this discovery benchmark. Our evaluation shows that, across disciplines, LLMs excel at inspiration retrieval-an out-of-distribution task-suggesting their ability to surface novel knowledge associations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ResearchBench：通过灵感驱动的任务分解对大语言模型在科学发现中的能力进行基准测试
大语言模型（LLMs）在辅助科学研究方面展现出潜力，但其发现高质量研究假设的能力尚未经过专门基准的检验。为填补这一空白，我们首次提出一个大规模基准，用于评估LLMs在科学发现的若干子任务上的表现——灵感检索、假设生成与假设排序，这些子任务的充分性意味着完美解决它们即等同于完美解决整体发现任务。我们开发了一个基于LLMs的自动化框架，从12个学科的论文中提取关键要素——研究问题、背景综述、灵感与假设，并通过专家验证确认其准确性。为防止数据污染，我们仅聚焦2024年及以后的出版物，确保与LLM预训练数据的最小重叠；随着LLM预训练截止时间的推移，我们的自动化框架还可自动提取更近期的论文，支持该发现基准的可扩展且无污染的自动更新。评估结果表明，LLMs在跨学科领域中擅长灵感检索——一项分布外任务——表明其能够揭示新颖的知识关联。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLMs' capacity to generate scientific hypotheses, this study introduces ResearchBench, the first large-scale benchmark designed to assess LLM performance across three core sub-tasks: inspiration retrieval, hypothesis composition, and hypothesis ranking. The method involves an automated LLM-driven framework that extracts research questions, background, inspirations, and hypotheses from 2024 and later publications across 12 disciplines, validated by experts to ensure accuracy and avoid contamination from pretraining data. Experimental results show that LLMs perform exceptionally well on inspiration retrieval—an out-of-distribution task—demonstrating their ability to uncover novel knowledge connections, while their performance on hypothesis composition and ranking remains more variable across disciplines.
为评估大语言模型在生成高质量科学假设方面的能力，本研究提出ResearchBench，这是首个将科学发现任务分解为灵感检索、假设生成与假设排序三个子任务的大规模基准。方法通过基于LLM的自动化框架，从2024年后的12个学科文献中提取研究要素（如研究问题、背景、灵感与假设），经专家验证确保准确性，并通过限定数据发布年份避免预训练数据污染。实验结果表明，LLM在灵感检索（一项分布外任务）上表现突出，显示出其发现新颖知识关联的能力，而在假设生成与排序任务上表现则因学科而异。
Plausibility as Commonsense Reasoning: Humans Succeed, Large Language Models Do not
Authors: Sercan Karakaş
First: 2026-04-06T16:27:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:43:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted to The Workshop on Cognitive Modeling and Computational Linguistics co-located with LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models achieve strong performance on many language tasks, yet it remains unclear whether they integrate world knowledge with syntactic structure in a human-like, structure-sensitive way during ambiguity resolution. We test this question in Turkish prenominal relative-clause attachment ambiguities, where the same surface string permits high attachment (HA) or low attachment (LA). We construct ambiguous items that keep the syntactic configuration fixed and ensure both parses remain pragmatically possible, while graded event plausibility selectively favors High Attachment vs.\ Low Attachment. The contrasts are validated with independent norming ratings. In a speeded forced-choice comprehension experiment, humans show a large, correctly directed plausibility effect. We then evaluate Turkish and multilingual LLMs in a parallel preference-based setup that compares matched HA/LA continuations via mean per-token log-probability. Across models, plausibility-driven shifts are weak, unstable, or reversed. The results suggest that, in the tested models, plausibility information does not guide attachment preferences as reliably as it does in human judgments, and they highlight Turkish RC attachment as a useful cross-linguistic diagnostic beyond broad benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可接受性作为常识推理：人类成功，大语言模型未能成功
大语言模型在许多语言任务中表现出色，但尚不清楚它们在解决歧义时是否像人类一样，以结构敏感的方式整合世界知识与句法结构。我们在土耳其语的前置定语从句依附歧义中测试了这一问题，其中相同的表层字符串允许高依附（HA）或低依附（LA）。我们构建了歧义项目，保持句法结构不变，确保两种解析在语用上皆可能，而事件可接受性则有选择性地偏向高依附或低依附。这些对比通过独立的标准化评分得到验证。在一项快速强制选择理解实验中，人类表现出显著且正确的可接受性效应。随后，我们在平行的偏好型设置中评估了土耳其语和多语言大语言模型，通过平均每词对数概率比较匹配的HA/LA延续部分。在所有模型中，可接受性驱动的偏移均较弱、不稳定或相反。结果表明，在所测试的模型中，可接受性信息未能像人类判断那样可靠地引导依附偏好，同时突显了土耳其语RC依附作为超越广泛基准的跨语言诊断工具的价值。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) integrate world knowledge with syntactic structure during ambiguity resolution, as humans do. Using Turkish prenominal relative-clause attachment ambiguities, the researchers designed stimuli where both syntactic parses are pragmatically viable but differ in event plausibility, with high attachment (HA) being more plausible. Human participants in a speeded forced-choice task showed a strong, consistent preference for HA over LA, demonstrating that plausibility guides their parsing. In contrast, Turkish and multilingual LLMs, evaluated via preference-based log-probability comparisons, exhibited weak, unstable, or even reversed plausibility effects, indicating that these models do not reliably use plausibility to resolve syntactic ambiguity like humans do.
该研究探讨大型语言模型是否像人类一样在句法歧义解析中结合常识合理性进行推理，聚焦土耳其语前置关系从句的依附歧义。研究人员设计了控制性语言刺激，使高依附（HA）和低依附（LA）两种句法结构在语用上皆可行，但事件合理性不同，并通过独立的人类评分验证。在快速选择理解任务中，人类显著偏好更合理的结构；而当对土耳其语及多语言LLM进行基于偏好、通过每词对数概率比较HA/LA延续结构的评估时，模型表现出弱、不稳定或反向的合理性效应，表明其未能像人类那样以结构敏感的方式整合合理性信息。
Too Correct to Learn: Reinforcement Learning on Saturated Reasoning Data
Authors: Zhenwen Liang, Yujun Zhou, Sidi Lu, Xiangliang Zhang, Haitao Mi, Dong Yu
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-20T16:43:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:43:28+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main Paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) enhances LLM reasoning, yet a paradox emerges as models scale: strong base models saturate standard benchmarks (e.g., MATH), yielding correct but homogeneous solutions. In such environments, the lack of failure cases causes the advantage signal in group-relative algorithms (e.g., GRPO) to vanish, driving policies into mode collapse. To address this, we propose Constrained Uniform Top-K Sampling (CUTS), a parameter-free decoding strategy enforcing structure-preserving exploration. Unlike standard sampling that follows model biases, CUTS flattens the local optimization landscape by sampling uniformly from constrained high-confidence candidates. We integrate this into Mixed-CUTS, a training framework synergizing exploitative and exploratory rollouts to amplify intra-group advantage variance. Experiments on Qwen3 models demonstrate that our approach prevents policy degeneration and significantly boosts out-of-domain generalization. Notably, Mixed-CUTS improves Pass@1 accuracy on the challenging AIME25 benchmark by up to 15.1% over standard GRPO, validating that maintaining diversity within the semantic manifold is critical for rigorous reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：过于正确而难以学习：在饱和推理数据上的强化学习
强化学习（RL）可增强大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力，但随着模型规模扩大，一个悖论显现：强基线模型在标准基准（如MATH）上趋于饱和，输出正确但同质化的解。在这样的环境中，缺乏失败案例导致基于群体相对优势的算法（如GRPO）的优势信号消失，使策略陷入模式坍缩。为解决此问题，我们提出无参数的解码策略——约束均匀Top-K采样（CUTS），强制保持结构探索。与遵循模型偏好的标准采样不同，CUTS通过从约束的高置信候选中均匀采样，平滑局部优化景观。我们将此方法整合进Mixed-CUTS训练框架，协同利用探索性与利用性 rollout 以放大群体内优势方差。在Qwen3模型上的实验表明，该方法可防止策略退化并显著提升跨域泛化能力。值得注意的是，Mixed-CUTS在具有挑战性的AIME25基准上，相较标准GRPO将Pass@1准确率提升高达15.1%，验证了在语义流形内保持多样性对严谨推理至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models saturate on reasoning benchmarks by producing homogeneous correct solutions, this work addresses the resulting mode collapse in reinforcement learning algorithms like GRPO, which lose advantage signals due to lack of failure cases. The authors introduce Constrained Uniform Top-K Sampling (CUTS), a parameter-free decoding method that enforces exploration by uniformly sampling from high-confidence, structure-preserving candidates, flattening the local optimization landscape. Integrated into Mixed-CUTS, a training framework that combines exploitative and exploratory rollouts, the method amplifies intra-group advantage variance. Experiments on Qwen3 show that CUTS prevents policy degeneration and enhances out-of-domain generalization, with Mixed-CUTS improving Pass@1 accuracy on AIME25 by up to 15.1% over standard GRPO.
鉴于大语言模型在推理基准上因输出同质化正确答案而饱和，该研究针对强化学习算法（如GRPO）因缺乏失败案例导致优势信号消失、策略退化的问题，提出无参数的约束均匀Top-K采样（CUTS）方法，通过从高置信度且结构保持的候选中均匀采样，平滑局部优化景观。该方法集成于Mixed-CUTS训练框架，结合探索与利用的 rollout 以增强组内优势方差。在Qwen3模型上的实验表明，CUTS可防止策略退化并提升跨域泛化能力，在挑战性AIME25基准上较标准GRPO提升Pass@1准确率最高达15.1%，验证了在语义流形内保持多样性对严谨推理的重要性。
Barrier-enforced multi-objective optimization for direct point and sharp interval forecasting
Authors: Worachit Amnuaypongsa, Yotsapat Suparanonrat, Pana Wanitchollakit, Jitkomut Songsiri
First: 2026-04-20T16:43:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:43:12+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 12 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a multi-step probabilistic forecasting framework using a single neural-network based model to generate simultaneous point and interval forecasts. Our approach ensures non-crossing prediction intervals (PIs) through a model structure design that strictly satisfy a target coverage probability (PICP) while maximizing sharpness. Unlike existing methods that rely on manual weight tuning for scalarized loss functions, we treat point and PI forecasting as a multi-objective optimization problem, utilizing multi-gradient descent to adaptively select optimal weights. Key innovations include a new PI loss function based on an extended log-barrier with an adaptive hyperparameter to guarantee the coverage, a hybrid architecture featuring a shared temporal model with horizon-specific submodels, and a training strategy. The proposed loss is scale-independent and universally applicable; combined with our training algorithm, the framework eliminates trial-and-error hyperparameter tuning for balancing multiple objectives. Validated by an intra-day solar irradiance forecasting application, results demonstrate that our proposed loss consistently outperforms those in current literature by achieving target coverage with the narrowest PI widths. Furthermore, when compared against LSTM encoder-decoder and Transformer architectures--including those augmented with Chronos foundation models--our method remains highly competitive and can be seamlessly adapted to any deep learning structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于屏障约束的多目标优化直接点预测与尖锐区间预测
本文提出了一种基于单个神经网络模型的多步概率预测框架，可同时生成点预测与区间预测。我们的方法通过模型结构设计确保预测区间不交叉，严格满足目标覆盖率（PICP）的同时最大化区间尖锐度。与现有依赖人工调整标量损失函数权重的方法不同，我们将点预测与区间预测视为多目标优化问题，采用多梯度下降法自适应选择最优权重。关键创新包括：基于扩展对数屏障的新型区间损失函数（含自适应超参数以保证覆盖率）、融合共享时序模型与分时段子模型的混合架构，以及配套的训练策略。所提损失函数无量纲且普适性强；结合训练算法，该框架消除了多目标平衡所需的试错式超参数调优。在日内太阳辐照度预测应用中验证，结果表明所提损失函数持续优于现有文献方法，实现目标覆盖率的同时获得最窄的预测区间宽度。此外，与LSTM编码器-解码器及Transformer架构（含Chronos基础模型增强）相比，本方法仍保持高度竞争力，且可无缝适配任意深度学习结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable and sharp probabilistic forecasts in solar irradiance, this paper introduces a barrier-enforced multi-objective optimization framework that simultaneously generates point and interval predictions using a single neural network. The method enforces non-crossing prediction intervals while maintaining target coverage probability via an extended log-barrier loss function with adaptive hyperparameters, and employs multi-gradient descent to dynamically balance objectives without manual tuning. Experimental results on intra-day solar forecasting show that the proposed loss consistently achieves the target coverage with the narrowest interval widths, outperforming existing methods, and remains competitive against LSTM and Transformer architectures—including those using Chronos foundation models—across diverse deep learning structures.
为提升太阳能辐照度预测的可靠性与精度，本文提出一种基于屏障约束的多目标优化框架，通过单一神经网络同时生成点预测与置信区间。该方法通过新型对数屏障损失函数与自适应超参数确保置信区间不交叉且满足目标覆盖率，并采用多梯度下降法自动平衡多个目标，无需人工调参。实验表明，该方法在日内太阳能预测任务中实现最窄的置信区间宽度同时严格满足覆盖率目标，优于现有方法，并在与LSTM、Transformer等架构（含Chronos增强模型）对比中保持高度竞争力。
Faster by Design: Interactive Aerodynamics via Neural Surrogates Trained on Expert-Validated CFD
Authors: Nicholas Thumiger, Andrea Bartezzaghi, Mattia Rigotti, Cezary Skura, Thomas Frick, Elisa Serioli, Fabrizio Arbucci, A. Cristiano I. Malossi
First: 2026-04-20T16:42:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:42:35+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) is central to race-car aerodynamic development, yet its cost -- tens of thousands of core-hours per high-fidelity evaluation -- severely limits the design space exploration feasible within realistic budgets. AI-based surrogate models promise to alleviate this bottleneck, but progress has been constrained by the limited complexity of public datasets, which are dominated by smoothed passenger-car shapes that fail to exercise surrogates on the thin, complex, highly loaded components governing motorsport performance. This work presents three primary contributions. First, we introduce a high-fidelity RANS dataset built on a parametric LMP2-class CAD model and spanning six operating conditions (map points) covering straight-line and cornering regimes, generated and validated by aerodynamics experts at Dallara to preserve features relevant to industrial motorsport. Second, we present the Gauge-Invariant Spectral Transformer (GIST), a graph-based neural operator whose spectral embeddings encode mesh connectivity to enhance predictions on tightly packed, complex geometries. GIST guarantees discretization invariance and scales linearly with mesh size, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy on both public benchmarks and the proposed race-car dataset. Third, we demonstrate that GIST achieves a level of predictive accuracy suitable for early-stage aerodynamic design, providing a first validation of the concept of interactive design-space exploration -- where engineers query a surrogate in place of the CFD solver -- within industrial motorsport workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：设计加速：基于专家验证CFD训练的神经代理实现交互式空气动力学
计算流体动力学（CFD）是赛车空气动力学开发的核心，但其成本——每次高保真评估需数万核心小时——严重限制了在现实预算下可探索的设计空间。基于AI的代理模型有望缓解这一瓶颈，但进展受限于公共数据集的复杂度不足，这些数据集主要由平滑的乘用车形状组成，无法有效测试代理模型在决定赛车性能的薄、复杂、高负载部件上的表现。本工作提出三项主要贡献：首先，我们构建了一个基于参数化LMP2级CAD模型、覆盖六种工况（映射点）——包括直线和弯道工况的高保真RANS数据集，由Dallara的空气动力学专家生成并验证，以保留与工业赛车相关的特征；其次，我们提出Gauge-Invariant Spectral Transformer（GIST），一种基于图的神经算子，其频谱嵌入编码网格连接性，以提升在紧密、复杂几何结构上的预测能力。GIST保证离散不变性，且随网格规模线性扩展，在公共基准和所提赛车数据集上均达到最先进精度；第三，我们证明GIST的预测精度足以支持早期空气动力学设计，首次在工业赛车工作流中验证了“交互式设计空间探索”概念——即工程师可直接查询代理模型替代CFD求解器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of CFD in motorsport aerodynamic design, this work introduces a high-fidelity RANS dataset based on a parametric LMP2 CAD model and expert-validated across six operating conditions. It proposes GIST, a graph-based neural operator using spectral embeddings to encode mesh connectivity, enabling accurate, discretization-invariant predictions on complex geometries while scaling linearly with mesh size. Experiments show GIST achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on both public benchmarks and the new race-car dataset, validating its use for interactive design-space exploration in industrial motorsport workflows.
为解决赛车空气动力学设计中CFD计算成本过高的问题，该研究构建了一个基于LMP2参数化CAD模型、经专家验证的高保真RANS数据集，并覆盖六种工况。提出GIST（Gauge-Invariant Spectral Transformer），一种基于图结构的神经算子，通过频谱嵌入编码网格连接性，实现离散不变性和网格规模线性扩展。实验表明，GIST在公开基准和新数据集上均达到最先进精度，验证了其在工业赛车设计流程中替代CFD进行交互式设计探索的可行性。
LQM: Linguistically Motivated Multidimensional Quality Metrics for Machine Translation
Authors: Samar M. Magdy, Fakhraddin Alwajih, Abdellah El Mekki, Wesam El-Sayed, Muhammad Abdul-Mageed
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-20T16:41:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:41:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026; resources available at https://github.com/UBC-NLP/LQM_MT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing MT evaluation frameworks, including automatic metrics and human evaluation schemes such as Multidimensional Quality Metrics (MQM), are largely language-agnostic. However, they often fail to capture dialect- and culture-specific errors in diglossic languages (e.g., Arabic), where translation failures stem from mismatches in language variety, content coverage, and pragmatic appropriateness rather than surface form alone.We introduce LQM: Linguistically Motivated Multidimensional Quality Metrics for MT. LQM is a hierarchical error taxonomy for diagnosing MT errors through six linguistically grounded levels: sociolinguistics, pragmatics, semantics, morphosyntax, orthography, and graphetics (Figure 1). We construct a bidirectional parallel corpus of 3,850 sentences (550 per variety) spanning seven Arabic dialects (Egyptian, Emirati, Jordanian, Mauritanian, Moroccan, Palestinian, and Yemeni), derived from conversational, culturally rich content. We evaluate six LLMs in a zero-shot setting and conduct expert span-level human annotation using LQM, producing 6,113 labeled error spans across 3,495 unique erroneous sentences, along with severity-weighted quality scores. We complement this analysis with an automatic metric (spBLEU). Though validated here on Arabic, LQM is a language-agnostic framework designed to be easily applied to or adapted for other languages. LQM annotated errors data, prompts, and annotation guidelines are publicly available at https://github.com/UBC-NLP/LQM_MT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LQM：面向机器翻译的语义驱动多维质量度量
现有机器翻译评估框架（包括自动指标和人类评估方案，如多维质量度量MQM）大多语言无关。然而，它们往往无法捕捉双语语言（如阿拉伯语）中因方言和文化差异导致的翻译错误，这些错误源于语言变体、内容覆盖和语用恰当性不匹配，而不仅限于表层形式。我们提出LQM：面向机器翻译的语义驱动多维质量度量。LQM是一种分层错误分类体系，通过六个语言学基础层面诊断MT错误：社会语言学、语用学、语义学、形态句法学、正字法和图形学（图1）。我们构建了一个包含3,850个句子（每种方言550句）的双向平行语料库，涵盖七种阿拉伯语方言（埃及、阿联酋、约旦、毛里塔尼亚、摩洛哥、巴勒斯坦、也门），源自对话式、文化丰富的文本。我们在零样本设置下评估了六种大语言模型，并通过LQM进行专家级跨度级人工标注，共标注6,113个错误片段，覆盖3,495个独特错误句子，并生成加权质量评分。我们还结合自动指标（spBLEU）进行补充分析。尽管本研究在阿拉伯语中验证，LQM是一个语言无关框架，易于应用于或适配其他语言。LQM标注错误数据、提示和标注指南已公开于https://github.com/UBC-NLP/LQM_MT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing machine translation evaluation methods in capturing dialect- and culture-specific errors in diglossic languages like Arabic, the authors propose LQM: a linguistically grounded multidimensional error taxonomy comprising six hierarchical levels—sociolinguistics, pragmatics, semantics, morphosyntax, orthography, and graphetics. Using a bidirectional parallel corpus of 3,850 sentences across seven Arabic dialects, they evaluate six large language models in zero-shot settings and annotate 6,113 error spans across 3,495 sentences with severity-weighted quality scores via expert human annotators. Their automatic spBLEU metric complements the human annotations, and while validated on Arabic, LQM is designed as a language-agnostic framework adaptable to other languages.
为解决现有机器翻译评估方法在处理如阿拉伯语等多语变体语言时难以捕捉方言与文化特异性错误的问题，作者提出LQM：一种基于语言学的多维错误分类体系，涵盖社会语言学、语用学、语义学、形态句法、正字法和图形学六个层级。基于七种阿拉伯方言的双向平行语料库（共3850句），他们在零样本设置下评估六种大语言模型，并由专家标注出3495个独特错误句子中的6113个错误片段，同时赋予严重性加权的质量评分。其自动评估指标spBLEU作为补充，而LQM框架本身设计为语言无关，便于扩展至其他语言。
Do LLMs Encode Functional Importance of Reasoning Tokens?
Authors: Janvijay Singh, Dilek Hakkani-Tür
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-01-06T14:50:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:41:34+00:00
Comments: Updated after ACL Main 2026 acceptance; 25 pages, 8 figures, 4 tables;
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models solve complex tasks by generating long reasoning chains, achieving higher accuracy at the cost of increased computational cost and reduced ability to isolate functionally relevant reasoning. Prior work on compact reasoning shortens such chains through probabilistic sampling, heuristics, or supervision from frontier models, but offers limited insight into whether models internally encode token-level functional importance for answer generation. We address this gap diagnostically and propose greedy pruning, a likelihood-preserving deletion procedure that iteratively removes reasoning tokens whose removal minimally degrades model likelihood under a specified objective, yielding length-controlled reasoning chains. We evaluate pruned reasoning in a distillation framework and show that students trained on pruned chains outperform a frontier-model-supervised compression baseline at matched reasoning lengths. Finally, our analysis reveals systematic pruning patterns and shows that attention scores can predict greedy pruning ranks, further suggesting that models encode a nontrivial functional importance structure over reasoning tokens.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型是否编码了推理令牌的功能重要性？
大语言模型通过生成长推理链解决复杂任务，以提高准确性为代价，增加了计算成本并降低了隔离功能相关推理的能力。先前关于紧凑推理的工作通过概率采样、启发式方法或前沿模型监督缩短推理链，但对模型内部是否编码了用于答案生成的令牌级功能重要性缺乏深入洞察。我们诊断性地填补这一空白，提出贪心剪枝方法——一种保留似然的删除过程，通过迭代移除在指定目标下移除后对模型似然影响最小的推理令牌，从而生成长度可控的推理链。我们在蒸馏框架中评估剪枝后的推理链，结果表明，使用剪枝链训练的学生模型在相同推理长度下优于前沿模型监督压缩基线。最后，我们的分析揭示了系统性的剪枝模式，并表明注意力分数可预测贪心剪枝排名，进一步表明模型编码了推理令牌的非平凡功能重要性结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand whether large language models internally encode functional importance for reasoning tokens, this study introduces greedy pruning—a likelihood-preserving token deletion method that iteratively removes tokens whose removal least impacts model likelihood under a given objective, thereby generating length-controlled reasoning chains. Evaluated in a distillation framework, models trained on pruned chains outperformed those trained on chains compressed via frontier-model supervision at equivalent lengths. Analysis further revealed systematic pruning patterns and demonstrated that attention scores correlate with pruning ranks, indicating that models possess a nontrivial internal structure for token-level functional importance.
为探究大语言模型是否在内部编码推理链中各token的功能重要性，该研究提出贪心剪枝方法，通过迭代删除对模型输出影响最小的token，生成可控长度的推理链，同时保持输出似然。在蒸馏框架下评估发现，使用剪枝后推理链训练的学生模型在相同长度下优于基于前沿模型监督压缩的基线方法。分析还揭示了系统性的剪枝模式，并表明注意力分数可预测剪枝顺序，暗示模型内部存在非平凡的功能重要性结构。
Aligning Language Models for Lyric-to-Melody Generation with Rule-Based Musical Constraints
Authors: Hao Meng, Siyuan Zheng, Shuran Zhou, Qiangqiang Wang, Yang Song
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2026-04-20T16:40:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:40:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IEEE ICASSP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise in lyric-to-melody generation, but models trained with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) often produce musically implausible melodies with issues like poor rhythm and unsuitable vocal ranges, a phenomenon we term "constraint violation". To address this, we propose a novel alignment framework that instills musical knowledge without human annotation. We define rule-based musical constraints to automatically generate a preference dataset from an SFT model's outputs. The model is then aligned through a sequential process, first using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) on paired preference data, followed by Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (KTO) on unpaired negative samples. Experimental results demonstrate that our aligned model substantially reduces rule violations and outperforms strong baselines in both objective and subjective evaluations, generating melodies with substantially improved musicality and coherence. An interactive demo with audio comparisons is available at https://arain233.github.io/AligningMelody-demo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于规则化音乐约束的语言模型对歌词到旋律生成的对齐方法
大型语言模型（LLM）在歌词到旋律生成中展现出潜力，但通过监督微调（SFT）训练的模型常产生音乐上不合理的旋律，如节奏不佳、音域不适宜等问题，我们称之为“约束违反”。为解决此问题，我们提出一种新颖的对齐框架，无需人工标注即可注入音乐知识。我们定义基于规则的音乐约束，自动从SFT模型输出中生成偏好数据集。模型随后通过分阶段对齐：首先在成对偏好数据上使用直接偏好优化（DPO），再在未配对负样本上使用卡尼曼-特弗斯基优化（KTO）。实验结果表明，我们的对齐模型显著减少规则违反，并在客观与主观评估中均优于强基线模型，生成的旋律在音乐性与连贯性上大幅提升。交互式演示及音频对比可访问：https://arain233.github.io/AligningMelody-demo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the musical implausibility in lyric-to-melody generation from standard supervised fine-tuning, this work introduces a rule-based alignment framework that embeds musical constraints without human annotation. It automatically generates preference data from SFT outputs and aligns the model via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) followed by Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (KTO) on unpaired negative samples. Experiments show the aligned model significantly reduces musical violations and outperforms baselines in both objective metrics and subjective assessments, producing more coherent and musically appropriate melodies.
为解决大语言模型在歌词转旋律任务中因监督微调导致的音乐不合理问题，本文提出一种基于规则的对齐框架，无需人工标注即可融入音乐知识。方法通过自动构建偏好数据并依次应用直接偏好优化（DPO）与卡尼曼-特弗斯基优化（KTO）对模型进行对齐。实验表明，对齐后的模型显著减少音乐规则违反，且在客观指标与主观听感评估中均优于基线模型，生成的旋律更具音乐性和节奏准确性。
Test-Time Reasoners Are Strategic Multiple-Choice Test-Takers
Authors: Nishant Balepur, Atrey Desai, Rachel Rudinger
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-10-09T04:00:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:38:40+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) now give reasoning before answering, excelling in tasks like multiple-choice question answering (MCQA). Yet, a concern is that LLMs do not solve MCQs as intended, as work finds LLMs sans reasoning succeed in MCQA without using the question, i.e., choices-only. Such partial-input success is often linked to trivial shortcuts, but reasoning traces could reveal if choices-only strategies are truly shallow. To examine these strategies, we have reasoning LLMs solve MCQs in full and choices-only inputs; test-time reasoning often boosts accuracy in full and in choices-only, half the time. While possibly due to shallow shortcuts, choices-only success is barely affected by the length of reasoning traces, and after finding traces pass faithfulness tests, we show they use less problematic strategies like inferring missing questions. In all, we challenge claims that partial-input success is always a flaw, so we propose how reasoning traces could separate problematic data from less problematic reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：测试时推理者是策略性的多项选择题应试者
大型语言模型（LLMs）现在在回答前会进行推理，在多项选择题（MCQA）等任务中表现优异。然而，人们担忧LLMs并未按预期解决MCQ，因为研究发现，不进行推理的LLMs也能在不使用题目、仅依赖选项的情况下完成MCQA。这种部分输入的成功常与简单捷径相关，但推理轨迹可能揭示仅依赖选项的策略是否真正肤浅。为检验这些策略，我们让推理LLMs在完整输入和仅选项输入下解决MCQ；测试时推理通常能提升完整输入和仅选项输入的准确率，约一半时间。尽管可能源于浅层捷径，仅选项输入的成功几乎不受推理轨迹长度影响，且在发现轨迹通过可信度测试后，我们表明其使用了更少问题的策略，如推断缺失题目。总体而言，我们质疑部分输入成功总是缺陷的说法，并提出推理轨迹可用于区分问题数据与较不问题的推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that large language models may solve multiple-choice questions via shallow shortcuts by ignoring the question text, this study investigates whether test-time reasoning reveals deeper strategic behavior. The authors compare reasoning-enabled LLMs solving MCQs with full inputs versus choices-only inputs, finding that test-time reasoning often improves accuracy in both settings. Crucially, success on choices-only tasks is not correlated with reasoning trace length or faithfulness, and instead correlates with strategies like inferring missing questions, suggesting these successes are not trivial. The findings challenge the assumption that partial-input success is inherently flawed and propose reasoning traces as a tool to distinguish between problematic and less problematic reasoning patterns.
鉴于大型语言模型可能通过捷径而非真正推理在多项选择题中取得成功，本研究探讨测试时推理是否能揭示更深层的策略。作者对比了启用推理的模型在完整输入与仅选项输入下解题的表现，发现测试时推理在两种情况下均能提升准确率。关键发现是，仅选项输入的成功与推理轨迹长度或忠实度无关，而是体现为更精细的策略，如推断缺失的问题。这些结果挑战了部分输入成功必然是缺陷的假设，并提出推理轨迹可用于区分表面化与实质性推理。
TOOLCAD: Exploring Tool-Using Large Language Models in Text-to-CAD Generation with Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yifei Gong, Xing Wu, Wenda Liu, Kang Tu
First: 2026-04-09T08:22:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:37:31+00:00
Comments: ACL2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computer-Aided Design (CAD) is an expert-level task that relies on long-horizon reasoning and coherent modeling actions. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable advancements in enabling language agents to tackle real-world tasks. Notably, there has been no investigation into how tool-using LLMs optimally interact with CAD engines, hindering the emergence of LLM-based agentic text-to-CAD modeling systems. We propose ToolCAD, a novel agentic CAD framework deploying LLMs as tool-using agents for text-to-CAD generation. Furthermore, we introduce an interactive CAD modeling gym to rollout reasoning and tool-augmented interaction trajectories with the CAD engine, incorporating hybrid feedback and human supervision. Meanwhile, an end-to-end post-training strategy is presented to enable the LLM agent to elicit refined CAD Modeling Chain of Thought (CAD-CoT) and evolve into proficient CAD tool-using agents via online curriculum reinforcement learning. Our findings demonstrate ToolCAD fills the gap in adopting and training open-source LLMs for CAD tool-using agents, enabling them to perform comparably to proprietary models, paving the way for more accessible and robust autonomous text-to-CAD modeling systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TOOLCAD：利用强化学习探索工具型大语言模型在文本到CAD生成中的应用

The method involves an interactive CAD modeling gym that enables reasoning and tool-augmented interactions with the CAD engine, supported by hybrid feedback and human supervision. An end-to-end post-training strategy using online curriculum reinforcement learning refines the LLM’s CAD-CoT reasoning and evolves it into a proficient tool-using agent. Experimental results show ToolCAD successfully trains open-source LLMs to match the performance of proprietary models in text-to-CAD tasks, demonstrating its potential for scalable, autonomous CAD generation.
为填补大语言模型与CAD引擎交互机制研究的空白，作者提出ToolCAD框架，将LLM作为工具使用代理用于文本到CAD生成。该方法构建交互式CAD建模环境，结合混合反馈与人工监督，并采用端到端后训练策略与在线课程强化学习，优化LLM的CAD建模思维链。实验表明，ToolCAD使开源LLM可达到与专有模型相当的性能，为可扩展、自主的文本到CAD系统提供了可行路径。
Adversarial Humanities Benchmark: Results on Stylistic Robustness in Frontier Model Safety
Authors: Marcello Galisai, Susanna Cifani, Francesco Giarrusso, Piercosma Bisconti, Matteo Prandi, Federico Pierucci, Federico Sartore, Daniele Nardi
First: 2026-04-20T16:37:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:37:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Adversarial Humanities Benchmark (AHB) evaluates whether model safety refusals survive a shift away from familiar harmful prompt forms. Starting from harmful tasks drawn from MLCommons AILuminate, the benchmark rewrites the same objectives through humanities-style transformations while preserving intent. This extends literature on Adversarial Poetry and Adversarial Tales from single jailbreak operators to a broader benchmark family of stylistic obfuscation and goal concealment. In the benchmark results reported here, the original attacks record 3.84% attack success rate (ASR), while transformed methods range from 36.8% to 65.0%, yielding 55.75% overall ASR across 31 frontier models. Under a European Union AI Act Code-of-Practice-inspired systemic-risk lens, Chemical, biological, radiological and nuclear (CBRN) is the highest bucket. Taken together, this lack of stylistic robustness suggests that current safety techniques suffer from weak generalization: deep understanding of 'non-maleficence' remains a central unresolved problem in frontier model safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗性人文基准：前沿模型安全中的风格鲁棒性评估结果
对抗性人文基准（AHB）评估模型安全拒绝是否能在脱离熟悉有害提示形式后仍保持有效。该基准从MLCommons AILuminate中的有害任务出发，通过人文风格的改写重构相同目标，同时保留意图。这将对抗性诗歌与对抗性故事文献从单一越狱操作扩展为涵盖风格混淆与目标隐藏的更广泛基准家族。在本文报告的基准结果中，原始攻击的成功率为3.84%，而改写方法的成功率介于36.8%至65.0%之间，31个前沿模型的整体攻击成功率为55.75%。从受欧盟AI法案行为准则启发的系统性风险视角看，化学、生物、放射性与核（CBRN）风险等级最高。综合来看，这种风格鲁棒性的缺失表明当前安全技术存在泛化能力薄弱的问题：对‘不伤害’的深刻理解仍是前沿模型安全的核心未解难题。

为评估前沿模型的安全机制是否能抵御有害提示的风格化变体，Adversarial Humanities Benchmark（AHB）将MLCommons AILuminate中的原始有害任务转化为人文风格的改写版本，同时保留其意图。该方法系统性地重写提示以隐藏有害目标，测试模型是否仍会生成不安全输出。实验结果显示，原始攻击的成功率为3.84%，而风格化改写版本的成功率在36.8%至65.0%之间，31个模型的整体成功率为55.75%。在受欧盟AI法案风险评估框架启发的系统性风险视角下，CBRN类提示风险最高，表明当前安全机制对风格化伪装缺乏鲁棒性，且未能实现对“无害性”的深层泛化理解。
OneVL: One-Step Latent Reasoning and Planning with Vision-Language Explanation
Authors: Jinghui Lu, Jiayi Guan, Zhijian Huang, Jinlong Li, Guang Li, Lingdong Kong, Yingyan Li, Han Wang, Shaoqing Xu, Yuechen Luo, Fang Li, Chenxu Dang, Junli Wang, Tao Xu, Jing Wu, Jianhua Wu, Xiaoshuai Hao, Wen Zhang, Tianyi Jiang, Lingfeng Zhang, Lei Zhou, Yingbo Tang, Jie Wang, Yinfeng Gao, Xizhou Bu, Haochen Tian, Yihang Qiu, Feiyang Jia, Lin Liu, Yigu Ge, Hanbing Li, Yuannan Shen, Jianwei Cui, Hongwei Xie, Bing Wang, Haiyang Sun, Jingwei Zhao, Jiahui Huang, Pei Liu, Zeyu Zhu, Yuncheng Jiang, Zibin Guo, Chuhong Gong, Hanchao Leng, Kun Ma, Naiyang Wang, Guang Chen, Kuiyuan Yang, Hangjun Ye, Long Chen
First: 2026-04-20T16:37:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:37:22+00:00
Comments: Technical Report; 49 pages, 22 figures, 10 tables; Project Page at https://xiaomi-embodied-intelligence.github.io/OneVL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has become a powerful driver of trajectory prediction in VLA-based autonomous driving, yet its autoregressive nature imposes a latency cost that is prohibitive for real-time deployment. Latent CoT methods attempt to close this gap by compressing reasoning into continuous hidden states, but consistently fall short of their explicit counterparts. We suggest that this is due to purely linguistic latent representations compressing a symbolic abstraction of the world, rather than the causal dynamics that actually govern driving. Thus, we present OneVL (One-step latent reasoning and planning with Vision-Language explanations), a unified VLA and World Model framework that routes reasoning through compact latent tokens supervised by dual auxiliary decoders. Alongside a language decoder that reconstructs text CoT, we introduce a visual world model decoder that predicts future-frame tokens, forcing the latent space to internalize the causal dynamics of road geometry, agent motion, and environmental change. A three-stage training pipeline progressively aligns these latents with trajectory, language, and visual objectives, ensuring stable joint optimization. At inference, the auxiliary decoders are discarded and all latent tokens are prefilled in a single parallel pass, matching the speed of answer-only prediction. Across four benchmarks, OneVL becomes the first latent CoT method to surpass explicit CoT, delivering state-of-the-art accuracy at answer-only latency, and providing direct evidence that tighter compression, when guided in both language and world-model supervision, produces more generalizable representations than verbose token-by-token reasoning. Project Page: https://xiaomi-embodied-intelligence.github.io/OneVL
中文标题/摘要
标题：OneVL：一步式潜在推理与规划及视觉-语言解释
思维链（CoT）推理已成为基于视觉-语言模型的自动驾驶轨迹预测的强大驱动力，但其自回归特性带来了不可接受的延迟，阻碍了实时部署。潜在CoT方法试图通过将推理压缩到连续隐藏状态来弥合这一差距，但始终未能达到显式方法的效果。我们认为，这是由于纯语言型潜在表示仅压缩了对世界的符号抽象，而非实际支配驾驶的因果动态。因此，我们提出了OneVL（一步式潜在推理与规划及视觉-语言解释），一个统一的视觉-语言模型与世界模型框架，通过双辅助解码器监督的紧凑潜在标记路由推理。除重建文本CoT的语言解码器外，我们引入了预测未来帧标记的视觉世界模型解码器，迫使潜在空间内化道路几何、代理运动和环境变化的因果动态。三阶段训练流程逐步对齐轨迹、语言和视觉目标，确保联合优化稳定。推理时，辅助解码器被丢弃，所有潜在标记在单次并行前填充，匹配仅答案预测的速度。在四个基准测试中，OneVL成为首个超越显式CoT的潜在CoT方法，在仅答案延迟下实现最先进精度，并直接证明：在语言与世界模型监督引导下，更紧致的压缩比逐标记详细推理产生更泛化的表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the latency issues of autoregressive chain-of-thought reasoning in vision-language agents for autonomous driving, the authors propose OneVL, a unified framework that compresses reasoning into latent tokens while preserving causal dynamics through dual supervision. OneVL integrates a language decoder to reconstruct text-based reasoning and a visual world model decoder to predict future frames, forcing the latent space to encode road geometry, agent motion, and environmental changes. Trained via a three-stage pipeline aligning trajectory, language, and visual objectives, OneVL achieves state-of-the-art accuracy at answer-only latency across four benchmarks, outperforming prior latent CoT methods and demonstrating that tightly compressed representations, guided by both linguistic and visual supervision, generalize better than verbose token-by-token reasoning.
为解决视觉语言代理在自动驾驶中链式思维推理的延迟问题，作者提出OneVL框架，将推理压缩为潜变量令牌，同时保留因果动态。该框架结合视觉语言代理与世界模型，通过双辅助解码器联合监督潜变量——一个重建文本推理，另一个预测未来视觉令牌——以确保潜变量内化道路几何、代理运动与环境变化的因果动态。通过三阶段训练流程，模型逐步对齐轨迹、语言与视觉目标，实现稳定联合优化。推理时丢弃辅助解码器，潜变量一次性并行填充，达到仅答案输出的延迟。在四个基准测试中，OneVL首次超越所有先前的潜式链式思维方法，达到或超越显式链式思维的性能，证明在语言与世界模型双重监督下，紧致压缩的表示比逐令牌推理更具泛化性与效率。
XEmbodied: A Foundation Model with Enhanced Geometric and Physical Cues for Large-Scale Embodied Environments
Authors: Kangan Qian, ChuChu Xie, Yang Zhong, Jingrui Pang, Siwen Jiao, Sicong Jiang, Zilin Huang, Yunlong Wang, Kun Jiang, Mengmeng Yang, Hao Ye, Guanghao Zhang, Hangjun Ye, Guang Chen, Long Chen, Diange Yang
First: 2026-04-20T16:37:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:37:16+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models drive next-generation autonomous systems, but training them requires scalable, high-quality annotations from complex environments. Current cloud pipelines rely on generic vision-language models (VLMs) that lack geometric reasoning and domain semantics due to their 2D image-text pretraining. To address this mismatch, we propose XEmbodied, a cloud-side foundation model that endows VLMs with intrinsic 3D geometric awareness and interaction with physical cues (e.g., occupancy grids, 3D boxes). Instead of treating geometry as auxiliary input, XEmbodied integrates geometric representations via a structured 3D Adapter and distills physical signals into context tokens using an Efficient Image-Embodied Adapter. Through progressive domain curriculum and reinforcement learning post-training, XEmbodied preserves general capabilities while demonstrating robust performance across 18 public benchmarks. It significantly improves spatial reasoning, traffic semantics, embodied affordance, and out-of-distribution generalization for large-scale scenario mining and embodied VQA.
中文标题/摘要

视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型驱动下一代自主系统，但训练它们需要来自复杂环境的可扩展、高质量标注。当前云管道依赖于通用视觉-语言模型（VLMs），由于其2D图像-文本预训练，缺乏几何推理和领域语义。为解决这一不匹配，我们提出XEmbodied，一种云端基础模型，赋予VLMs内在的3D几何感知能力及与物理线索（如占用网格、3D框）的交互能力。XEmbodied不将几何视为辅助输入，而是通过结构化3D适配器整合几何表示，并利用高效图像-具身适配器将物理信号蒸馏为上下文标记。通过渐进式领域课程和训练后强化学习，XEmbodied在保持通用能力的同时，在18个公开基准上展现出稳健性能，显著提升空间推理、交通语义、具身可操作性及分布外泛化能力，适用于大规模场景挖掘和具身视觉问答。

Motivated by the need for VLA models to handle complex embodied environments, this paper introduces XEmbodied, a foundation model that enhances generic vision-language models with 3D geometric and physical reasoning. It achieves this by integrating structured 3D geometric representations via a 3D Adapter and distilling physical cues into context tokens using an Efficient Image-Embodied Adapter. Experimental results across 18 benchmarks show that XEmbodied significantly improves spatial reasoning, traffic semantics, embodied affordance, and out-of-distribution generalization, while preserving general VLM capabilities through progressive curriculum and reinforcement learning post-training.
为弥合2D视觉语言模型与具身环境所需的几何和物理特性之间的差距，XEmbodied提出了一种云端基础模型，通过结构化3D适配器整合3D几何表示，并利用高效图像-具身适配器将物理信号（如占据网格和3D框）提炼为上下文令牌。该模型在18个公开基准测试中展现出显著优势，尤其在空间推理、交通语义、具身可操作性及分布外泛化能力方面表现突出，同时通过渐进式课程与训练后强化学习保持了通用视觉语言能力。
BenchMarker: An Education-Inspired Toolkit for Highlighting Flaws in Multiple-Choice Benchmarks
Authors: Nishant Balepur, Bhavya Rajasekaran, Jane Oh, Michael Xie, Atrey Desai, Vipul Gupta, Steven James Moore, Eunsol Choi, Rachel Rudinger, Jordan Lee Boyd-Graber
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-02-05T21:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:34:51+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) is standard in NLP, but benchmarks lack rigorous quality control. We present BenchMarker, an education-inspired toolkit using LLM judges to flag three common MCQ flaws: 1) contamination: items appearing exactly online; 2) shortcuts: cues in the choices that enable guessing; and 3) writing errors: structural/grammatical issues based on a 19-rule education rubric. We validate BenchMarker with human annotations, then run the tool to audit 12 benchmarks, revealing: 1) flaws persist in MCQA benchmarks, especially automatically-made and crowdsourced data - we detect 47% of TruthfulQA appears online and 100% of HellaSwag violates multiple writing rules; 2) contaminated MCQs tend to inflate accuracy, while writing errors tend to lower it and change rankings beyond random; and 3) prior benchmark repairs address their targeted issues (i.e., lowering accuracy with LLM-written distractors), but inadvertently add new flaws (i.e. implausible distractors, many correct answers). Overall, flaws in MCQs degrade NLP evaluation, but education research offers a path forward. We release BenchMarker to bridge the fields and improve MCQA benchmark design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BenchMarker：一种受教育启发的工具包，用于揭示多项选择题基准中的缺陷
多项选择题回答（MCQA）是自然语言处理的标准任务，但现有基准缺乏严格的质量控制。我们提出了BenchMarker，一种受教育启发的工具包，利用大语言模型裁判识别三项常见MCQ缺陷：1）污染：题目内容与网络上完全相同；2）捷径：选项中包含可猜答案的线索；3）写作错误：基于19条教育评分标准的结构/语法问题。我们通过人工标注验证BenchMarker，随后用该工具审计12个基准，发现：1）MCQA基准中缺陷普遍存在，尤其在自动生成和众包数据中——我们发现47%的TruthfulQA题目在线上出现，100%的HellaSwag违反多项写作规则；2）污染题目倾向于虚高准确率，而写作错误则降低准确率并显著改变排名；3）先前基准修复措施虽解决特定问题（如用LLM生成干扰项降低准确率），却无意中引入新缺陷（如不合理的干扰项、多个正确答案）。总体而言，MCQ中的缺陷会损害NLP评估，但教育研究提供了改进路径。我们发布BenchMarker以弥合两个领域并提升MCQA基准设计。

Motivated by the lack of rigorous quality control in multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) benchmarks, the authors introduce BenchMarker, an education-inspired toolkit that uses large language models as judges to detect three common flaws: contamination (items appearing online), shortcuts (guessable choice cues), and writing errors (structural/grammatical issues based on a 19-rule rubric). Applied to 12 benchmarks, the tool revealed that 47% of TruthfulQA items were contaminated and 100% of HellaSwag violated writing rules; contaminated questions inflated accuracy while writing errors lowered it and distorted rankings; prior benchmark fixes often introduced new flaws. 鉴于多选题问答（MCQA）基准缺乏严格的质量控制，作者提出了BenchMarker工具，该工具借鉴教育理念，利用大语言模型作为裁判，检测三种常见缺陷：污染（题目内容直接来自网络）、捷径（选项中包含可猜答案的线索）和写作错误（依据19条教育评分标准的结构/语法问题）。在12个基准数据集上应用该工具发现：TruthfulQA中47%的题目存在污染，HellaSwag全部违反写作规则；污染题目会虚高准确率，写作错误则降低准确率并显著改变排名；此前的基准修复虽解决特定问题，却常引入新缺陷。研究指出，MCQA基准的系统性缺陷削弱了评估可靠性，而教育研究方法可为此提供改进路径。
Safe Control using Learned Safety Filters and Adaptive Conformal Inference
Authors: Sacha Huriot, Ihab Tabbara, Hussein Sibai
First: 2026-04-20T16:34:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:34:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted to L4DC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety filters have been shown to be effective tools to ensure the safety of control systems with unsafe nominal policies. To address scalability challenges in traditional synthesis methods, learning-based approaches have been proposed for designing safety filters for systems with high-dimensional state and control spaces. However, the inevitable errors in the decisions of these models raise concerns about their reliability and the safety guarantees they offer. This paper presents Adaptive Conformal Filtering (ACoFi), a method that combines learned Hamilton-Jacobi reachability-based safety filters with adaptive conformal inference. Under ACoFi, the filter dynamically adjusts its switching criteria based on the observed errors in its predictions of the safety of actions. The range of possible safety values of the nominal policy's output is used to quantify uncertainty in safety assessment. The filter switches from the nominal policy to the learned safe one when that range suggests it might be unsafe. We show that ACoFi guarantees that the rate of incorrectly quantifying uncertainty in the predicted safety of the nominal policy is asymptotically upper bounded by a user-defined parameter. This gives a soft safety guarantee rather than a hard safety guarantee. We evaluate ACoFi in a Dubins car simulation and a Safety Gymnasium environment, empirically demonstrating that it significantly outperforms the baseline method that uses a fixed switching threshold by achieving higher learned safety values and fewer safety violations, especially in out-of-distribution scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用学习安全滤波器与自适应一致性推断的安全控制
安全滤波器已被证明是确保具有不安全名义策略的控制系统安全的有效工具。为应对传统综合方法中的可扩展性挑战，已提出基于学习的方法来设计高维状态和控制空间系统的安全滤波器。然而，这些模型决策中不可避免的误差引发了对其可靠性和所提供安全保证的担忧。本文提出了自适应一致性滤波（ACoFi），该方法结合了基于学习的哈密顿-雅可比可达性安全滤波器与自适应一致性推断。在ACoFi中，滤波器根据其对动作安全性的预测误差动态调整切换标准。利用名义策略输出的安全值可能范围来量化安全评估的不确定性，当该范围暗示可能不安全时，滤波器将从名义策略切换到学习到的安全策略。我们证明ACoFi保证对名义策略预测安全性的不确定性量化错误率在渐近意义上被用户定义参数上界约束，从而提供软安全保证而非硬安全保证。我们在Dubins汽车仿真和Safety Gymnasium环境中评估ACoFi，实证表明其显著优于使用固定切换阈值的基线方法，实现了更高的学习安全值和更少的安全违规，尤其是在分布外场景中。
