Motivated by the need for scalable and reliable safety filters in high-dimensional control systems, this paper introduces Adaptive Conformal Filtering (ACoFi), which combines learned Hamilton-Jacobi reachability-based safety filters with adaptive conformal inference. ACoFi dynamically adjusts its switching threshold based on the uncertainty in predicted safety values, derived from the range of possible safety outcomes of the nominal policy. Experimental results in Dubins car and Safety Gymnasium environments show that ACoFi significantly outperforms a fixed-threshold baseline by achieving higher safety values and fewer violations, particularly in out-of-distribution settings, while providing a soft safety guarantee bounded by a user-defined parameter.
为解决高维控制系统中安全滤波器的可扩展性与可靠性问题，本文提出自适应一致性滤波（ACoFi），结合基于哈密顿-雅可比可达性的学习安全滤波器与自适应一致性推断。该方法根据名义策略安全预测值的不确定性范围动态调整切换阈值，当该范围暗示潜在不安全时，切换至学习安全策略。在Dubins车与Safety Gymnasium环境中的实验表明，ACoFi相比固定阈值基线显著提升安全值并减少违规次数，尤其在分布外场景表现更优，同时提供由用户参数控制的软安全保证。
Physics-Informed Neural Networks: A Didactic Derivation of the Complete Training Cycle
Authors: Abdeladhim Tahimi
First: 2026-04-20T16:34:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:34:47+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 5 figures, companion code at https://github.com/Tahimi/PINN-Didactic-Training-Cycle
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper is a step-by-step, self-contained guide to the complete training cycle of a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) -- a topic that existing tutorials and guides typically delegate to automatic differentiation libraries without exposing the underlying algebra. Using a first-order initial value problem with a known analytical solution as a running example, we walk through every stage of the process: forward propagation of both the network output and its temporal derivative, evaluation of a composite loss function built from the ODE residual and the initial condition, backpropagation of gradients -- with particular attention to the product rule that arises in hidden layers -- and a gradient descent parameter update. Every calculation is presented with explicit, verifiable numerical values using a 1-3-3-1 multilayer perceptron with two hidden layers and 22 trainable parameters. From these concrete examples, we derive general recursive formulas -- expressed as sensitivity propagation relations -- that extend the gradient computation to networks of arbitrary depth, and we connect these formulas to the automatic differentiation engines used in practice. The trained network is then validated against the exact solution, achieving a relative $L^2$ error of $4.290 \times 10^{-4}$ using only the physics-informed loss, without any data from the true solution. A companion Jupyter/PyTorch notebook reproduces every manual calculation and the full training pipeline, providing mutual validation between hand-derived and machine-computed gradients.
中文标题/摘要
标题：物理信息神经网络：完整训练周期的循序渐进推导
本文是一份逐步、自包含的物理信息神经网络（PINN）完整训练周期指南——现有教程通常将此过程委托给自动微分库而不揭示底层代数。以一个已知解析解的一阶初值问题为例，我们详述了每个阶段：网络输出及其时间导数的前向传播、由ODE残差和初始条件构建的复合损失函数的评估、梯度的反向传播——特别关注隐藏层中出现的乘积法则——以及梯度下降参数更新。所有计算均使用显式、可验证的数值，基于一个含两个隐藏层、共22个可训练参数的1-3-3-1多层感知机。从这些具体实例中，我们推导出通用递归公式——以敏感性传播关系表达——将梯度计算扩展至任意深度网络，并将其与实际应用中的自动微分引擎关联。训练后的网络在无真实解数据的情况下，仅使用物理信息损失，与精确解对比，相对L²误差为4.290×10⁻⁴。配套的Jupyter/PyTorch笔记本复现了所有手动计算和完整训练流程，实现手推与机器计算梯度的相互验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of transparent, step-by-step explanations for training Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), this paper derives the complete training cycle from first principles using a simple ODE with an analytical solution. It details forward propagation of network outputs and derivatives, construction of a composite loss combining ODE residual and initial condition, backpropagation with explicit handling of the product rule in hidden layers, and gradient descent updates using a 1-3-3-1 MLP with 22 parameters. The method yields a trained network achieving a relative L² error of 4.290 × 10⁻⁴ without any training data, and general recursive sensitivity formulas are derived to extend the approach to arbitrary network depths, mirroring automatic differentiation engines.
为弥补现有教程对物理信息神经网络（PINN）梯度计算与参数更新过程缺乏透明、逐步指导的不足，本文从第一性原理出发，以一个具有解析解的一阶初值问题为例，完整推导了PINN的训练全过程。文章详细说明了网络输出与时间导数的前向传播、由ODE残差与初值条件构成的复合损失函数的构建、隐藏层中乘积法则的梯度反向传播，以及基于1-3-3-1结构（22个可训练参数）的梯度下降更新。该方法训练出的网络在无真实数据情况下，仅使用物理约束损失即可达到4.290 × 10⁻⁴的相对L²误差，并推导出适用于任意深度网络的递归敏感性公式，与实际自动微分引擎相衔接。配套Jupyter/PyTorch笔记本验证了所有手工推导与机器计算梯度的一致性。
VLM-3R: Vision-Language Models Augmented with Instruction-Aligned 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Zhiwen Fan, Jian Zhang, Renjie Li, Junge Zhang, Runjin Chen, Hezhen Hu, Kevin Wang, Huaizhi Qu, Shijie Zhou, Dilin Wang, Zhicheng Yan, Hongyu Xu, Justin Theiss, Tianlong Chen, Jiachen Li, Zhengzhong Tu, Zhangyang Wang, Rakesh Ranjan
First: 2025-05-26T17:56:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:32:17+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://vlm-3r.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The rapid advancement of Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) for 2D images and videos has motivated extending these models to understand 3D scenes, aiming for human-like visual-spatial intelligence. Nevertheless, achieving deep spatial understanding comparable to human capabilities poses significant challenges in model encoding and data acquisition. Existing methods frequently depend on external depth sensors for geometry capture or utilize off-the-shelf algorithms for pre-constructing 3D maps, thereby limiting their scalability, especially with prevalent monocular video inputs and for time-sensitive applications. In this work, we introduce VLM-3R, a unified framework for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that incorporates 3D Reconstructive instruction tuning. VLM-3R processes monocular video frames by employing a geometry encoder to derive implicit 3D tokens that represent spatial understanding. Leveraging our Spatial-Visual-View Fusion and over 200K curated 3D reconstructive instruction tuning question-answer (QA) pairs, VLM-3R effectively aligns real-world spatial context with language instructions. This enables monocular 3D spatial assistance and embodied reasoning. To facilitate the evaluation of temporal reasoning, we introduce the Vision-Spatial-Temporal Intelligence benchmark, featuring over 138.6K QA pairs across five distinct tasks focused on evolving spatial relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model, VLM-3R, not only facilitates robust visual-spatial reasoning but also enables the understanding of temporal 3D context changes, excelling in both accuracy and scalability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VLM-3R：通过指令对齐的3D重建增强的视觉-语言模型
随着大型多模态模型（LMMs）在2D图像和视频领域的快速发展，推动了将其扩展至理解3D场景的研究，旨在实现类人视觉-空间智能。然而，实现与人类能力相当的深度空间理解，在模型编码和数据采集方面仍面临重大挑战。现有方法常依赖外部深度传感器获取几何信息，或使用现成算法预构建3D地图，从而限制了其可扩展性，尤其在单目视频输入和时效性应用中。本文提出VLM-3R，一种统一的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）框架，结合3D重建指令调优。VLM-3R通过几何编码器处理单目视频帧，生成隐式3D令牌以表征空间理解。借助我们的空间-视觉-视角融合机制及超过20万条精心筛选的3D重建指令调优问答对，VLM-3R有效将现实世界空间上下文与语言指令对齐，从而支持单目3D空间辅助与具身推理。为评估时序推理能力，我们引入视觉-空间-时序智能基准，包含超过13.86万条问答对，覆盖五个聚焦空间关系演变的任务。大量实验表明，我们的模型VLM-3R不仅支持稳健的视觉-空间推理，还能理解时序3D上下文变化，在准确性和可扩展性方面均表现优异。

Evaluated on a new Vision-Spatial-Temporal Intelligence benchmark with 138.6K QA pairs across five tasks, VLM-3R demonstrates superior accuracy and scalability in modeling evolving 3D spatial relationships over time.
为实现类人三维空间理解，VLM-3R提出一种统一框架，通过指令对齐的三维重建增强视觉语言模型。该框架利用几何编码器处理单目视频帧生成隐式三维标记，并结合20万+人工筛选的问答对，将空间语义与语言指令对齐。在包含13.86万问答对的视觉-空间-时序智能基准测试中，VLM-3R在动态三维空间关系推理上展现出更高的准确率与可扩展性。
WorldDB: A Vector Graph-of-Worlds Memory Engine with Ontology-Aware Write-Time Reconciliation
Authors: Harish Santhanalakshmi Ganesan
First: 2026-04-20T16:30:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:30:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Persistent memory is the bottleneck separating stateless chatbots from long-running agentic systems. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over flat vector stores fragments facts into chunks, loses cross-session identity, and has no first-class notion of supersession or contradiction. Recent bitemporal knowledge-graph systems (Graphiti, Memento, Hydra DB) add typed edges and valid-time metadata, but the graph itself remains flat: no recursive composition, no content-addressed invariants on nodes, and edge types carry no behavior beyond a label. We present WorldDB, a memory engine built on three commitments: (i) every node is a world -- a container with its own interior subgraph, ontology scope, and composed embedding, recursive to arbitrary depth; (ii) nodes are content-addressed and immutable, so any edit produces a new hash at the node and every ancestor, giving a Merkle-style audit trail for free; (iii) edges are write-time programs -- each edge type ships on_insert/on_delete/on_query_rewrite handlers (supersession closes validity, contradicts preserves both sides, same_as stages a merge proposal), so no raw append path exists. On LongMemEval-s (500 questions, ~115k-token conversational stacks), WorldDB with Claude Opus 4.7 as answerer achieves 96.40% overall / 97.11% task-averaged accuracy, a +5.61pp improvement over the previously reported Hydra DB state-of-the-art (90.79%) and +11.20pp over Supermemory (85.20%), with perfect single-session-assistant recall and robust performance on temporal reasoning (96.24%), knowledge update (98.72%), and preference synthesis (96.67%). Ablations show that the engine's graph layer -- resolver-unified entities and typed refers_to edges -- contributes +7.0pp task-averaged independently of the underlying answerer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldDB：一种具备本体感知写时协调的向量图世界记忆引擎
持久内存是无状态聊天机器人与长期运行代理系统之间的瓶颈。基于扁平向量存储的检索增强生成（RAG）将事实切分为片段，丢失跨会话身份，且缺乏对覆盖或矛盾的原生支持。近期的双时态知识图谱系统（Graphiti、Memento、Hydra DB）增加了带类型边和有效时间元数据，但图结构本身仍为扁平：无递归组合、节点无内容寻址不变量，边类型仅携带标签外无行为。我们提出WorldDB，一种基于三项承诺构建的记忆引擎：（i）每个节点即为一个世界——一个拥有自身内部子图、本体作用域和组合嵌入的容器，可递归至任意深度；（ii）节点内容寻址且不可变，任何编辑将在节点及其所有祖先生成新哈希，自动提供Merkle式审计轨迹；（iii）边为写时程序——每种边类型配备on_insert/on_delete/on_query_rewrite处理器（覆盖关闭有效性，矛盾保留双方，same_as触发合并提案），故无原始追加路径。在LongMemEval-s（500题，约11.5万token对话栈）上，WorldDB搭配Claude Opus 4.7作答者，整体准确率96.40%，任务平均准确率97.11%，较此前报道的Hydra DB最先进水平（90.79%）提升+5.61pp，较Supermemory（85.20%）提升+11.20pp，单会话助理召回率完美，且在时序推理（96.24%）、知识更新（98.72%）和偏好合成（96.67%）上表现稳健。消融实验表明，引擎的图层——统一实体解析与带类型refers_to边——独立于底层答者，贡献+7.0pp任务平均提升。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of flat vector stores in persistent memory systems for long-running agentic systems, WorldDB introduces a vector graph-of-worlds memory engine that treats each node as a self-contained world with its own subgraph, ontology, and composed embedding. Nodes are content-addressed and immutable, ensuring a Merkle-style audit trail upon any edit, while edges are write-time programs that trigger behavior such as supersession or contradiction resolution. Evaluated on LongMemEval-s with Claude Opus 4.7, WorldDB achieved 96.40% overall accuracy and 97.11% task-averaged accuracy, outperforming Hydra DB by +5.61pp and Supermemory by +11.20pp, with perfect single-session recall and strong performance across temporal reasoning, knowledge update, and preference synthesis tasks.
为解决扁平向量存储在长时代理系统中的局限性，WorldDB提出一种基于世界图的向量记忆引擎，每个节点是一个包含子图、本体范围和嵌入的独立世界，支持递归组合。节点内容可寻址且不可变，每次编辑都会生成新的哈希值并追溯至祖先节点，形成默克尔式审计链；边则作为写入时程序，触发如超时、矛盾保留或合并提议等行为。在LongMemEval-s数据集上使用Claude Opus 4.7评估，WorldDB整体准确率达96.40%，优于Hydra DB 5.61个百分点、Supermemory 11.20个百分点，单会话召回率完美，且在时序推理、知识更新和偏好合成任务中表现优异。
Language Models Don't Know What You Want: Evaluating Personalization in Deep Research Needs Real Users
Authors: Nishant Balepur, Malachi Hamada, Varsha Kishore, Sergey Feldman, Amanpreet Singh, Pao Siangliulue, Joseph Chee Chang, Eunsol Choi, Jordan Lee Boyd-Graber, Aakanksha Naik
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-03-17T04:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:29:15+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep Research (DR) systems help researchers cope with ballooning publishing counts. Such tools synthesize scientific papers to answer research queries, but lack understanding of their users. We address this with MyScholarQA (MySQA), a personalized DR agent that: 1) infers a profile with a user's research interests; 2) proposes personalized actions for a user's input query; and 3) writes a multi-section report for the query that follows user-approved actions. We first test MySQA with NLP's standard protocol: we build a benchmark with synthetic users and LLM judges, where MySQA beats baselines in citation metrics and personalized action-following. However, we suspect this process does not cover all aspects of personalized DR users value, so we interview users in an online version of MySQA to unmask them. We reveal nine nuanced errors of personalized DR undetectable by our LLM judges, and we study qualitative feedback to form lessons for future DR design. In all, we argue for a pillar of personalization that easy-to-use LLM judges can lead NLP to overlook: real progress in personalization is only possible with real users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型不知道你想要什么：评估深度研究中的个性化需要真实用户
深度研究（DR）系统帮助研究人员应对日益增长的出版物数量。这类工具通过综合科学论文来回答研究查询，但缺乏对用户的理解。我们通过MyScholarQA（MySQA）——一个个性化的DR代理——解决这一问题：1）推断用户的研究兴趣档案；2）为用户的输入查询提出个性化操作建议；3）根据用户批准的操作撰写多部分报告。我们首先按照NLP标准协议测试MySQA：构建合成用户和LLM裁判的基准，结果显示MySQA在引用指标和个性化操作遵循方面优于基线。然而，我们怀疑该过程未能涵盖个性化DR用户价值的所有方面，因此在MySQA的在线版本中采访用户以揭示其真实需求。我们发现了九种LLM裁判无法检测到的个性化DR的细微错误，并通过定性反馈总结未来DR设计的经验。总之，我们主张个性化的一个核心支柱：仅靠易用的LLM裁判可能导致NLP忽视真实进展——真正的个性化进步必须依赖真实用户。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between deep research tools and real user needs, the paper introduces MyScholarQA, a personalized agent that infers user profiles, proposes tailored actions, and generates reports following user-approved steps. Evaluated first with synthetic users and LLM judges, MySQA outperformed baselines in citation metrics and action adherence. However, interviews with real users revealed nine nuanced personalization failures invisible to automated judges, underscoring that true personalization requires real user feedback and cannot be fully captured by standard NLP benchmarks.
为弥补深度研究工具在合成评估与真实用户需求间的差距，本文提出MyScholarQA个性化代理，可推断用户研究兴趣、提出定制化操作并依据用户反馈生成多章节报告。在合成用户与LLM裁判的基准测试中，MySQA在引用指标和操作遵循度上优于基线。但对真实用户的访谈揭示了九种自动化裁判无法识别的个性化缺陷，表明深度研究系统的真正个性化进步必须依赖真实用户反馈，而非仅靠标准NLP评测。
ScienceBoard: Evaluating Multimodal Autonomous Agents in Realistic Scientific Workflows
Authors: Qiushi Sun, Zhoumianze Liu, Chang Ma, Zichen Ding, Fangzhi Xu, Zhangyue Yin, Haiteng Zhao, Zhenyu Wu, Kanzhi Cheng, Zhaoyang Liu, Jianing Wang, Qintong Li, Xiangru Tang, Tianbao Xie, Xiachong Feng, Xiang Li, Ben Kao, Wenhai Wang, Biqing Qi, Lingpeng Kong, Zhiyong Wu
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-05-26T12:27:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:29:04+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026 Camera Ready Version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have extended their impact beyond Natural Language Processing, substantially fostering the development of interdisciplinary research. Recently, various LLM-based agents have been developed to assist scientific discovery progress across multiple aspects and domains. Among these, computer-using agents, capable of interacting with operating systems as humans do, are paving the way to automated scientific problem-solving and addressing routines in researchers' workflows. Recognizing the transformative potential of these agents, we introduce ScienceBoard, which encompasses two complementary contributions: (i) a realistic, multi-domain environment featuring dynamic and visually rich scientific workflows with integrated professional software, where agents can autonomously interact via different interfaces to accelerate complex research tasks and experiments; and (ii) a challenging benchmark of 169 high-quality, rigorously validated real-world tasks curated by humans, spanning scientific-discovery workflows in domains such as biochemistry, astronomy, and geoinformatics. Extensive evaluations of agents with state-of-the-art backbones (e.g., GPT-4o, Claude 3.7, UI-TARS) show that, despite some promising results, they still fall short of reliably assisting scientists in complex workflows, achieving only a 15% overall success rate. In-depth analysis further provides valuable insights for addressing current agent limitations and more effective design principles, paving the way to build more capable agents for scientific discovery. Our code, environment, and benchmark are at https://qiushisun.github.io/ScienceBoard-Home/.

Multi-Scale Reversible Chaos Game Representation: A Unified Framework for Sequence Classification
Authors: Sarwan Ali, Taslim Murad
First: 2026-04-20T16:28:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:28:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biological classification with interpretability remains a challenging task. For this, we introduce a novel encoding framework, Multi-Scale Reversible Chaos Game Representation (MS-RCGR), that transforms biological sequences into multi-resolution geometric representations with guaranteed reversibility. Unlike traditional sequence encoding methods, MS-RCGR employs rational arithmetic and hierarchical k-mer decomposition to generate scale-invariant features that preserve complete sequence information while enabling diverse analytical approaches. Our framework bridges three distinct paradigms for sequence analysis: (1) traditional machine learning using extracted geometric features, (2) computer vision models operating on CGR-generated images, and (3) hybrid approaches combining protein language model embeddings with CGR features. Through comprehensive experiments on synthetic DNA and protein datasets encompassing seven distinct sequence classes, we demonstrate that MS-RCGR features consistently enhance classification performance across all paradigms. Notably, our hybrid approach combining pre-trained language model embeddings (ESM2, ProtT5) with MS-RCGR features achieves superior performance compared to either method alone. The reversibility property of our encoding ensures no information loss during transformation, while multi-scale analysis captures patterns ranging from individual nucleotides to complex motif structures. Our results indicate that MS-RCGR provides a flexible, interpretable, and high-performing foundation for biological sequence analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多尺度可逆混沌游戏表示：序列分类的统一框架
具有可解释性的生物分类仍是一项挑战。为此，我们提出了一种新颖的编码框架——多尺度可逆混沌游戏表示（MS-RCGR），将生物序列转换为具有可逆性的多分辨率几何表示。与传统序列编码方法不同，MS-RCGR采用有理数运算和分层k-mer分解，生成尺度不变特征，既保留完整序列信息，又支持多样化的分析方法。我们的框架融合了三种序列分析范式：（1）使用提取几何特征的传统机器学习方法；（2）在CGR生成图像上运行的计算机视觉模型；（3）结合蛋白质语言模型嵌入与CGR特征的混合方法。在涵盖七类序列的合成DNA和蛋白质数据集上的全面实验表明，MS-RCGR特征在所有范式中均持续提升分类性能。值得注意的是，结合预训练语言模型嵌入（ESM2、ProtT5）与MS-RCGR特征的混合方法表现优于任一方法单独使用。我们的编码可逆性确保转换过程中无信息损失，而多尺度分析捕捉从单个核苷酸到复杂基序结构的模式。结果表明，MS-RCGR为生物序列分析提供了一种灵活、可解释且高性能的基础框架。

The reversibility ensures no data loss, and multi-scale analysis captures patterns from nucleotides to motifs, offering a flexible and interpretable foundation for sequence analysis.
为解决生物序列分类中可解释性不足的问题，本文提出多尺度可逆混沌游戏表示（MS-RCGR）框架，通过可逆有理数运算与分层k-mer分解，将序列转化为多分辨率几何表示，完整保留原始信息。该方法支持三种分析范式：传统机器学习、基于CGR图像的计算机视觉模型，以及结合蛋白质语言模型嵌入与CGR特征的混合模型。在包含七类序列的合成DNA和蛋白质数据集上，MS-RCGR在所有范式中均提升分类性能，其中融合ESM2或ProtT5嵌入的混合模型表现最优。其可逆性确保无信息丢失，多尺度分析可捕捉从单核苷酸到复杂基序的模式，为生物序列分析提供灵活、可解释且高性能的基础。
Preparation of Fractal-Inspired Computational Architectures for Automated Neural Design Exploration
Authors: Yash Mittal, Dmitry Ignatov, Radu Timofte
First: 2025-11-10T17:31:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:28:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It introduces FractalNet, a fractal-inspired computational architectures for advanced large language model analysis that mainly challenges model diversity on a large scale in an efficient manner. The new set-up involves a template-driven generator, runner, and evaluation framework that, through systematic permutations of convolutional, normalization, activation, and dropout layers, can create more than 1,200 variants of neural networks. Fractal templates allow for structural recursion and multi-column pathways, thus, models become deeper and wider in a balanced way. Training utilizes PyTorch, Automatic Mixed Precision (AMP), and gradient checkpointing and is carried out on the CIFAR-10 dataset for five epochs. The outcomes show that fractal-based architectures are capable of strong performance and are computationally efficient. The paper positions fractal design as a feasible and resource-efficient method of automated architecture exploration.

标题：分形启发式计算架构的构建用于自动化神经网络设计探索

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and diverse neural architecture exploration, this paper proposes FractalNet, a fractal-inspired computational framework that systematically generates over 1,200 neural network variants through template-driven permutations of convolutional, normalization, activation, and dropout layers. The architecture leverages structural recursion and multi-column pathways to balance depth and width, enabling scalable and balanced model design. Evaluated on CIFAR-10 with PyTorch, AMP, and gradient checkpointing over five epochs, FractalNet demonstrates strong performance while maintaining computational efficiency, positioning fractal design as a viable and resource-conscious approach for automated neural architecture search.
为实现高效且多样化的神经网络架构探索，本文提出FractalNet，一种受分形启发的计算框架，通过模板驱动的卷积、归一化、激活和Dropout层排列，系统生成超过1200种网络变体。该架构利用结构递归与多列路径平衡深度与宽度，支持可扩展且资源高效的模型设计。在CIFAR-10数据集上使用PyTorch、自动混合精度与梯度检查点技术训练五轮后，FractalNet展现出优异性能与计算效率，证明分形设计是自动化架构搜索的可行方法。
SemLT3D: Semantic-Guided Expert Distillation for Camera-only Long-Tailed 3D Object Detection
Authors: Hao Vo, Khoa Vo, Thinh Phan, Ngo Xuan Cuong, Gianfranco Doretto, Hien Nguyen, Anh Nguyen, Ngan Le
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-20T16:28:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:28:01+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Camera-only 3D object detection has emerged as a cost-effective and scalable alternative to LiDAR for autonomous driving, yet existing methods primarily prioritize overall performance while overlooking the severe long-tail imbalance inherent in real-world datasets. In practice, many rare but safety-critical categories such as children, strollers, or emergency vehicles are heavily underrepresented, leading to biased learning and degraded performance. This challenge is further exacerbated by pronounced inter-class ambiguity (e.g., visually similar subclasses) and substantial intra-class diversity (e.g., objects varying widely in appearance, scale, pose, or context), which together hinder reliable long-tail recognition. In this work, we introduce SemLT3D, a Semantic-Guided Expert Distillation framework designed to enrich the representation space for underrepresented classes through semantic priors. SemLT3D consists of: (1) a language-guided mixture-of-experts module that routes 3D queries to specialized experts according to their semantic affinity, enabling the model to better disentangle confusing classes and specialize on tail distributions; and (2) a semantic projection distillation pipeline that aligns 3D queries with CLIP-informed 2D semantics, producing more coherent and discriminative features across diverse visual manifestations. Although motivated by long-tail imbalance, the semantically structured learning in SemLT3D also improves robustness under broader appearance variations and challenging corner cases, offering a principled step toward more reliable camera-only 3D perception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SemLT3D：基于语义引导的专家蒸馏方法用于仅摄像头长尾3D目标检测
仅摄像头3D目标检测已成为自动驾驶领域成本效益高且可扩展的替代方案，但现有方法主要关注整体性能，忽视了真实世界数据集中严重的长尾不平衡问题。实践中，许多罕见但安全关键的类别（如儿童、婴儿车或应急车辆）严重欠采样，导致学习偏差和性能下降。这一挑战进一步因类间歧义（如视觉上相似的子类）和类内多样性（如物体在外观、尺度、姿态或上下文上差异显著）而加剧，共同阻碍了可靠的长尾识别。本文提出SemLT3D，一种语义引导的专家蒸馏框架，通过语义先验丰富欠采样类别的表征空间。SemLT3D包含：（1）语言引导的专家混合模块，根据3D查询的语义亲和度将其路由至专用专家，使模型能更好区分混淆类别并专注于尾部分布；（2）语义投影蒸馏流程，将3D查询与CLIP引导的2D语义对齐，生成更连贯且具判别性的跨多样视觉表现特征。尽管旨在解决长尾不平衡，SemLT3D的语义结构化学习也提升了模型在外观变化和挑战性边缘案例下的鲁棒性，为更可靠的仅摄像头3D感知提供了原则性进展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the long-tail imbalance in camera-only 3D object detection datasets, where rare but safety-critical classes are underrepresented and hinder reliable recognition, SemLT3D introduces a semantic-guided expert distillation framework. It employs a language-guided mixture-of-experts module to route 3D queries to specialized experts based on semantic affinity, helping disentangle ambiguous classes and focus on tail distributions. Coupled with a semantic projection distillation pipeline that aligns 3D queries with CLIP-informed 2D semantics, the method generates more discriminative and coherent features across diverse appearances. Experimental results demonstrate improved detection performance for long-tail classes while also enhancing robustness under appearance variations and challenging scenarios, offering a principled approach to more reliable camera-only 3D perception.
为解决相机-only 3D目标检测中长尾分布导致的罕见但关键类别（如儿童、婴儿车等）严重欠采样问题，SemLT3D提出一种语义引导的专家蒸馏框架。该框架包含语言引导的专家混合模块，根据语义亲和度将3D查询路由至专用专家，以增强混淆类别的解耦与尾部分布专业化；并结合语义投影蒸馏流程，利用CLIP引导的2D语义对齐3D查询，生成更具判别力和一致性的特征。实验表明，该方法在长尾类别上显著提升性能，同时增强对视觉变化和边缘场景的鲁棒性，验证了其在提升相机-only 3D感知可靠性方面的有效性。
Stable Language Guidance for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Zhihao Zhan, Yuhao Chen, Jiaying Zhou, Qinhan Lyu, Hao Liu, Keze Wang, Liang Lin, Guangrun Wang
First: 2026-01-07T16:16:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:27:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL2026 main conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generalized robotic control; however, they remain notoriously brittle to linguistic perturbations. We identify a critical ``modality collapse'' phenomenon where strong visual priors overwhelm sparse linguistic signals, causing agents to overfit to specific instruction phrasings while ignoring the underlying semantic intent. To address this, we propose Residual Semantic Steering (RSS), a probabilistic framework that disentangles physical affordance from semantic execution. RSS introduces two theoretical innovations: (1) Monte Carlo Syntactic Integration, which approximates the true semantic posterior via dense, LLM-driven distributional expansion, and (2) Residual Affordance Steering, a dual-stream decoding mechanism that explicitly isolates the causal influence of language by subtracting the visual affordance prior. Theoretical analysis suggests that RSS effectively maximizes the mutual information between action and intent while suppressing visual distractors. Empirical results across diverse manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that RSS achieves state-of-the-art robustness, maintaining performance even under adversarial linguistic perturbations. We release our code at https://github.com/Doo-mon/RSS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言-动作模型的稳定语言引导
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用机器人控制方面展现了出色能力，但对语言扰动极为脆弱。我们发现了一种关键的“模态坍缩”现象：强视觉先验会压制稀疏的语言信号，导致智能体过度拟合特定指令表达，而忽略其语义意图。为解决此问题，我们提出残差语义引导（RSS），一种概率框架，将物理可操作性与语义执行解耦。RSS包含两项理论创新：（1）蒙特卡洛句法积分，通过密集的LLM驱动分布扩展近似真实语义后验；（2）残差可操作性引导，一种双流解码机制，通过减去视觉可操作性先验，显式隔离语言的因果影响。理论分析表明，RSS能有效最大化动作与意图之间的互信息，同时抑制视觉干扰。在多种操作基准上的实验证明，RSS达到最先进鲁棒性，在对抗性语言扰动下仍保持性能。代码已发布于 https://github.com/Doo-mon/RSS。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of Vision-Language-Action models to linguistic variations, this work identifies 'modality collapse'—where visual priors dominate sparse language signals, causing overfitting to specific phrasings. To resolve this, the authors introduce Residual Semantic Steering (RSS), a probabilistic method combining Monte Carlo Syntactic Integration for semantic posterior approximation and Residual Affordance Steering to isolate language’s causal effect by subtracting visual priors. Experiments across manipulation benchmarks show RSS achieves state-of-the-art robustness, maintaining performance under adversarial linguistic perturbations while maximizing action-intent mutual information.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型对语言扰动敏感的问题，本文提出残差语义引导（RSS）框架，旨在分离语义意图与视觉可操作性。RSS通过蒙特卡洛句法积分利用大语言模型生成语义后验分布，并通过残差可操作性引导在解码时减去视觉先验，从而凸显语言的因果作用。在多个操作基准测试中，RSS展现出当前最优的鲁棒性，在对抗性语言扰动下仍能保持性能稳定并提升泛化能力。
Train Separately, Merge Together: Modular Post-Training with Mixture-of-Experts
Authors: Jacob Morrison, Sanjay Adhikesaven, Akshita Bhagia, Matei Zaharia, Noah A. Smith, Sewon Min
First: 2026-04-20T16:24:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:24:41+00:00
Comments: 9 content pages, 23 pages overall, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Extending a fully post-trained language model with new domain capabilities is fundamentally limited by monolithic training paradigms: retraining from scratch is expensive and scales poorly, while continued training often degrades existing capabilities. We present BAR (Branch-Adapt-Route), which trains independent domain experts, each through its own mid-training, supervised finetuning, and reinforcement learning pipeline, and composes them via a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with lightweight router training. Unlike retraining approaches that mix all domains and require full reprocessing for any update (with cost scaling quadratically), BAR enables updating individual experts independently with linear cost scaling and no degradation to existing domains. At the 7B scale, with experts for math, code, tool use, and safety, BAR achieves an overall score of 49.1 (averaged across 7 evaluation categories), matching or exceeding re-training baselines (47.8 without mid-training, 50.5 with). We further show that modular training provides a structural advantage: by isolating each domain, it avoids the catastrophic forgetting that occurs when late-stage RL degrades capabilities from earlier training stages, while significantly reducing the cost and complexity of updating or adding a domain. Together, these results suggest that decoupled, expert-based training is a scalable alternative to monolithic retraining for extending language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分别训练，合并集成：基于专家混合的模块化后训练
扩展一个完全后训练的语言模型以获得新领域能力，本质上受限于单体训练范式：从头训练成本高昂且扩展性差，而持续训练常导致现有能力退化。我们提出BAR（分支-适应-路由），该方法通过独立的领域专家训练，每个专家通过自身的中期监督微调和强化学习流程训练，并通过轻量级路由器的专家混合架构进行组合。与混合所有领域并需完整重处理的重训练方法不同（成本呈二次方增长），BAR支持独立更新各专家，成本呈线性增长且不损害现有领域能力。在7B规模下，针对数学、代码、工具使用和安全领域的专家，BAR获得综合得分49.1（跨7项评估类别平均），匹配或超越重训练基线（无中期训练为47.8，有中期训练为50.5）。我们进一步表明，模块化训练具有结构优势：通过隔离各领域，避免后期强化学习导致早期训练能力的灾难性遗忘，同时显著降低更新或添加新领域的成本与复杂度。这些结果共同表明，解耦的专家式训练是扩展语言模型的一种可扩展替代方案，优于单体重训练。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency and capability degradation inherent in monolithic retraining of large language models, the authors propose BAR, a modular post-training method that trains domain-specific experts independently via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, then combines them using a lightweight router. Unlike full retraining, which scales quadratically and risks catastrophic forgetting, BAR enables linear-cost updates to individual experts without degrading existing capabilities. At the 7B scale, BAR achieves a 49.1 average score across seven evaluation categories, matching or surpassing retraining baselines (47.8 without mid-training, 50.5 with), while demonstrating structural advantages in avoiding forgetting and reducing update complexity.
为解决语言模型在新增领域时重训效率低、能力退化的问题，作者提出BAR方法，通过独立的中阶段监督微调与强化学习训练各领域专家，再用轻量路由器组合成混合专家架构。该方法支持对单个专家线性成本更新，不损害原有能力，而传统重训成本呈二次增长。在7B规模下，针对数学、代码、工具使用与安全四个领域的专家，BAR平均得分49.1（七项评估平均），优于或持平基线（无中阶段训练为47.8，有中阶段训练为50.5），同时有效避免灾难性遗忘，降低更新复杂度。
NI Sampling: Accelerating Discrete Diffusion Sampling by Token Order Optimization
Authors: Enshu Liu, Xuefei Ning, Yu Wang, Zinan Lin
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-20T16:22:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:22:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to traditional autoregressive approaches, offering the flexibility to generate tokens in arbitrary orders and the potential of parallel decoding. However, existing heuristic sampling strategies remain inefficient: they choose only a small part of tokens to sample at each step, leaving substantial room for improvement. In this work, we study the problem of token sampling order optimization and demonstrate its significant potential for acceleration. Specifically, we find that fully leveraging correct predictions at each step can reduce the number of sampling iterations by an order of magnitude without compromising accuracy. Based on this, we propose Neural Indicator Sampling (NI Sampling), a general sampling order optimization framework that utilize a neural indicator to decide which tokens should be sampled at each step. We further propose a novel trajectory-preserving objective to train the indicator. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream models across multiple benchmarks show that our method achieves up to 14.3$\times$ acceleration over full-step sampling with negligible performance drop, and consistently outperforms confidence threshold sampling in the accuracy-step trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/imagination-research/NI-Sampling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NI采样：通过令牌顺序优化加速离散扩散采样
离散扩散语言模型（dLLMs）近期作为传统自回归方法的有前景替代方案出现，其优势在于可按任意顺序生成令牌并支持并行解码。然而，现有启发式采样策略效率低下：每步仅采样少量令牌，仍有巨大优化空间。本文研究令牌采样顺序优化问题，证明其加速潜力显著。具体而言，我们发现充分利用每步正确预测可将采样迭代次数减少一个数量级，且不牺牲精度。基于此，我们提出神经指示采样（NI采样），一种通用的采样顺序优化框架，利用神经指示器决定每步应采样的令牌。我们进一步提出一种新颖的轨迹保持目标函数以训练指示器。在LLaDA和Dream模型多个基准上的实验表明，我们的方法相比全步采样加速高达14.3倍，性能损失微乎其微，并在精度-步数权衡中持续优于置信度阈值采样。代码见https://github.com/imagination-research/NI-Sampling。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of existing heuristic sampling strategies in discrete diffusion language models, which sample only a subset of tokens per step and leave significant computational waste, this work introduces Neural Indicator Sampling (NI Sampling) to optimize token sampling order. The method employs a neural indicator trained via a trajectory-preserving objective to determine which tokens to sample at each step, enabling full utilization of correct predictions and reducing sampling iterations by an order of magnitude without accuracy loss. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream models across multiple benchmarks demonstrate up to 14.3x speedup over full-step sampling with negligible performance degradation, and consistently outperform confidence threshold sampling in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off.
为了解决现有离散扩散语言模型中启发式采样策略效率低下、每步仅采样少量token导致计算浪费的问题，本文提出神经指示采样（NI Sampling）方法，通过神经指示器优化token采样顺序。该指示器基于轨迹保持目标训练，能充分利用每步正确预测，将采样迭代次数降低一个数量级且不损失精度。在LLaDA和Dream模型多个基准测试中，该方法相较全步采样提速最高达14.3倍，性能损失可忽略，并在准确率-效率权衡上持续优于置信度阈值采样方法。
A Generalized Synthetic Control Method for Baseline Estimation in Demand Response Services
Authors: Jonas Sievers, Mardavij Roozbehani
First: 2026-04-20T16:21:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:21:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Baseline estimation is critical to Demand Response (DR) settlement in electricity markets, yet existing machine learning methods remain limited in predictive performance, while methodologies from causal inference and counterfactual prediction are still underutilized in this domain. We introduce a Generalized Synthetic Control Method that builds on the classical Synthetic Control Method (SCM) from econometrics. While SCM provides a powerful framework for counterfactual estimation, classical SCM remains a static estimator: it fits the treated unit as a combination of contemporaneous donor units and therefore ignores predictable temporal structure in the residual error. We develop a generalized SCM framework that transforms baseline estimation into a dynamic counterfactual prediction problem by augmenting the donor representation with exogenous features, lagged treated load, and selected lagged donor signals. This enriched representation allows the estimator to capture autoregressive dependence, delayed donor-response patterns, and error-correction effects beyond the scope of standard SCM. The framework further accommodates nonlinear predictors when linear weighting is inadequate, with the greatest benefit arising in limited-data settings. Experiments on the Ausgrid smart-meter dataset show consistent improvements over classical SCM and strong benchmark methods, with the dominant performance gains driven by dynamic augmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于需求响应服务基线估计的广义合成控制方法
基线估计对电力市场中的需求响应（DR）结算至关重要，但现有机器学习方法在预测性能上仍有限，而因果推断和反事实预测方法在该领域尚未充分应用。我们提出一种广义合成控制方法，基于计量经济学中的经典合成控制方法（SCM）。虽然SCM为反事实估计提供了强大框架，但经典SCM仍是静态估计器：它将处理单元视为同期供体单元的组合，因而忽略了残差误差中的可预测时间结构。我们开发的广义SCM框架通过在供体表示中加入外生特征、滞后处理负荷和选定的滞后供体信号，将基线估计转化为动态反事实预测问题。这种增强的表示使估计器能够捕捉自回归依赖、延迟供体响应模式和标准SCM之外的误差修正效应。该框架还支持在线性加权不足时使用非线性预测器，尤其在数据有限场景下收益显著。在Ausgrid智能电表数据集上的实验表明，该方法在经典SCM和主流基准方法上均取得一致改进，主要性能提升源于动态增强。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate baseline estimation in demand response services and the limitations of existing machine learning and causal inference methods, the paper proposes a Generalized Synthetic Control Method that extends the classical SCM by incorporating temporal dynamics. The method augments donor unit representations with exogenous features, lagged treated load, and lagged donor signals to model autoregressive dependencies and delayed responses. Experimental results on the Ausgrid smart-meter dataset demonstrate consistent performance gains over classical SCM and other benchmarks, with the most significant improvements attributed to the dynamic augmentation of the model.
为提升需求响应服务中基线估计的准确性，针对现有机器学习方法预测性能不足及经典合成控制方法忽略时间动态的问题，本文提出一种广义合成控制方法，通过引入外生变量、滞后处理负荷和滞后供体信号，增强对自回归结构、延迟响应和误差修正效应的建模能力。在Ausgrid智能电表数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较经典合成控制及其他基准方法表现更优，性能提升主要源于动态增强机制。
Asset Harvester: Extracting 3D Assets from Autonomous Driving Logs for Simulation
Authors: Tianshi Cao, Jiawei Ren, Yuxuan Zhang, Jaewoo Seo, Jiahui Huang, Shikhar Solanki, Haotian Zhang, Mingfei Guo, Haithem Turki, Muxingzi Li, Yue Zhu, Sipeng Zhang, Zan Gojcic, Sanja Fidler, Kangxue Yin
First: 2026-04-20T16:20:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:20:57+00:00
Comments: NVIDIA white paper. The project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/asset-harvester/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Closed-loop simulation is a core component of autonomous vehicle (AV) development, enabling scalable testing, training, and safety validation before real-world deployment. Neural scene reconstruction converts driving logs into interactive 3D environments for simulation, but it does not produce complete 3D object assets required for agent manipulation and large-viewpoint novel-view synthesis. To address this challenge, we present Asset Harvester, an image-to-3D model and end-to-end pipeline that converts sparse, in-the-wild object observations from real driving logs into complete, simulation-ready assets. Rather than relying on a single model component, we developed a system-level design for real-world AV data that combines large-scale curation of object-centric training tuples, geometry-aware preprocessing across heterogeneous sensors, and a robust training recipe that couples sparse-view-conditioned multiview generation with 3D Gaussian lifting. Within this system, SparseViewDiT is explicitly designed to address limited-angle views and other real-world data challenges. Together with hybrid data curation, augmentation, and self-distillation, this system enables scalable conversion of sparse AV object observations into reusable 3D assets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：资产采集器：从自动驾驶日志中提取3D资产用于仿真
闭环仿真作为自动驾驶（AV）开发的核心组成部分，能够在真实部署前实现可扩展的测试、训练与安全验证。神经场景重建可将驾驶日志转化为交互式3D环境用于仿真，但无法生成用于代理操控和大视角新视角合成所需的完整3D物体资产。为解决此问题，我们提出资产采集器（Asset Harvester），这是一种图像到3D的模型及端到端流程，可将真实驾驶日志中的稀疏野外物体观测转换为完整、可直接用于仿真的3D资产。该系统采用系统级设计，结合大规模以物体为中心的训练元组整理、跨异构传感器的几何感知预处理，以及将稀疏视角条件多视角生成与3D高斯提升相结合的稳健训练方案。其中，SparseViewDiT专为应对有限视角及其他真实数据挑战而设计。结合混合数据整理、增强与自蒸馏，该系统可实现稀疏自动驾驶物体观测到可复用3D资产的规模化转换。
Summary / 总结
To support scalable closed-loop simulation for autonomous vehicles, the paper addresses the gap between neural scene reconstruction and the need for complete, manipulable 3D object assets. Asset Harvester introduces an end-to-end pipeline that converts sparse, real-world object observations from driving logs into full 3D assets suitable for simulation. The method combines large-scale object-centric data curation, geometry-aware preprocessing across sensors, and a training framework that integrates sparse-view-conditioned multiview generation with 3D Gaussian lifting, powered by the SparseViewDiT model designed for limited-angle views. Experimental results demonstrate the system’s ability to reliably generate high-fidelity 3D assets from real-world data, enabling novel-view synthesis and agent interaction in simulation environments.
为支持自动驾驶车辆的可扩展闭环仿真，作者解决了神经场景重建与完整可操控3D物体资产需求之间的差距。Asset Harvester 是一个端到端系统，将来自驾驶日志的稀疏真实世界物体观测转换为适用于仿真的完整3D资产。该系统结合了以物体为中心的数据整理、跨传感器的几何感知预处理，以及将稀疏视角多视角生成与3D高斯提升相结合的训练方案，其中SparseViewDiT专为处理有限视角等现实数据挑战而设计。实验结果表明，该系统能从真实数据中成功生成高保真、适用于仿真的3D资产，支持新视角合成和仿真环境中代理交互。
An Integrated Deep-Learning Framework for Peptide-Protein Interaction Prediction and Target-Conditioned Peptide Generation with ConGA-PePPI and TC-PepGen
Authors: Chupei Tang, Junxiao Kong, Moyu Tang, Di Wang, Jixiu Zhai, Ronghao Xie, Shangkun Sima, Tianchi Lu
First: 2026-04-20T16:20:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:20:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Motivation: Peptide-protein interactions (PepPIs) are central to cellular regulation and peptide therapeutics, but experimental characterization remains too slow for large-scale screening. Existing methods usually emphasize either interaction prediction or peptide generation, leaving candidate prioritization, residue-level interpretation, and target-conditioned expansion insufficiently integrated. Results: We present an integrated framework for early-stage peptide screening that combines a partner-aware prediction and localization model (ConGA-PepPI) with a target-conditioned generative model (TC-PepGen). ConGA-PepPI uses asymmetric encoding, bidirectional cross-attention, and progressive transfer from pair prediction to binding-site localization, while TC-PepGen preserves target information throughout autoregressive decoding via layerwise conditioning. In five-fold cross-validation, ConGA-PepPI achieved 0.839 accuracy and 0.921 AUROC, with binding-site AUPR values of 0.601 on the protein side and 0.950 on the peptide side, and remained competitive on external benchmarks. Under a controlled length-conditioned benchmark, 40.39% of TC-PepGen peptides exceeded native templates in AlphaFold 3 ipTM, and unconstrained generation retained evidence of target-conditioned signal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于肽-蛋白相互作用预测与靶向条件肽生成的集成深度学习框架ConGA-PePPI与TC-PepGen
动机：肽-蛋白相互作用（PepPIs）在细胞调控和肽类药物中至关重要，但实验表征仍难以满足大规模筛选需求。现有方法通常侧重于相互作用预测或肽生成，而候选物优先排序、残基级解释及靶向条件扩展尚未充分整合。结果：我们提出了一种早期阶段肽筛选的集成框架，结合了伙伴感知的预测与定位模型（ConGA-PePPI）与靶向条件生成模型（TC-PepGen）。ConGA-PePPI采用非对称编码、双向交叉注意力及从配对预测到结合位点定位的渐进迁移；TC-PepGen通过逐层条件化在自回归解码中保留靶标信息。五折交叉验证中，ConGA-PePPI准确率达0.839，AUROC为0.921，蛋白侧与肽侧结合位点AUPR分别为0.601与0.950，并在外部基准测试中保持竞争力。在长度条件控制基准下，40.39%的TC-PepGen肽在AlphaFold 3 ipTM中优于天然模板，无约束生成仍保留靶向条件信号。

Driven by the need for efficient peptide-protein interaction screening and target-conditioned peptide design, this study introduces an integrated deep-learning framework combining ConGA-PepPI for interaction prediction and binding-site localization with TC-PepGen for target-conditioned peptide generation. LLM Hypnosis: Exploiting User Feedback for Unauthorized Knowledge Injection to All Users
Authors: Almog Hilel, Riddhi Bhagwat, Idan Shenfeld, Jacob Andreas, Leshem Choshen
First: 2025-07-03T17:55:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:20:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We describe a vulnerability in language models (LMs) trained with user feedback, whereby a single user can persistently alter LM knowledge and behavior given only the ability to provide prompts and upvote / downvote feedback on LM outputs. To implement the attack, the attacker prompts the LM to stochastically output either a "poisoned" or benign response, then upvotes the poisoned response or downvotes the benign one. When feedback signals are used in a subsequent preference tuning behavior, LMs exhibit increased probability of producing poisoned responses even in contexts without malicious prompts. We show that this attack can be used to (1) insert factual knowledge the model did not previously possess, (2) modify code generation patterns in ways that introduce exploitable security flaws, and (3) inject fake financial news. Our finding both identifies a new qualitative feature of language model preference tuning (showing that it even highly restricted forms of preference data can be used to exert fine-grained control over behavior), and a new attack mechanism for LMs trained with user feedback (extending work on pretraining-time data poisoning and deployment-time prompt injection).
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型催眠：利用用户反馈向所有用户非法注入知识
我们描述了一种在使用用户反馈训练的语言模型（LMs）中存在的漏洞：单个用户仅通过提供提示和对LM输出进行点赞/点踩反馈，即可持续改变LM的知识和行为。攻击者通过提示LM随机输出“中毒”或良性响应，然后对中毒响应点赞或对良性响应点踩。当后续偏好调优行为使用这些反馈信号时，即使在无恶意提示的上下文中，LMs也更可能生成中毒响应。我们表明，该攻击可用于：（1）插入模型此前不具有的事实性知识；（2）以引入可利用安全漏洞的方式修改代码生成模式；（3）注入虚假金融新闻。我们的发现既揭示了语言模型偏好调优的一种新质特征（表明即使高度受限的偏好数据也能实现对行为的精细控制），也提出了一种针对使用用户反馈训练的LMs的新攻击机制（扩展了关于预训练阶段数据投毒和部署阶段提示注入的研究）
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability in language models trained with user feedback, this study demonstrates how a single user can persistently manipulate model knowledge and behavior by strategically prompting and selectively upvoting or downvoting outputs. The attacker induces stochastic outputs—either poisoned or benign—and reinforces the poisoned responses through feedback, which then biases subsequent preference tuning. Experiments show this method successfully injects new factual knowledge, alters code generation to introduce security flaws, and spreads fake financial news, revealing that even limited feedback can exert fine-grained control over model behavior.
该研究基于用户反馈训练的语言模型存在漏洞，展示了一种攻击方式：攻击者通过提示模型随机生成有毒或良性响应，并通过点赞或点踩反馈引导模型偏好调整。实验表明，即使无恶意提示，该方法也能成功注入新事实知识、修改代码生成以引入安全漏洞，并传播虚假金融新闻。研究揭示了高度受限的偏好数据仍可对模型行为施加精细控制，暴露了除预训练数据投毒和部署时提示注入之外的新攻击途径。
AvatarPointillist: AutoRegressive 4D Gaussian Avatarization
Authors: Hongyu Liu, Xuan Wang, Zijian Wu, Yating Wang, Ziyu Wan, Yue Ma, Runtao Liu, Boyao Zhou, Yujun Shen, Qifeng Chen
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-06T15:56:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:19:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted by the CVPR 2026 main conference. Project page: https://kumapowerliu.github.io/AvatarPointillist/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce AvatarPointillist, a novel framework for generating dynamic 4D Gaussian avatars from a single portrait image. At the core of our method is a decoder-only Transformer that autoregressively generates a point cloud for 3D Gaussian Splatting. This sequential approach allows for precise, adaptive construction, dynamically adjusting point density and the total number of points based on the subject's complexity. During point generation, the AR model also jointly predicts per-point binding information, enabling realistic animation. After generation, a dedicated Gaussian decoder converts the points into complete, renderable Gaussian attributes. We demonstrate that conditioning the decoder on the latent features from the AR generator enables effective interaction between stages and markedly improves fidelity. Extensive experiments validate that AvatarPointillist produces high-quality, photorealistic, and controllable avatars. We believe this autoregressive formulation represents a new paradigm for avatar generation, and we will release our code inspire future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AvatarPointillist：自回归4D高斯头像生成

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity, controllable 4D avatar generation from a single image, AvatarPointillist introduces an autoregressive Transformer-based framework that sequentially builds a 3D Gaussian point cloud while dynamically adjusting density and point count based on subject complexity. Experimental results show the method produces photorealistic, controllable avatars with superior quality compared to prior approaches, establishing autoregressive generation as a promising paradigm for dynamic avatar synthesis.
为从单张肖像图生成高保真、可控制的4D虚拟人像，AvatarPointillist提出一种基于自回归Transformer的框架，通过动态调整点密度与数量，逐步构建3D高斯点云，并联合预测每点绑定信息以实现真实动画。生成后，专用高斯解码器将点云转换为可渲染属性，且通过将解码器与自回归生成器的潜在特征联动，显著提升各阶段协同与图像保真度。实验表明，该方法生成的虚拟人像在真实感与可控性上优于现有方法。
Semantic Step Prediction: Multi-Step Latent Forecasting in LLM Reasoning Trajectories via Step Sampling
Authors: Yidi Yuan
First: 2026-04-20T16:19:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:19:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Semantic Tube Prediction (STP) leverages representation geometric to regularize LLM hidden-state trajectories toward locally linear geodesics during fine-tuning, thereby greatly improving data efficiency. The original STP recipe samples random token sub-spans, which is compatible with the base large language model (LLM) training architecture. Inspired by STP, we are interested to investigate whether the sampling position can further enhance the semantic structure of multi-step reasoning, and hence affect its geometric impact. We applied STP at consecutive semantic reasoning step boundaries and achieved 168x more accurate multi-step latent prediction than frozen baselines on ProcessBench (3,400 samples), compared to only 4x for the random-token STP. Probing the latent manifold with a learned non-linear predictor reveals that STP-shaped trajectories are smooth curves, not straight lines: a 3-layer MLP reduces prediction error by a further 3-12x over linear extrapolation on step-boundary models. Removing the language modeling loss yields trajectories that are 2x more MLP-predictable than the combined loss, revealing a tradeoff between generation quality and geometric purity. Our results identify sampling position as the critical variable in geometric regularization and establish multi-step latent prediction MSE as a new evaluation metric for this class of methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语义步骤预测：通过步骤采样在LLM推理轨迹中实现多步潜在预测
语义管预测（STP）利用表示几何特性，在微调过程中将LLM隐藏状态轨迹正则化为局部线性测地线，从而大幅提升数据效率。原始STP方法采样随机token子片段，与基础大语言模型（LLM）训练架构兼容。受STP启发，我们探究采样位置是否能进一步增强多步推理的语义结构，从而影响其几何效应。我们在连续语义推理步骤边界处应用STP，在ProcessBench（3,400样本）上相比冻结基线实现了168倍更准确的多步潜在预测，而随机token STP仅提升4倍。通过学习非线性预测器探测潜在流形发现，STP形状轨迹为平滑曲线而非直线：3层MLP在步骤边界模型上相比线性外推进一步降低预测误差3-12倍。移除语言建模损失后，轨迹比联合损失模型更易被MLP预测2倍，揭示生成质量与几何纯度之间的权衡。我们的结果将采样位置确定为几何正则化中的关键变量，并确立多步潜在预测均方误差作为该类方法的新评估指标。

Removing the language modeling loss improves MLP predictability by 2x, indicating a tradeoff between generation quality and geometric purity, and establishing multi-step latent prediction MSE as a new evaluation metric.
受语义管预测（STP）几何正则化效果启发，本研究探索在语义推理步骤边界采样是否能提升多步潜在预测精度。方法在步骤边界应用STP，相较随机令牌采样，在ProcessBench上使多步潜在预测精度提升168倍，而随机采样仅提升4倍。通过非线性预测器探测潜在流形发现，STP轨迹为平滑曲线而非直线，三层MLP较线性外推进一步降低误差3–12倍。移除语言建模损失使MLP可预测性提升2倍，揭示生成质量与几何纯度间的权衡，并确立多步潜在预测MSE为该类方法的新评估指标。
CHIMERA: A Knowledge Base of Scientific Idea Recombinations for Research Analysis and Ideation
Authors: Noy Sternlicht, Tom Hope
First: 2025-05-27T06:36:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:18:27+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://noy-sternlicht.github.io/CHIMERA-Web
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
A hallmark of human innovation is recombination -- the creation of novel ideas by integrating elements from existing concepts and mechanisms. In this work, we introduce CHIMERA, the first large-scale Knowledge Base (KB) of recombination examples automatically mined from the scientific literature. CHIMERA enables empirical analysis of how scientists recombine concepts and draw inspiration from different areas, and enables training models that propose cross-disciplinary research directions. To construct this KB, we define a new information extraction task: identifying recombination instances in papers. We curate an expert-annotated dataset and use it to fine-tune an LLM-based extraction model, which we apply to a broad corpus of AI papers. We also demonstrate generalization to a biological domain. We showcase the utility of CHIMERA through two applications. First, we analyze patterns of recombination across AI subfields. Second, we train a scientific hypothesis generation model using the KB, showing that it can propose directions that researchers rate as inspiring.
中文标题/摘要

Experimental results show CHIMERA enables cross-disciplinary analysis of recombination trends and supports a hypothesis generation model that produces research directions rated as inspiring by human experts.

Using large language models for embodied planning introduces systematic safety risks
Authors: Tao Zhang, Kaixian Qu, Zhibin Li, Jiajun Wu, Marco Hutter, Manling Li, Fan Shi
First: 2026-04-20T16:18:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:18:08+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://despite-safety.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly used as planners for robotic systems, yet how safely they plan remains an open question. To evaluate safe planning systematically, we introduce DESPITE, a benchmark of 12,279 tasks spanning physical and normative dangers with fully deterministic validation. Across 23 models, even near-perfect planning ability does not ensure safety: the best-planning model fails to produce a valid plan on only 0.4% of tasks but produces dangerous plans on 28.3%. Among 18 open-source models from 3B to 671B parameters, planning ability improves substantially with scale (0.4-99.3%) while safety awareness remains relatively flat (38-57%). We identify a multiplicative relationship between these two capacities, showing that larger models complete more tasks safely primarily through improved planning, not through better danger avoidance. Three proprietary reasoning models reach notably higher safety awareness (71-81%), while non-reasoning proprietary models and open-source reasoning models remain below 57%. As planning ability approaches saturation for frontier models, improving safety awareness becomes a central challenge for deploying language-model planners in robotic systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用大语言模型进行具身规划会引入系统性安全风险
大语言模型正越来越多地被用作机器人系统的规划器，但它们如何安全地规划仍是一个开放性问题。为系统性评估安全规划，我们引入了DESPITE基准，该基准包含12,279个任务，涵盖物理和规范性危险，并具有完全确定性的验证。在23个模型中，即使规划能力接近完美，也不保证安全：表现最佳的模型仅在0.4%的任务中无法生成有效计划，但在28.3%的任务中生成危险计划。在3B至671B参数的18个开源模型中，规划能力随模型规模显著提升（0.4%-99.3%），而安全意识基本持平（38%-57%）。我们发现这两种能力之间存在乘法关系，表明更大模型能更安全地完成更多任务，主要得益于规划能力的提升，而非危险规避能力的增强。三个专有推理模型的安全意识显著更高（71%-81%），而非推理专有模型及开源推理模型仍低于57%。随着前沿模型的规划能力接近饱和，提升安全意识成为在机器人系统中部署语言模型规划器的核心挑战。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates safety risks in using large language models for robotic planning, motivated by the lack of systematic evaluation of safety alongside planning performance. The authors introduce DESPITE, a benchmark with 12,279 tasks covering physical and normative dangers, validated deterministically. Across 23 models, even the best-planning model produced dangerous plans on 28.3% of tasks despite failing only 0.4% of tasks to produce valid plans. Safety awareness improved little with model scale (38-57% for open-source models), while planning ability rose sharply (0.4-99.3%). The findings reveal a multiplicative relationship: larger models complete more tasks safely mainly due to better planning, not enhanced danger avoidance. Proprietary reasoning models achieved higher safety awareness (71-81%), while open-source and non-reasoning proprietary models lagged below 57%, indicating safety awareness remains a key bottleneck for deployment.
鉴于大语言模型在机器人规划中的广泛应用及其安全性的不确定性，该研究提出了DESPITE基准测试，包含12,279个涵盖物理与规范危险的任务，并采用确定性验证。在23个模型中，表现最佳的规划模型仅在0.4%的任务中无法生成有效计划，但在28.3%的任务中生成了危险计划。随着模型规模增大，规划能力显著提升（0.4%-99.3%），而安全意识提升有限（38%-57%），揭示出二者呈乘法关系：安全性的提升主要源于规划能力增强而非危险规避能力。专有推理模型的安全意识达71%-81%，而开源模型及非推理专有模型均低于57%，表明当规划能力趋于饱和时，提升安全意识成为部署语言模型规划器的关键挑战。
Learning Invariant Modality Representation for Robust Multimodal Learning from a Causal Inference Perspective
Authors: Sijie Mai, Shiqin Han
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-20T16:16:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:16:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal affective computing aims to predict humans' sentiment, emotion, intention, and opinion using language, acoustic, and visual modalities. However, current models often learn spurious correlations that harm generalization under distribution shifts or noisy modalities. To address this, we propose a causal modality-invariant representation (CmIR) learning framework for robust multimodal learning. At its core, we introduce a theoretically grounded disentanglement method that separates each modality into `causal invariant representation' and `environment-specific spurious representation' from a causal inference perspective. CmIR ensures that the learned invariant representations retain stable predictive relationships with labels across different environments while preserving sufficient information from the raw inputs via invariance constraint, mutual information constraint, and reconstruction constraint. Experiments across multiple multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that CmIR achieves state-of-the-art performance. CmIR particularly excels on out-of-distribution data and noisy data, confirming its robustness and generalizability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从因果推断视角学习不变模态表示以实现鲁棒多模态学习
多模态情感计算旨在利用语言、声学和视觉模态预测人类的情感、意图和观点。然而，当前模型常学习到虚假相关性，导致在分布偏移或噪声模态下泛化能力受损。为此，我们提出了一种因果模态不变表示（CmIR）学习框架，以实现鲁棒多模态学习。该框架基于因果推断视角，引入一种理论支撑的解耦方法，将每个模态分解为“因果不变表示”和“环境特定虚假表示”。CmIR通过不变性约束、互信息约束和重建约束，确保学习到的不变表示在不同环境中与标签保持稳定预测关系，同时保留原始输入的充分信息。在多个多模态基准测试中，CmIR均取得最先进性能，尤其在分布外数据和噪声数据上表现优异，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem of spurious correlations in multimodal affective computing that degrade model generalization under distribution shifts or noisy inputs, this paper proposes a causal modality-invariant representation (CmIR) framework. The method disentangles each modality into a causal invariant component and an environment-specific spurious component using a theoretically grounded causal inference approach. It enforces invariance, mutual information, and reconstruction constraints to ensure stable label prediction while preserving input information. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show CmIR achieves state-of-the-art performance, especially on out-of-distribution and noisy data, confirming its robustness and generalizability.
为了解决当前多模态情感计算模型因学习虚假相关性而在分布偏移或噪声模态下泛化能力差的问题，本文提出了一种因果模态不变表示（CmIR）框架。该方法从因果推断角度将每个模态分解为因果不变表示和环境特定的虚假表示，并通过不变性、互信息和重建约束确保学习到的表示在不同环境下仍能稳定预测标签，同时保留原始输入信息。实验表明，CmIR在多个基准数据集上达到最先进性能，尤其在分布外和噪声数据上表现突出，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。
Procedural Knowledge at Scale Improves Reasoning
Authors: Di Wu, Devendra Singh Sachan, Wen-tau Yih, Mingda Chen
First: 2026-04-01T20:01:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:16:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-time scaling has emerged as an effective way to improve language models on challenging reasoning tasks. However, most existing methods treat each problem in isolation and do not systematically reuse knowledge from prior reasoning trajectories. In particular, they underutilize procedural knowledge: how to reframe a problem, choose an approach, and verify or backtrack when needed. We introduce Reasoning Memory, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework for reasoning models that explicitly retrieves and reuses procedural knowledge at scale. Starting from existing corpora of step-by-step reasoning trajectories, we decompose each trajectory into self-contained subquestion-subroutine pairs, yielding a datastore of 32 million compact procedural knowledge entries. At inference time, a lightweight in-thought prompt lets the model verbalize the core subquestion, retrieve relevant subroutines within its reasoning trace, and reason under diverse retrieved subroutines as implicit procedural priors. Across six math, science, and coding benchmarks, Reasoning Memory consistently outperforms RAG with document, trajectory, and template knowledge, as well as a compute-matched test-time scaling baseline. With a higher inference budget, it improves over no retrieval by up to 19.2% and over the strongest compute-matched baseline by 7.9% across task types. Ablation studies show that these gains come from two key factors: the broad procedural coverage of the source trajectories and our decomposition and retrieval design, which together enable effective extraction and reuse of procedural knowledge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模程序性知识提升推理能力
测试时扩展已被证明是提升语言模型在复杂推理任务上表现的有效方法。然而，现有大多数方法将每个问题独立处理，未能系统性地重用先前推理轨迹中的知识，特别是未充分利用程序性知识：如何重新表述问题、选择方法以及在必要时验证或回溯。我们提出了推理记忆（Reasoning Memory），这是一种检索增强生成（RAG）框架，专门用于推理模型，可大规模显式检索并重用程序性知识。我们从现有的分步推理轨迹语料库出发，将每个轨迹分解为自包含的子问题-子程序对，构建了一个包含3200万条紧凑程序性知识条目的数据存储库。推理时，一个轻量级的思维内提示使模型能口头表达核心子问题，检索其推理轨迹中相关的子程序，并在多种检索到的子程序下进行推理，作为隐式程序性先验。在六个数学、科学和编程基准测试中，推理记忆始终优于使用文档、轨迹和模板知识的RAG方法，以及计算匹配的测试时扩展基线。在更高的推理预算下，其性能在各类任务中相比无检索提升最高达19.2%，相比最强的计算匹配基线提升7.9%。消融研究表明，这些性能增益源于两个关键因素：源轨迹的广泛程序性覆盖，以及我们的分解与检索设计，二者共同实现了程序性知识的有效提取与重用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance reasoning performance through scalable test-time methods, the paper introduces Reasoning Memory, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that explicitly reuses procedural knowledge from prior reasoning trajectories. It decomposes existing step-by-step reasoning corpora into 32 million subquestion-subroutine pairs, enabling the model to retrieve and apply relevant procedural steps during inference via lightweight in-thought prompting. Experimental results across six reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over document, trajectory, and template-based RAG methods, as well as a compute-matched scaling baseline, with up to 19.2% improvement over no retrieval and 7.9% over the strongest baseline. Ablation studies confirm that broad procedural coverage and the decomposition-retrieval design are key to these improvements.
为提升语言模型在推理任务中的表现，该研究提出Reasoning Memory框架，通过从现有推理轨迹中提取并复用程序性知识。方法将轨迹分解为3200万个子问题-子程序对，推理时通过轻量提示引导模型检索并应用相关子程序作为隐式先验。在六个基准测试中，该方法显著优于文档、轨迹和模板RAG方法及计算匹配的基线模型，最高提升达19.2%，验证了程序性知识复用的有效性。
Progressive Online Video Understanding with Evidence-Aligned Timing and Transparent Decisions
Authors: Kecheng Zhang, Zongxin Yang, Mingfei Han, Haihong Hao, Yunzhi Zhuge, Changlin Li, Junhan Zhao, Zhihui Li, Xiaojun Chang
First: 2026-04-20T16:15:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-20T16:15:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual agents operating in the wild must respond to queries precisely when sufficient evidence first appears in a video stream, a critical capability that is overlooked by conventional video LLMs evaluated in offline settings. The shift to an online, streaming paradigm introduces significant challenges: a lack of decision transparency, the difficulty of aligning response timing with visual evidence, and the need to maintain a global, causally consistent understanding under tight computational budgets. To address these issues, we propose a novel framework that decouples reasoning control from memory integration. We introduce \textbf{\model{}}, an instantiation of this framework with two core components. First, the \emph{Active Thinking Decision Maker (ATDM)} is a transparent reasoning controller that externalizes its decision process using observable progress ($\boldsymbolρ$) and confidence ($\boldsymbol{c}$) metrics. This allows it to precisely time its response $t_r$ to match the first-sufficient-evidence timestamp $t^\star$ while streaming its reasoning to the user. Second, the \emph{Hierarchical Progressive Semantic Integration (HPSI)} module acts as an efficient memory system. It employs a set of learnable, multi-level aggregation tokens that are propagated across clips to build a rich, global cognitive state without exceeding token budgets. %Our approach sets a new standard on key online video understanding benchmarks, achieving strong performance of \textbf{71.6\%} on StreamingBench and \textbf{46.9\%} on OVOBench, demonstrating a robust solution for evidence-aligned and transparent online video analysis. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of ATDM and HPSI, e.g., Thinking-QwenVL improves the accuracy of the previous state-of-the-art from 67.63\% to 71.60\% on the StreamingBench benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于证据对齐时间与透明决策的渐进式在线视频理解
在真实环境中运行的视觉代理必须在视频流中首次出现足够证据时精确响应查询，这一关键能力在离线评估的常规视频大语言模型中常被忽视。转向在线流式范式带来了重大挑战：决策缺乏透明性、响应时机难以与视觉证据对齐，以及在计算预算受限下维持全局因果一致理解的需求。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种新颖框架，将推理控制与记忆整合解耦。我们引入\textbf{\model{}}，该框架的实例包含两个核心组件。首先，\emph{主动思考决策器（ATDM）}是一个透明推理控制器，通过可观察的进度（\boldsymbolρ）和置信度（\boldsymbol{c}）指标外部化其决策过程，使其能精确对齐首次充分证据时间戳\(t^\star\)并流式向用户输出推理过程。其次，\emph{分层渐进语义整合（HPSI）}模块作为高效记忆系统，采用一组可学习的多级聚合标记，在片段间传播以构建丰富全局认知状态，同时不超出标记预算。我们的方法在关键在线视频理解基准上树立了新标准，在StreamingBench上达到\textbf{71.6\%}，在OVOBench上达到\textbf{46.9\%}，展示了证据对齐与透明在线视频分析的稳健解决方案。大量实验验证了ATDM与HPSI的有效性，例如，Thinking-QwenVL在StreamingBench基准上将先前最先进方法的准确率从67.63\%提升至71.60\%。
Summary / 总结
To enable visual agents to respond precisely when sufficient visual evidence first appears in streaming video—a capability missing in offline video LLMs—the paper proposes a novel online framework featuring two core components: the Active Thinking Decision Maker (ATDM), which externally tracks reasoning progress and confidence to align response timing with evidence timestamps, and the Hierarchical Progressive Semantic Integration (HPSI), which uses multi-level aggregation tokens to maintain a global, causally consistent understanding within token budget constraints. Experiments show this approach achieves 71.6% accuracy on StreamingBench and 46.9% on OVOBench, surpassing prior state-of-the-art by 3.97 percentage points on StreamingBench.
为使视觉代理能在流媒体视频中首次出现足够证据时精准响应（传统离线视频LLM缺乏此能力），该文提出\model{}框架，将推理与记忆解耦。其主动思考决策器（ATDM）通过外部化推理进度与置信度，实现响应时间与证据时间对齐并实时向用户流式输出推理过程；分层渐进语义集成模块（HPSI）则通过多级聚合令牌构建全局因果一致的认知状态，且不超令牌预算。实验表明，\model{}在StreamingBench上达71.6%，在OVOBench上达46.9%，在StreamingBench上超越前序最先进方法3.97个百分点。
